     1	"""Mask-aware Residual Vector Quantizer (RVQ) for the Graph-VQVAE tokenizer.
     2	
     3	Design source: handoff/20260608_graph_vqvae_l5_pipeline_plan.md §"Quantizer" +
     4	handoff/20260608_0600_graph_vqvae_review_verdict_and_forks.md (forks F3/F4,
     5	amendment-1 four leakage paths, amendment-3 DDP EMA sync).
     6	
     7	This is a NEW module under src/models/vq_model/ — it does NOT import or modify
     8	the Gaussian Graph-VAE. Inspired by MoGenTS residual_vq/quantizer (EMA codebook,
     9	dead-code reset, residual stages, quantizer dropout) but adapted to our
    10	arbitrary-topology setting: tokens are graph-pooled COARSE SLOTS with a per-token
    11	validity mask, NOT fixed human-joint grid cells. Padded slots / padded latent
    12	frames must never touch the codebook or contribute to the commit loss.
    13	
    14	Hard requirements implemented here (mask-aware quantization, amendment 1):
    15	  (1a) dead-code RESET samples replacement codes from VALID tokens only
    16	       (never the padded zero-vectors that flood the batch).
    17	  (1b) EMA cluster-size + cluster-sum DENOMINATORS exclude padded tokens
    18	       (one-hot is zeroed on invalid tokens before the EMA accumulation).
    19	  (1c) straight-through gradient is masked AFTER the STE detach:
    20	       z_q = (x + (q - x).detach()) * valid_mask[..., None]  — so a padded
    21	       token contributes exactly zero to z_q AND has zero gradient path.
    22	  (F4) commit loss is returned RAW (unweighted). commit_weight (0.02) is
    23	       applied ONCE downstream in the loss wrapper. Never squared here.
    24	
    25	Numerics (bf16-safe): codebook distance, argmin, and ALL EMA bookkeeping run in
    26	fp32 even under bf16 autocast. The input x is cast to fp32 inside forward; the
    27	returned quantized tensor is cast back to x's original dtype so the decoder sees
    28	the autocast dtype it expects (mirrors the existing GraphAttentionBlock pattern:
    29	sensitive reductions forced fp32, surrounding flow keeps the autocast dtype).
    30	
    31	DDP (amendment 3): the EMA update all-reduces (SUM) the per-rank cluster-size and
    32	cluster-sum statistics so every rank applies the SAME codebook update from the
    33	GLOBAL valid-token population. Commit-loss GLOBAL normalization is handled by the
    34	loss wrapper (it needs the global valid-token count, which it all-reduces there).
    35	"""
    36	
    37	from __future__ import annotations
    38	
    39	import torch
    40	import torch.nn as nn
    41	import torch.nn.functional as F
    42	import torch.distributed as dist
    43	
    44	
    45	def _ddp_active() -> bool:
    46	    """True iff a torchrun DDP process group is initialized."""
    47	    return dist.is_available() and dist.is_initialized()
    48	
    49	
    50	class _EMACodebook(nn.Module):
    51	    """One EMA-updated codebook for a single residual stage.
    52	
    53	    Buffers (NOT parameters — updated by EMA, not by the optimizer):
    54	      embed         [num_codes, code_dim]  current codebook vectors
    55	      cluster_size  [num_codes]            EMA count of VALID tokens per code
    56	      embed_avg     [num_codes, code_dim]  EMA sum of VALID assigned tokens
    57	      initted       []  scalar 0/1         (reserved; codebook is randn-init)
    58	
    59	    Mask-aware: forward takes a flat [N, D] token tensor + a [N] bool valid mask.
    60	    All padded (invalid) tokens are excluded from argmin-driven EMA stats and
    61	    from dead-code reset sampling.
    62	    """
    63	
    64	    def __init__(
    65	        self,
    66	        num_codes: int,
    67	        code_dim: int,
    68	        ema_mu: float = 0.99,
    69	        eps: float = 1e-5,
    70	        dead_code_threshold: float = 1.0,
    71	    ) -> None:
    72	        super().__init__()
    73	        if num_codes <= 0 or code_dim <= 0:
    74	            raise ValueError(f"num_codes/code_dim must be > 0, got {num_codes}/{code_dim}")
    75	        if not (0.0 < ema_mu < 1.0):
    76	            raise ValueError(f"ema_mu must be in (0,1), got {ema_mu}")
    77	        self.num_codes = num_codes
    78	        self.code_dim = code_dim
    79	        self.ema_mu = ema_mu
    80	        self.eps = eps
    81	        self.dead_code_threshold = dead_code_threshold
    82	
    83	        embed = torch.randn(num_codes, code_dim)
    84	        self.register_buffer("embed", embed)
    85	        self.register_buffer("cluster_size", torch.zeros(num_codes))
    86	        self.register_buffer("embed_avg", embed.clone())
    87	        self.register_buffer("initted", torch.tensor(0.0))
    88	
    89	    @torch.no_grad()
    90	    def _laplace_normalized_embed(self) -> torch.Tensor:
    91	        """Laplace-smoothed EMA embedding (cluster_size + eps in the denominator)."""
    92	        n = self.cluster_size.sum()
    93	        smoothed = (self.cluster_size + self.eps) / (n + self.num_codes * self.eps) * n
    94	        return self.embed_avg / smoothed.unsqueeze(1).clamp(min=self.eps)
    95	
    96	    @torch.no_grad()
    97	    def _reset_dead_codes(self, stage_input: torch.Tensor, valid_mask: torch.Tensor,
    98	                          allow_collectives: bool = True) -> int:
    99	        """Reset codes whose EMA cluster_size < threshold by resampling from the
   100	        VALID tokens of this stage (amendment 1a — never sample padded
   101	        zero-vectors).
   102	
   103	        stage_input : [N, D] fp32 — all tokens entering this stage (valid+padded).
   104	        valid_mask  : [N] bool    — True = real token.
   105	
   106	        Bug-2 fix: this is DATA-INDEPENDENT in its collective. cluster_size is
   107	        globally synced by the preceding EMA all_reduce, so every rank agrees on
   108	        WHICH codes are dead and HOW MANY. The replacement vectors are sampled on
   109	        rank 0 (from rank-0's valid tokens) and BROADCAST to all ranks, so the
   110	        broadcast fires on every rank regardless of each rank's local valid count
   111	        — no rank can skip the collective and deadlock. (Sampling only on rank 0
   112	        also keeps codebooks bit-identical across ranks.)
   113	
   114	        allow_collectives=False (rank-0-only eval pass / single-GPU): no broadcast;
   115	        sample from THIS rank's own valid tokens. Dead-code reset only runs in
   116	        training (caller gates on self.training).
   117	        """
   118	        dead = self.cluster_size < self.dead_code_threshold  # [num_codes] bool (synced)
   119	        n_dead = int(dead.sum().item())
   120	        if n_dead == 0:
   121	            return 0
   122	
   123	        ddp = allow_collectives and _ddp_active()
   124	        rank0 = (not ddp) or (dist.get_rank() == 0)
   125	        D = stage_input.shape[1]
   126	        new_vecs = torch.zeros(n_dead, D, dtype=stage_input.dtype, device=stage_input.device)
   127	
   128	        # Only the sampling source (rank 0 under DDP, else this rank) populates
   129	        # new_vecs from its valid tokens; if it has none, leave zeros and skip the
   130	        # in-place overwrite below so a code is never replaced by a padded zero.
   131	        has_valid_source = False
   132	        if rank0:
   133	            src = stage_input[valid_mask]   # [M, D] valid tokens on the source rank
   134	            M = src.shape[0]
   135	            if M > 0:
   136	                idx = torch.randint(0, M, (n_dead,), device=src.device)
   137	                new_vecs = src[idx]
   138	                has_valid_source = True
   139	
   140	        if ddp:
   141	            # Always-fired collectives (every rank participates): broadcast both
   142	            # the source-has-valid flag and the sampled vectors from rank 0.
   143	            flag = torch.tensor([1 if has_valid_source else 0],
   144	                                device=stage_input.device)
   145	            dist.broadcast(flag, src=0)
   146	            has_valid_source = bool(int(flag.item()))
   147	            dist.broadcast(new_vecs, src=0)
   148	
   149	        if not has_valid_source:
   150	            # No valid tokens to sample from this step → do not overwrite any code
   151	            # with zeros; report 0 resets.
   152	            return 0
   153	
   154	        self.embed[dead] = new_vecs
   155	        self.embed_avg[dead] = new_vecs
   156	        # Revive with cluster mass = threshold so the reset code is not counted as
   157	        # dead by THIS step's report. NOTE: with EMA decay it falls below threshold
   158	        # again next step unless it wins assignments — i.e. dead codes keep getting
   159	        # fresh samples until one is actually used. That recurring churn is the
   160	        # intended anti-collapse behaviour, not a bug.
   161	        self.cluster_size[dead] = float(self.dead_code_threshold)
   162	        return n_dead
   163	
   164	    def quantize(self, x_fp32: torch.Tensor) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   165	        """Nearest-code lookup in fp32. x_fp32: [N, D] -> (codes [N], q [N, D]).
   166	
   167	        Distance = ||x - e||^2 expanded as ||x||^2 - 2 x·e + ||e||^2 (fp32).
   168	        """
   169	        # (x - e)^2 = x^2 - 2xe + e^2 ; drop the constant x^2 term for argmin.
   170	        e = self.embed  # [K, D] fp32
   171	        dist_sq = (
   172	            x_fp32.pow(2).sum(dim=1, keepdim=True)
   173	            - 2.0 * x_fp32 @ e.t()
   174	            + e.pow(2).sum(dim=1).unsqueeze(0)
   175	        )  # [N, K]
   176	        codes = dist_sq.argmin(dim=1)  # [N]
   177	        q = F.embedding(codes, e)      # [N, D]
   178	        return codes, q
   179	
   180	    @torch.no_grad()
   181	    def ema_update(
   182	        self,
   183	        x_fp32: torch.Tensor,   # [N, D] all tokens (valid + padded), fp32
   184	        codes: torch.Tensor,    # [N] argmin code ids for ALL tokens
   185	        valid: torch.Tensor,    # [N] bool — True = real token
   186	        allow_collectives: bool = True,
   187	    ) -> float:
   188	        """EMA codebook update from VALID tokens only (amendments 1a/1b + DDP sync).
   189	
   190	        allow_collectives gates the cross-rank all_reduce (amendment 3): True in
   191	        the all-ranks training step (statistics summed over the GLOBAL valid-token
   192	        population); False on a rank-0-only pass so it never blocks on a collective
   193	        the other ranks are not making.
   194	
   195	        Returns the GLOBAL valid-token count this step. If it is zero (every token
   196	        on every rank is padded — degenerate, but not proven impossible), the
   197	        codebook is left UNTOUCHED: applying EMA decay with zero new mass would
   198	        shrink cluster_size toward 0 and make _laplace_normalized_embed divide by a
   199	        vanishing denominator → blow-up. The all_reduce still fires unconditionally
   200	        (every rank participates) so DDP collective ordering is preserved.
   201	        """
   202	        onehot = F.one_hot(codes, self.num_codes).to(x_fp32.dtype)  # [N, K]
   203	        # (1b) zero out padded tokens BEFORE accumulation so they contribute to
   204	        # neither the per-code count nor the per-code sum.
   205	        vmask = valid.to(x_fp32.dtype).unsqueeze(1)  # [N, 1]
   206	        onehot = onehot * vmask                       # [N, K]
   207	        cluster_size_batch = onehot.sum(dim=0)        # [K]
   208	        embed_sum_batch = onehot.t() @ x_fp32         # [K, D]
   209	
   210	        # (amendment 3) all-reduce the GLOBAL statistics across ranks so every
   211	        # rank applies the identical update from the global valid-token set.
   212	        # This collective fires on EVERY rank regardless of local/global validity
   213	        # (must stay before the zero-valid early-out so ranks never diverge).
   214	        if allow_collectives and _ddp_active():
   215	            dist.all_reduce(cluster_size_batch, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)
   216	            dist.all_reduce(embed_sum_batch, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)
   217	
   218	        global_valid = float(cluster_size_batch.sum().item())
   219	        if global_valid <= 0.0:
   220	            # No valid tokens anywhere → no-op (do NOT decay; would corrupt embed).
   221	            return 0.0
   222	
   223	        mu = self.ema_mu
   224	        self.cluster_size.mul_(mu).add_(cluster_size_batch, alpha=1 - mu)
   225	        self.embed_avg.mul_(mu).add_(embed_sum_batch, alpha=1 - mu)
   226	        self.embed.copy_(self._laplace_normalized_embed())
   227	        self.initted.fill_(1.0)
   228	        return global_valid
   229	
   230	
   231	class MaskedResidualVQ(nn.Module):
   232	    """Residual VQ over graph-pooled coarse-slot tokens, mask-aware end-to-end.
   233	
   234	    forward(x [B, T_lat, C, D], mask [B, T_lat, C] bool) -> dict:
   235	      quantized   [B, T_lat, C, D]   STE-passthrough, zeroed on padded tokens
   236	      indices     [B, T_lat, C, Q]   long, -1 on padded tokens
   237	      commit_loss scalar (fp32)      RAW unweighted MSE commit term (F4); the
   238	                                     caller applies commit_weight ONCE. Summed
   239	                                     over residual stages, averaged over valid
   240	                                     tokens * D within THIS rank (the loss
   241	                                     wrapper re-normalizes over GLOBAL tokens).
   242	      commit_sq_sum scalar (fp32)    sum of per-stage squared residuals over
   243	                                     VALID tokens only (numerator for global norm)
   244	      n_valid_tokens scalar (fp32)   number of valid tokens this rank (denominator
   245	                                     helper for the global commit normalization)
   246	      perplexity  [Q] fp32           codebook perplexity per stage (valid tokens)
   247	      active_codes[Q] long           #codes used this batch per stage (valid)
   248	      dead_codes  [Q] long           #codes reset this step per stage
   249	
   250	    Codebook update (EMA + dead-code reset) runs ONLY in training mode and ONLY
   251	    when there is at least one valid token. Quantizer dropout (F3) randomly
   252	    truncates the residual depth per FORWARD during training so later stages stay
   253	    trainable / used (MoGenTS-style); eval always uses the full depth.
   254	    """
   255	
   256	    def __init__(
   257	        self,
   258	        code_dim: int = 512,
   259	        num_codes: int = 512,
   260	        num_quantizers: int = 4,
   261	        ema_mu: float = 0.99,
   262	        quantize_dropout_prob: float = 0.1,
   263	        dead_code_threshold: float = 1.0,
   264	    ) -> None:
   265	        super().__init__()
   266	        if num_quantizers <= 0:
   267	            raise ValueError(f"num_quantizers must be > 0, got {num_quantizers}")
   268	        if not (0.0 <= quantize_dropout_prob < 1.0):
   269	            raise ValueError(
   270	                f"quantize_dropout_prob must be in [0,1), got {quantize_dropout_prob}")
   271	        self.code_dim = code_dim
   272	        self.num_codes = num_codes
   273	        self.num_quantizers = num_quantizers
   274	        self.quantize_dropout_prob = quantize_dropout_prob
   275	        self.codebooks = nn.ModuleList([
   276	            _EMACodebook(num_codes, code_dim, ema_mu=ema_mu,
   277	                        dead_code_threshold=dead_code_threshold)
   278	            for _ in range(num_quantizers)
   279	        ])
   280	
   281	    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, mask: torch.Tensor,
   282	                allow_collectives: bool = True) -> dict:
   283	        """allow_collectives gates EVERY cross-rank collective (EMA all_reduce,
   284	        dead-code broadcast, quantizer-dropout depth broadcast, perplexity
   285	        all_reduce). Pass True on the all-ranks training step (so the SMOKE
   286	        exercises amendment 3's DDP EMA sync); pass False on a rank-0-only eval
   287	        pass so the quantizer never blocks on a collective the other ranks are not
   288	        making (the deadlock that a naive `if _ddp_active()` would cause)."""
   289	        if x.dim() != 4 or x.shape[-1] != self.code_dim:
   290	            raise ValueError(
   291	                f"MaskedResidualVQ: x must be [B,T_lat,C,{self.code_dim}], "
   292	                f"got {tuple(x.shape)}")
   293	        B, T_lat, C, D = x.shape
   294	        if mask.shape != (B, T_lat, C) or mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   295	            raise ValueError(
   296	                f"MaskedResidualVQ: mask must be [B,T_lat,C]={(B, T_lat, C)} bool, "
   297	                f"got {tuple(mask.shape)} dtype {mask.dtype}")
   298	
   299	        orig_dtype = x.dtype
   300	        # Flatten to [N, D]; do all VQ math in fp32 (bf16-safe).
   301	        x_flat_fp32 = x.reshape(-1, D).float()           # [N, D]
   302	        valid_flat = mask.reshape(-1)                    # [N] bool
   303	        N = x_flat_fp32.shape[0]
   304	        device = x_flat_fp32.device
   305	        n_valid = int(valid_flat.sum().item())
   306	
   307	        residual = x_flat_fp32                           # running residual, fp32
   308	        q_total = torch.zeros_like(x_flat_fp32)          # accumulated quantized, fp32
   309	        indices_stages: list[torch.Tensor] = []
   310	        perplexities: list[torch.Tensor] = []
   311	        active_counts: list[torch.Tensor] = []
   312	        dead_counts: list[torch.Tensor] = []
   313	        commit_sq_sum = x_flat_fp32.new_zeros(())        # numerator for global norm
   314	
   315	        # Quantizer dropout (F3): in training, WITH PROBABILITY quantize_dropout_prob
   316	        # truncate the residual depth to a random level (so later stages still
   317	        # receive gradient/usage, MoGenTS-style); otherwise use full depth. Eval =
   318	        # always full depth. We always RUN all stages structurally (so DDP sees an
   319	        # identical graph + all codebook buffers participate), but stages beyond
   320	        # `keep_depth` are detached from q_total/commit and emit -1 indices
   321	        # (treated like "no residual code").
   322	        keep_depth = self.num_quantizers
   323	        if self.training and self.quantize_dropout_prob > 0.0 and self.num_quantizers > 1:
   324	            # Bernoulli(p): with probability quantize_dropout_prob this forward
   325	            # TRUNCATES the residual depth (so later stages still receive
   326	            # gradient/usage, MoGenTS-style); otherwise full depth. A dropout draw
   327	            # must ALWAYS truncate, so the kept depth is sampled from [1, Q-1]
   328	            # (NOT [1, Q] — drawing Q would mean "no truncation" and make the
   329	            # effective truncation rate p*(Q-1)/Q instead of p). Draw on rank 0 and
   330	            # broadcast BOTH the do_dropout decision and the depth so every rank
   331	            # picks the same keep_depth (identical DDP autograd graph).
   332	            do_dropout = bool(torch.rand((), device=device).item() < self.quantize_dropout_prob)
   333	            rand_depth = int(torch.randint(1, self.num_quantizers, (1,)).item())  # [1, Q-1]
   334	            if allow_collectives and _ddp_active():
   335	                payload = torch.tensor([1 if do_dropout else 0, rand_depth], device=device)
   336	                dist.broadcast(payload, src=0)
   337	                do_dropout = bool(int(payload[0].item()))
   338	                rand_depth = int(payload[1].item())
   339	            if do_dropout:
   340	                keep_depth = rand_depth
   341	
   342	        for qi, cb in enumerate(self.codebooks):
   343	            codes, q = cb.quantize(residual)             # [N], [N, D] fp32
   344	            active = qi < keep_depth
   345	            # Per-stage indices: -1 on padded tokens always; -1 on dropped stages.
   346	            stage_idx = torch.full((N,), -1, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
   347	            if active:
   348	                stage_idx[valid_flat] = codes[valid_flat]
   349	            indices_stages.append(stage_idx.reshape(B, T_lat, C))
   350	
   351	            if active:
   352	                # Commit term: pull encoder output toward the chosen code. Only
   353	                # valid tokens count (padded residuals are meaningless). Detach
   354	                # the code (codebook is EMA-updated, not gradient-updated).
   355	                sq = (residual - q.detach()).pow(2).sum(dim=1)   # [N] per-token
   356	                commit_sq_sum = commit_sq_sum + (sq * valid_flat.to(sq.dtype)).sum()
   357	                # `stage_input` is the residual the codes were assigned from —
   358	                # the EMA / dead-code-reset target for this stage (standard RVQ:
   359	                # each codebook clusters the residual entering it). Capture it
   360	                # BEFORE subtracting q below.
   361	                stage_input = residual
   362	                # Accumulate the quantized residual (fp32). Padded tokens are
   363	                # zeroed at the very end via the valid mask, so we leave them in
   364	                # the running residual here (their codes are discarded anyway).
   365	                q_total = q_total + q
   366	                residual = residual - q
   367	
   368	                # ---- codebook bookkeeping / EMA update (train only) ----
   369	                # Bug-2 fix: collective participation must NOT depend on the local
   370	                # valid-token count. EVERY rank runs the SAME collectives for an
   371	                # active stage during training, so a rank that happens to have zero
   372	                # local valid tokens cannot diverge from peers and deadlock. A
   373	                # zero-valid rank simply contributes zero stats (its one-hot is
   374	                # zeroed by the valid mask inside ema_update; its bincount is empty),
   375	                # which is the correct global-sum behaviour.
   376	                if self.training:
   377	                    # ema_update returns the GLOBAL valid count and no-ops (no decay,
   378	                    # no embed corruption) when it is 0. _reset_dead_codes is already
   379	                    # zero-valid-safe: when rank 0 has no valid source it fires the
   380	                    # broadcast (collective ordering preserved) but overwrites no code
   381	                    # and reports 0 resets. So both are safe even on a degenerate
   382	                    # all-padded global batch.
   383	                    cb.ema_update(x_fp32=stage_input, codes=codes, valid=valid_flat,
   384	                                  allow_collectives=allow_collectives)
   385	                    n_dead = cb._reset_dead_codes(
   386	                        stage_input, valid_flat,
   387	                        allow_collectives=allow_collectives)
   388	                else:
   389	                    n_dead = 0
   390	
   391	                # ---- diagnostics ----
   392	                with torch.no_grad():
   393	                    vcodes = codes[valid_flat]   # may be empty on a zero-valid rank
   394	                    counts = torch.bincount(vcodes, minlength=self.num_codes).float()
   395	                    if allow_collectives and _ddp_active():
   396	                        # Same collective on every rank (empty-rank contributes 0s).
   397	                        dist.all_reduce(counts, op=dist.ReduceOp.SUM)
   398	                    total_count = counts.sum().clamp(min=1.0)
   399	                    probs = counts / total_count
   400	                    nz = probs > 0
   401	                    entropy = -(probs[nz] * probs[nz].log()).sum() if nz.any() else torch.zeros((), device=device)
   402	                    perplexities.append(entropy.exp())
   403	                    active_counts.append((counts > 0).sum().long())
   404	                    dead_counts.append(torch.tensor(n_dead, dtype=torch.long, device=device))
   405	            else:
   406	                # Dropped stage: no commit, no accumulation, no codebook update.
   407	                perplexities.append(torch.zeros((), device=device))
   408	                active_counts.append(torch.zeros((), dtype=torch.long, device=device))
   409	                dead_counts.append(torch.zeros((), dtype=torch.long, device=device))
   410	
   411	        # (1c) Straight-through estimator, masked AFTER detach. q_total is the
   412	        # quantized fp32 sum; gradients flow to x through the (x - x.detach())
   413	        # identity, then the valid mask zeroes BOTH the value and the grad path
   414	        # of padded tokens.
   415	        x_q_flat = x_flat_fp32 + (q_total - x_flat_fp32).detach()  # [N, D] fp32
   416	        x_q_flat = x_q_flat * valid_flat.to(x_q_flat.dtype).unsqueeze(1)
   417	        quantized = x_q_flat.reshape(B, T_lat, C, D).to(orig_dtype)
   418	
   419	        # RAW (unweighted) commit loss for THIS rank: mean over valid tokens * D.
   420	        # F4 — commit_weight applied ONCE in the loss wrapper, never here.
   421	        denom = max(n_valid, 1) * D
   422	        commit_loss = commit_sq_sum / denom
   423	
   424	        indices = torch.stack(indices_stages, dim=-1)   # [B, T_lat, C, Q]
   425	        return {
   426	            "quantized": quantized,
   427	            "indices": indices,
   428	            "commit_loss": commit_loss,
   429	            "commit_sq_sum": commit_sq_sum,                       # for global norm
   430	            "n_valid_tokens": torch.tensor(float(n_valid), device=device),
   431	            "perplexity": torch.stack(perplexities),             # [Q]
   432	            "active_codes": torch.stack(active_counts),          # [Q]
   433	            "dead_codes": torch.stack(dead_counts),              # [Q]
   434	        }

codex
I’ve confirmed the tokenizer and RVQ sections mostly line up with current code. One nuance is already visible: the document describes “current usually” defaults, while some values are true only for this intended recipe or CLI default, not hard-coded invariants in every load path.

/usr/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba src/models/vq_model/masked_motion_decoder.py | sed -n '1,241p'" in /iridisfs/scratch/ts1v23/workspace/noKslot_clean
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""Masked Motion Decoder for the Graph-VQVAE tokenizer (fork F1).
     2	
     3	WHY A FORK (not a wrapper over the shared MotionDecoder):
     4	The shared src/models/motion_decoder.py SlotToJointCrossAttention biases the
     5	attention scores with `assignment.clamp(min=1e-8).log()` (motion_decoder.py:64).
     6	A PADDED slot column has assignment == 0 → clamp(1e-8).log() ≈ -18.42, a large
     7	FINITE negative — NOT -inf. So padded slots still receive a tiny but non-zero
     8	softmax weight, and a large positive raw score (q·k) can overwhelm -18.42. The
     9	shared decoder also has NO coarse_mask parameter. Therefore a wrapper CANNOT
    10	achieve the STRICT -inf slot-key masking the review (forks F1 + amendment 1d)
    11	requires. Per the user's F1 decision, we fork a masked copy HERE inside
    12	src/models/vq_model/ with an explicit `coarse_mask`, and DO NOT touch the shared
    13	MotionDecoder (which is used by the running Gaussian-VAE + diffusion training).
    14	
    15	This module is otherwise a structural mirror of motion_decoder.py:20-206 (same
    16	sub-blocks, same dims, same einsum unpool, same temporal refine, same output
    17	masking) so its reconstruction behavior matches the validated coarse_xattn path —
    18	the ONLY semantic change is the strict padded-slot-key mask in the slot
    19	cross-attention (amendment 1d / F1).
    20	
    21	bf16-safe: softmax is forced to fp32 then cast back (mirrors the shared decoder).
    22	The -inf is injected in the fp32 score space; a fully-(-inf) query row cannot
    23	occur because every valid joint always has ≥1 valid slot key (the root slot is
    24	always valid), and padded-joint query rows are masked to zero at the output.
    25	"""
    26	
    27	from __future__ import annotations
    28	
    29	import math
    30	import torch
    31	import torch.nn as nn
    32	import torch.nn.functional as F
    33	
    34	
    35	class MaskedSlotToJointCrossAttention(nn.Module):
    36	    """Slot cross-attention with STRICT -inf masking of padded slot KEYS.
    37	
    38	    Mirrors motion_decoder.py::SlotToJointCrossAttention (same projections, same
    39	    learnable assign_bias_scale, same fp32 softmax) but adds a `coarse_mask`
    40	    [B, K] bool that forces padded slot keys to exactly -inf BEFORE softmax →
    41	    exactly zero attention weight on padded slots (amendment 1d / F1).
    42	    """
    43	
    44	    def __init__(self, d_model: int, n_heads: int, dropout: float = 0.1):
    45	        super().__init__()
    46	        self.n_heads = n_heads
    47	        self.d_head = d_model // n_heads
    48	
    49	        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
    50	        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
    51	        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
    52	        self.o_proj = nn.Linear(d_model, d_model)
    53	
    54	        self.norm_q = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
    55	        self.norm_kv = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
    56	        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
    57	
    58	        self.assign_bias_scale = nn.Parameter(torch.tensor(1.0))
    59	
    60	    def forward(
    61	        self,
    62	        joint_queries: torch.Tensor,   # [B, J, D]
    63	        slot_features: torch.Tensor,   # [B, K, D]
    64	        assignment: torch.Tensor,      # [B, J, K] — attention bias (log P)
    65	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,     # [B, K] bool — True = valid slot key
    66	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
    67	        B, J, D = joint_queries.shape
    68	        K = slot_features.shape[1]
    69	
    70	        q = self.q_proj(self.norm_q(joint_queries))
    71	        kv_in = self.norm_kv(slot_features)
    72	        k = self.k_proj(kv_in)
    73	        v = self.v_proj(kv_in)
    74	
    75	        q = q.view(B, J, self.n_heads, self.d_head).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
    76	        k = k.view(B, K, self.n_heads, self.d_head).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
    77	        v = v.view(B, K, self.n_heads, self.d_head).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
    78	
    79	        # Attention scores [B, H, J, K]
    80	        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(self.d_head)
    81	
    82	        # Assignment log-bias (joints attend more to their assigned slots).
    83	        assign_bias = assignment.unsqueeze(1).clamp(min=1e-8).log()  # [B, 1, J, K]
    84	        scores = scores + self.assign_bias_scale * assign_bias
    85	
    86	        # STRICT padded-slot-KEY mask (F1): set padded slot columns to -inf so
    87	        # their softmax weight is EXACTLY 0. Done in fp32 score space below.
    88	        # [B, K] -> [B, 1, 1, K]
    89	        key_mask = coarse_mask.bool().unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)  # [B,1,1,K]
    90	        scores = scores.float().masked_fill(~key_mask, float("-inf"))
    91	
    92	        attn = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1)              # fp32 softmax
    93	        # A query row with at least one valid key never produces all-(-inf); but
    94	        # guard NaN defensively (padded-joint query rows are zeroed downstream).
    95	        attn = attn.nan_to_num(0.0).to(v.dtype)
    96	        attn = self.dropout(attn)
    97	
    98	        out = torch.matmul(attn, v)  # [B, H, J, d_head]
    99	        out = out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B, J, D)
   100	        return joint_queries + self.o_proj(out)
   101	
   102	
   103	class TemporalRefineBlock(nn.Module):
   104	    """1D temporal convolution for smoothing decoded motion.
   105	
   106	    Structural mirror of motion_decoder.py::TemporalRefineBlock, but FRAME-MASK
   107	    AWARE: the two convs are split (not an nn.Sequential) so a frame mask can be
   108	    applied to (a) the normed conv input, (b) the hidden activation BETWEEN the
   109	    two convs, and (c) the output after the residual add. Without (b), conv1's
   110	    bias makes padded-frame positions nonzero after GELU, and conv2 (kernel>1)
   111	    would then mix those padded positions into adjacent VALID frames — the
   112	    intra-block leak the final output mask alone cannot stop.
   113	    """
   114	
   115	    def __init__(self, d_model: int, kernel_size: int = 9, dropout: float = 0.1):
   116	        super().__init__()
   117	        padding = (kernel_size - 1) // 2
   118	        self.conv1 = nn.Conv1d(d_model, d_model, kernel_size, padding=padding)
   119	        self.act = nn.GELU()
   120	        self.drop1 = nn.Dropout(dropout)
   121	        self.conv2 = nn.Conv1d(d_model, d_model, kernel_size, padding=padding)
   122	        self.drop2 = nn.Dropout(dropout)
   123	        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
   124	
   125	    def forward(self, x: torch.Tensor, frame_mask: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   126	        """x: [N, T, D]  frame_mask: [N, T, 1] (float, 1=valid) -> [N, T, D]."""
   127	        residual = x
   128	        fm_c = frame_mask.transpose(1, 2)            # [N, 1, T] for conv (channel-first)
   129	        h = self.norm(x).permute(0, 2, 1)            # [N, D, T]
   130	        h = h * fm_c                                  # zero padded frames at conv1 input
   131	        h = self.drop1(self.act(self.conv1(h)))      # [N, D, T]
   132	        h = h * fm_c                                  # (b) zero padded frames BEFORE conv2
   133	        h = self.drop2(self.conv2(h))                # [N, D, T]
   134	        h = (h * fm_c).permute(0, 2, 1)              # [N, T, D] (zero padded frames out)
   135	        return residual + h
   136	
   137	
   138	class MaskedMotionDecoder(nn.Module):
   139	    """Decode coarse-slot features -> per-joint motion features, with strict
   140	    padded-slot-key masking in every cross-attention layer (F1).
   141	
   142	    Structural mirror of motion_decoder.py::MotionDecoder (same unpool einsum,
   143	    same per-frame cross-attn + FFN, same per-joint temporal refine, same output
   144	    norm + joint/frame output masking). Returns the masked pre-output-projection
   145	    features [B, T, J, D] (the VQ tokenizer's anytop13 head does the real output,
   146	    mirroring the shared VAE's return_features=True usage).
   147	    """
   148	
   149	    def __init__(
   150	        self,
   151	        d_model: int = 512,
   152	        n_heads: int = 8,
   153	        n_cross_layers: int = 3,
   154	        n_temporal_layers: int = 2,
   155	        temporal_kernel: int = 9,
   156	        dropout: float = 0.1,
   157	    ):
   158	        super().__init__()
   159	        self.d_model = d_model
   160	
   161	        self.cross_layers = nn.ModuleList([
   162	            MaskedSlotToJointCrossAttention(d_model, n_heads, dropout)
   163	            for _ in range(n_cross_layers)
   164	        ])
   165	        self.ffn_layers = nn.ModuleList([
   166	            nn.Sequential(
   167	                nn.LayerNorm(d_model),
   168	                nn.Linear(d_model, d_model * 4),
   169	                nn.GELU(),
   170	                nn.Dropout(dropout),
   171	                nn.Linear(d_model * 4, d_model),
   172	                nn.Dropout(dropout),
   173	            )
   174	            for _ in range(n_cross_layers)
   175	        ])
   176	        self.temporal_layers = nn.ModuleList([
   177	            TemporalRefineBlock(d_model, temporal_kernel, dropout)
   178	            for _ in range(n_temporal_layers)
   179	        ])
   180	        self.output_norm = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
   181	
   182	    def forward(
   183	        self,
   184	        slot_features: torch.Tensor,        # [B, T, K, D]
   185	        skeleton_embeddings: torch.Tensor,  # [B, J, D]
   186	        assignment: torch.Tensor,           # [B, J, K] real pool assignment P
   187	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,          # [B, K] bool — True = valid slot
   188	        joint_mask: torch.Tensor,           # [B, J] bool
   189	        frame_mask: torch.Tensor,           # [B, T] bool
   190	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   191	        """Returns masked pre-output features [B, T, J, D]."""
   192	        B, T, K, D = slot_features.shape
   193	        J = skeleton_embeddings.shape[1]
   194	
   195	        # 1. Initial unpool: [B, T, J, D]. Assignment already zeroes padded slot
   196	        # columns (P[:, :, c]=0 for padded c), so the einsum never mixes in
   197	        # padded slot features here. The cross-attention below adds the strict
   198	        # -inf key mask on top.
   199	        unpool_features = torch.einsum('bjk,btkd->btjd', assignment, slot_features)
   200	
   201	        # 2. Topology-conditioned joint queries.
   202	        joint_features = unpool_features + skeleton_embeddings.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, T, -1, -1)
   203	
   204	        # mask broadcasts reused below: [B,1,J,1] joints, [B,T,1,1] frames.
   205	        jm_b = joint_mask[:, None, :, None].to(joint_features.dtype)
   206	        fm_b = frame_mask[:, :, None, None].to(joint_features.dtype)
   207	
   208	        # 3. Cross-attention refinement (per frame).
   209	        cm_e = coarse_mask.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, T, -1).reshape(B * T, K)  # [B*T, K]
   210	        for cross_attn, ffn in zip(self.cross_layers, self.ffn_layers):
   211	            jf = joint_features.reshape(B * T, J, D)
   212	            sf = slot_features.reshape(B * T, K, D)
   213	            assign_expand = assignment.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, T, -1, -1).reshape(B * T, J, K)
   214	            jf = cross_attn(jf, sf, assign_expand, cm_e)
   215	            jf = jf + ffn(jf)
   216	            joint_features = jf.reshape(B, T, J, D)
   217	
   218	        # Re-mask padded joints + frames BEFORE the temporal conv. Cross-attn +
   219	        # the added skeleton embeddings made padded (joint,frame) entries nonzero;
   220	        # the per-joint temporal conv (kernel>1) mixes neighboring frames, so a
   221	        # nonzero padded FRAME would leak into adjacent valid frames. Zeroing here
   222	        # (and after each temporal layer below) makes padded frames contribute
   223	        # nothing — clean by construction, not just at the final output mask.
   224	        joint_features = joint_features * jm_b * fm_b
   225	
   226	        # 4. Temporal refinement (per joint across time). The block is frame-mask
   227	        # aware (masks the conv input, the inter-conv hidden activation, AND its
   228	        # output), so padded frames neither produce output nor leak into valid
   229	        # frames through the convs. The input `joint_features` is already masked
   230	        # above; each block returns a clean (padded-frame-zero) result.
   231	        jf = joint_features.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(B * J, T, D)  # [B*J, T, D]
   232	        fm_t = frame_mask[:, None, :, None].expand(B, J, T, 1).reshape(B * J, T, 1).to(jf.dtype)
   233	        for temp_layer in self.temporal_layers:
   234	            jf = temp_layer(jf, fm_t)
   235	        joint_features = jf.reshape(B, J, T, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)   # [B, T, J, D]
   236	
   237	        # 5. Output norm + mask invalid joints / frames.
   238	        features = self.output_norm(joint_features)
   239	        features = features * joint_mask[:, None, :, None].to(features.dtype)
   240	        features = features * frame_mask[:, :, None, None].to(features.dtype)
   241	        return features

    29	the dataset, keeping the post-RVQ branch decoupled from the Gaussian VAE / latent

    33	from __future__ import annotations
    34	
    35	import torch
    36	import torch.nn as nn
    37	
    38	from .graph_codeflow import GraphStructuredCodeFlow
    39	from .graph_pscf import GraphPSCFFlowNet

    41	
    42	class GraphCodeFlow(nn.Module):
    43	    """Rectified-flow wrapper around GraphStructuredCodeFlow.
    44	
    45	    Holds the velocity net + the frozen empirical-norm buffers. The frozen
    46	    Graph-VQVAE tokenizer is NOT held here (it is passed to `decode`/sampling by
    47	    the caller) so this stays a pure flow module.
    48	
    49	    Empirical normalization (LOCKED): set_latent_stats(mean, std) registers frozen
    50	    buffers `latent_mean`/`latent_std` shaped [1,1,1,D]; `normalize` maps raw z_q
    51	    -> model space, `denormalize` inverts it. Defaults are mean 0 / std 1 (identity)
    52	    until the trainer computes empirical stats over VALID exported z_q tokens.
    53	    """
    54	
    55	    def __init__(
    56	        self,
    57	        code_dim: int = 512,
    58	        n_heads: int = 8,
    59	        d_ff: int | None = None,
    60	        n_layers: int = 5,
    61	        d_text: int = 768,
    62	        text_token_dim: int = 768,
    63	        dropout: float | None = None,
    64	        noise_scale: float = 1.0,
    65	        t_eps: float = 1e-4,
    66	        model_variant: str = "level_a",
    67	        depth_double: int = 6,
    68	        depth_single: int = 12,
    69	        max_T_lat: int = 75,
    70	        mlp_ratio: float = 4.0,
    71	    ) -> None:
    72	        super().__init__()
    73	        self.code_dim = code_dim
    74	        self.model_variant = model_variant
    75	        self.noise_scale = float(noise_scale)
    76	        self.t_eps = float(t_eps)
    77	        # Resolve dropout per variant if unset (spec §5.4: graph_pscf=0.05, level_a=0.1)
    78	        # so a bare GraphCodeFlow(model_variant="graph_pscf") does NOT inherit 0.1.
    79	        if dropout is None:
    80	            dropout = 0.05 if model_variant == "graph_pscf" else 0.1
    81	        # model_variant selector (handoff §5.3): old ckpts default to level_a so they
    82	        # still load; graph_pscf is the formal backbone. Both nets share the EXACT
    83	        # 11-positional-arg forward contract, so predict_velocity/CFG/empirical-norm
    84	        # below are unchanged and drop-in for either variant.
    85	        if model_variant == "level_a":
    86	            self.net = GraphStructuredCodeFlow(
    87	                code_dim=code_dim, n_heads=n_heads, d_ff=d_ff, n_layers=n_layers,
    88	                d_text=d_text, text_token_dim=text_token_dim, dropout=dropout)
    89	        elif model_variant == "graph_pscf":
    90	            self.net = GraphPSCFFlowNet(
    91	                code_dim=code_dim, n_heads=n_heads,
    92	                d_ff=(2048 if d_ff is None else d_ff),
    93	                depth_double=depth_double, depth_single=depth_single,
    94	                d_text=d_text, text_token_dim=text_token_dim,
    95	                max_T_lat=max_T_lat, mlp_ratio=mlp_ratio, dropout=dropout)
    96	        else:
    97	            raise ValueError(
    98	                f"unknown model_variant {model_variant!r} (expected level_a | graph_pscf)")
    99	        # Empirical latent stats (frozen buffers; identity until set). persistent
   100	        # so they travel with the checkpoint (handoff §5.2: normalizer saved with
   101	        # the backbone ckpt).
   102	        self.register_buffer("latent_mean", torch.zeros(1, 1, 1, code_dim))
   103	        self.register_buffer("latent_std", torch.ones(1, 1, 1, code_dim))
   104	
   105	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   106	    # Empirical normalization (frozen)                                   #
   107	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   108	    @torch.no_grad()
   109	    def set_latent_stats(self, mean: torch.Tensor, std: torch.Tensor,
   110	                         eps: float = 1e-6) -> None:
   111	        """Install empirical z_q stats. mean/std are [D] (or broadcastable to
   112	        [1,1,1,D]); std is floored at eps to avoid divide-by-zero."""
   113	        mean = mean.reshape(1, 1, 1, self.code_dim).float()
   114	        std = std.reshape(1, 1, 1, self.code_dim).float().clamp_min(eps)
   115	        self.latent_mean.copy_(mean)
   116	        self.latent_std.copy_(std)
   117	
   118	    def normalize(self, z: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   119	        m = self.latent_mean.to(device=z.device, dtype=z.dtype)
   120	        s = self.latent_std.to(device=z.device, dtype=z.dtype)
   121	        return (z - m) / s
   122	
   123	    def denormalize(self, z: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   124	        m = self.latent_mean.to(device=z.device, dtype=z.dtype)
   125	        s = self.latent_std.to(device=z.device, dtype=z.dtype)
   126	        return z * s + m
   127	
   128	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   129	    # Velocity prediction (thin pass-through to the graph net)           #
   130	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   131	    def predict_velocity(
   132	        self,
   133	        z_t: torch.Tensor,
   134	        timesteps: torch.Tensor,
   135	        cond: dict,
   136	        *,
   137	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
   138	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   139	        """cond carries the (already-prepared) conditioning tensors:
   140	        text_global, text_tokens, text_token_mask, has_text, pooled_adjacency,
   141	        pooled_geodesic, pooled_skeleton_embeddings, coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat.
   142	        """
   143	        return self.net(
   144	            z_t, timesteps,
   145	            cond["text_global"], cond["text_tokens"], cond["text_token_mask"],
   146	            cond["has_text"], cond["pooled_adjacency"], cond["pooled_geodesic"],
   147	            cond["pooled_skeleton_embeddings"], cond["coarse_mask"],
   148	            cond["frame_mask_lat"], validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   149	
   150	    def predict_clean_from_velocity(
   151	        self, z_t: torch.Tensor, timesteps: torch.Tensor, velocity: torch.Tensor,
   152	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   153	        """clean = z_t + (1 - t) * v  (CodeFlow motion_code_flow.py:405-413)."""
   154	        t = timesteps
   155	        while t.ndim < z_t.ndim:
   156	            t = t[..., None]
   157	        return z_t + (1.0 - t).clamp_min(self.t_eps) * velocity
   158	
   159	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   160	    # Rectified-flow training loss (flow-only, masked over valid tokens) #
   161	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   162	    def forward(self, *args, **kwargs) -> dict:
   163	        """DDP entry point: forward == flow_loss, so DDP's gradient all_reduce
   164	        fires on the training step. Single-process callers may use flow_loss
   165	        directly; under DDP the trainer MUST call the wrapped module via
   166	        flow(...) (NOT flow.module.flow_loss, which would bypass the DDP hooks
   167	        and desync gradients across ranks)."""
   168	        return self.flow_loss(*args, **kwargs)
   169	
   170	    def flow_loss(
   171	        self,
   172	        z_q: torch.Tensor,                 # [B,T_lat,C,D] RAW frozen RVQ z_q
   173	        token_mask: torch.Tensor,          # [B,T_lat,C] bool (valid tokens)
   174	        cond: dict,                        # conditioning (with CFG drop applied)
   175	        *,
   176	        noise: torch.Tensor | None = None,
   177	        timesteps: torch.Tensor | None = None,
   178	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
   179	    ) -> dict:
   180	        """Rectified-flow masked MSE. Returns {flow_loss, velocity_pred,
   181	        velocity_target, z_t, timesteps} (the extras let the trainer log the
   182	        continuous-vs-snapped projection on the SAME predicted clean latent).
   183	
   184	        Flow math (CodeFlow): work in NORMALIZED latent space.
   185	          x = normalize(z_q)
   186	          z_t = t*x + (1-t)*noise ;  v_target = x - noise
   187	          v_pred = net(z_t, t, cond)
   188	          loss = mean over (valid tokens * D) of (v_pred - v_target)^2
   189	        2D `[T_lat,C]` masking is applied to z_t (noise-init), and the loss
   190	        reduction divides by (#valid tokens * D). fp32 reductions.
   191	        """
   192	        if z_q.dim() != 4 or z_q.shape[-1] != self.code_dim:
   193	            raise ValueError(
   194	                f"flow_loss: z_q must be [B,T_lat,C,D={self.code_dim}], got "
   195	                f"{tuple(z_q.shape)}")
   196	        B, T_lat, C, D = z_q.shape
   197	        if token_mask.shape != (B, T_lat, C) or token_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   198	            raise ValueError(
   199	                f"flow_loss: token_mask must be [B,T_lat,C]={(B, T_lat, C)} bool, "
   200	                f"got {tuple(token_mask.shape)} {token_mask.dtype}")
   201	        x = self.normalize(z_q)
   202	        valid = token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(x.dtype)       # [B,T_lat,C,1]
   203	        # zero padded targets so noise never injects signal there.
   204	        x = x * valid
   205	        if noise is None:
   206	            noise = torch.randn_like(x) * self.noise_scale
   207	        else:
   208	            noise = noise.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
   209	        noise = noise * valid
   210	        if timesteps is None:
   211	            t = torch.rand(B, device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)   # uniform t (recipe)
   212	        else:
   213	            t = timesteps.to(device=x.device, dtype=x.dtype)
   214	            if t.ndim == 0:
   215	                t = t.expand(B)
   216	        t_view = t[:, None, None, None]
   217	        z_t = (t_view * x + (1.0 - t_view) * noise) * valid
   218	        v_target = (x - noise) * valid
   219	
   220	        v_pred = self.predict_velocity(z_t, t, cond, validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   221	
   222	        # Masked flow MSE in fp32 (CodeFlow :509-510 adapted to 2D mask + sum/D).
   223	        vmask = token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()           # [B,T_lat,C,1]
   224	        diff_sq = (v_pred.float() - v_target.float()).pow(2) * vmask
   225	        denom = vmask.sum().clamp_min(1.0) * 1.0            # (#valid tokens) * D via broadcast below
   226	        # diff_sq summed over the D axis is folded into the numerator already
   227	        # (vmask broadcasts over D); divide by valid_token_count * D.
   228	        n_valid_tokens = token_mask.float().sum().clamp_min(1.0)
   229	        loss = diff_sq.sum() / (n_valid_tokens * D)
   230	        return {
   231	            "flow_loss": loss,
   232	            "velocity_pred": v_pred,
   233	            "velocity_target": v_target,
   234	            "z_t": z_t,
   235	            "timesteps": t,
   236	        }
   237	
   238	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   239	    # ODE + CFG sampler (continuous z_hat in RAW latent space)           #
   240	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   241	    @torch.no_grad()
   242	    def sample(
   243	        self,
   244	        cond: dict,
   245	        token_mask: torch.Tensor,          # [B,T_lat,C] bool
   246	        T_lat: int,
   247	        C: int,
   248	        *,
   249	        steps: int = 50,
   250	        cfg_scale: float = 4.0,
   251	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
   252	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   253	        """ODE-integrate from t=0 to t=1 with classifier-free guidance, returning
   254	        the DEnormalized continuous z_hat [B,T_lat,C,D] (raw RVQ latent space, to
   255	        be fed to tokenizer.nearest_residual_ids). Padded tokens are kept 0.
   256	
   257	        CFG (handoff §8): the uncond branch drops BOTH text streams (has_text
   258	        False + token mask all-masked). `cond` must contain a sibling
   259	        `cond_uncond` dict already built with text dropped, OR has_text False; we
   260	        build the uncond view here by flipping has_text -> all-False (the token
   261	        cross-attn zeroes those rows; the global add is gated to 0). cfg_scale=1.0
   262	        skips the uncond pass.
   263	        """
   264	        device = token_mask.device
   265	        B = token_mask.shape[0]
   266	        D = self.code_dim
   267	        valid = token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()           # [B,T_lat,C,1]
   268	        z = torch.randn(B, T_lat, C, D, device=device) * self.noise_scale
   269	        z = z * valid
   270	        grid = torch.linspace(0.0, 1.0, steps + 1, device=device)
   271	
   272	        # Build the uncond conditioning view (text dropped) once.
   273	        cond_uncond = dict(cond)
   274	        cond_uncond["has_text"] = torch.zeros_like(cond["has_text"])
   275	
   276	        for i in range(steps):
   277	            t_cur = grid[i]
   278	            dt = grid[i + 1] - t_cur
   279	            t_b = t_cur.expand(B)
   280	            v_cond = self.predict_velocity(z, t_b, cond, validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   281	            if float(cfg_scale) == 1.0:
   282	                v = v_cond
   283	            else:
   284	                v_uncond = self.predict_velocity(
   285	                    z, t_b, cond_uncond, validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   286	                v = v_uncond + float(cfg_scale) * (v_cond - v_uncond)
   287	            z = (z + dt * v) * valid
   288	        # De-normalize back to RAW RVQ latent space (so nearest_residual_ids snaps
   289	        # against the real codebooks), then re-mask padded tokens to 0.
   290	        z_hat = self.denormalize(z) * valid
   291	        return z_hat

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""GraphStructuredCodeFlow — Level-A graph rectified-flow backbone over the
     2	FROZEN Graph-VQVAE post-RVQ z_q grid.
     3	
     4	Plan: handoff/20260609_graph_codeflow_rvq_backbone_plan.md §7 (Level A) +
     5	handoff/20260609_0530_graph_codeflow_locked_recipe_and_state.md (LOCKED recipe).
     6	
     7	This is the SEPARATE post-RVQ generation branch (handoff §16): it does NOT touch
     8	the Gaussian VAE / latent diffusion / graph_salad denoiser. It REUSES (by import,
     9	never copy) the shared graph-temporal + dual-text ingredients:
    10	
    11	  - GraphAttentionBlock          (graph_salad.attention) — graph-spatial attn
    12	                                   over the C coarse-slot axis (pooled adjacency /
    13	                                   geodesic bias), per latent frame.
    14	  - TemporalSelfAttention        (motion_decoder)        — temporal attn over the
    15	                                   T_lat latent-frame axis, per coarse slot.
    16	  - SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding,
    17	    DenseFiLM, TextCrossAttention (graph_salad.denoiser)  — timestep FiLM, the
    18	                                   dual_text global-add path, and token-level text
    19	                                   cross-attention (CFG-uncond rows zeroed).
    20	
    21	I/O is the post-RVQ token grid `[B, T_lat, C, D]` (D = RVQ code_dim). The model
    22	predicts the rectified-flow velocity `v` at the same shape. Strict 2D `[T_lat,C]`
    23	masking (`token_mask = coarse_mask & frame_mask_lat`) is re-applied after every
    24	sub-block AND at input/output — padded slots / padded latent frames never leak.
    25	
    26	Text conditioning is the project-default DUAL text (T5-768):
    27	  - GLOBAL: mean-pooled caption [B,768] -> Linear -> additive per-layer (gated by
    28	    has_text for CFG).  Mirrors the denoiser's dual_text global path.
    29	  - TOKEN:  token-level caption [B,L,768] -> Linear -> per-layer cross-attention
    30	    (key-padding-masked; CFG-uncond rows contribute exactly 0).
    31	
    32	NOTE on precision: like the graph_salad denoiser, the graph-spatial block requires
    33	fp32 adjacency/geodesic and forces fp32 softmax. This model is intended to run in
    34	fp32 for the flow math (the trainer keeps z_q / graph tensors fp32); bf16 autocast
    35	may wrap the matmuls (GraphAttentionBlock is bf16-safe for features), but the
    36	adjacency/geodesic/text/skeleton conditioning tensors must match z_t.dtype on the
    37	fp32 path (enforced below, same contract as GraphSaladDenoiser).
    38	"""
    39	
    40	from __future__ import annotations
    41	
    42	import torch
    43	import torch.nn as nn
    44	
    45	from src.models.graph_salad.attention import GraphAttentionBlock
    46	from src.models.motion_decoder import TemporalSelfAttention
    47	from src.models.graph_salad.denoiser import (
    48	    SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding,
    49	    DenseFiLM,
    50	    TextCrossAttention,
    51	)
    52	
    53	
    54	class GraphCodeFlowLayer(nn.Module):
    55	    """One Level-A flow layer over the `[B,T_lat,C,D]` token grid.
    56	
    57	    Ordering mirrors the proven GraphSaladDenoiserLayer (graph_salad/denoiser.py):
    58	      graph-spatial attn -> FiLM -> temporal attn -> FiLM ->
    59	      [token cross-attn] + [global text add] -> FiLM -> strict padded re-mask.
    60	
    61	    The graph-spatial sub-block uses pooled_adjacency / pooled_geodesic exactly as
    62	    CoarseGraphTemporalLayer / the denoiser do; the temporal sub-block self-attends
    63	    over T_lat per slot. Timestep modulation is via DenseFiLM (zero-init -> identity
    64	    at init). Token cross-attn + global add are both gated by has_text for CFG.
    65	    """
    66	
    67	    def __init__(self, d_model: int, n_heads: int, d_ff: int, d_t: int,
    68	                 dropout: float = 0.1) -> None:
    69	        super().__init__()
    70	        self.spatial = GraphAttentionBlock(d_model, n_heads, d_ff, dropout=dropout)
    71	        self.temporal = TemporalSelfAttention(d_model, n_heads, dropout=dropout)
    72	        self.text_cross_attn = TextCrossAttention(d_model, n_heads, dropout=dropout)
    73	        self.film_after_spatial = DenseFiLM(d_t, d_model)
    74	        self.film_after_temporal = DenseFiLM(d_t, d_model)
    75	        self.film_after_text = DenseFiLM(d_t, d_model)
    76	
    77	    def forward(
    78	        self,
    79	        x: torch.Tensor,                  # [B, T_lat, C, D]
    80	        t_emb: torch.Tensor,              # [B, D_t]
    81	        text_global: torch.Tensor,        # [B, D] projected mean caption
    82	        has_text: torch.Tensor,           # [B] bool
    83	        tok_emb: torch.Tensor,            # [B, L, D] projected token caption
    84	        text_key_padding_mask: torch.Tensor,  # [B, L] bool, True = mask
    85	        pooled_adj: torch.Tensor,         # [B, C, C] fp32
    86	        pooled_geo: torch.Tensor,         # [B, C, C] fp32
    87	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,        # [B, C] bool
    88	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,     # [B, T_lat] bool
    89	        *,
    90	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
    91	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
    92	        B, T_lat, C, D = x.shape
    93	
    94	        # --- 1. Graph-spatial self-attn (per latent frame, over C slots) ---
    95	        x_sp_in = x.reshape(B * T_lat, C, D)
    96	        adj_exp = pooled_adj.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C, C)
    97	        geo_exp = pooled_geo.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C, C)
    98	        cm_exp = coarse_mask.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C)
    99	        x_sp = self.spatial(x_sp_in, adj_exp, geo_exp, cm_exp,
   100	                            validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   101	        x = x_sp.reshape(B, T_lat, C, D)
   102	        x = self.film_after_spatial(x, t_emb)
   103	
   104	        # --- 2. Temporal self-attn (per slot, over T_lat frames) ---
   105	        x_t_in = x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().reshape(B * C, T_lat, D)
   106	        fm_exp = frame_mask_lat.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, C, T_lat).reshape(B * C, T_lat)
   107	        x_t = self.temporal(x_t_in, fm_exp)
   108	        x = x_t.reshape(B, C, T_lat, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
   109	        x = self.film_after_temporal(x, t_emb)
   110	
   111	        # --- 3. Dual text: token cross-attn THEN global add (both has_text-gated) ---
   112	        q = x.reshape(B, T_lat * C, D)
   113	        ca = self.text_cross_attn(q, tok_emb, text_key_padding_mask)
   114	        x = x + ca.reshape(B, T_lat, C, D)
   115	        text_gated = text_global * has_text[:, None].to(text_global.dtype)  # [B, D]
   116	        x = x + text_gated[:, None, None, :]
   117	        x = self.film_after_text(x, t_emb)
   118	
   119	        # --- 4. Strict padded re-mask (padded slots/frames must be 0 after layer) ---
   120	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(x.dtype)
   121	        fm = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(x.dtype)
   122	        return x * cm * fm
   123	
   124	
   125	class GraphStructuredCodeFlow(nn.Module):
   126	    """Level-A graph rectified-flow velocity network over post-RVQ z_q.
   127	
   128	    forward(z_t, timesteps, text_global, text_tokens, text_token_mask, has_text,
   129	            pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic, pooled_skeleton_embeddings,
   130	            coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat) -> v_pred [B, T_lat, C, D].
   131	
   132	    Skip-transformer (SALAD-style, n_layers odd: enc + mid + dec with symmetric
   133	    skips) of GraphCodeFlowLayer blocks, mirroring GraphSaladDenoiser's structure
   134	    but operating on the frozen RVQ code grid (D = code_dim) instead of the
   135	    Gaussian latent. Output zero-init so v_pred ≈ 0 at init (flow-stable).
   136	    """
   137	
   138	    def __init__(
   139	        self,
   140	        code_dim: int = 512,
   141	        n_heads: int = 8,
   142	        d_ff: int | None = None,
   143	        n_layers: int = 5,
   144	        d_text: int = 768,
   145	        text_token_dim: int = 768,
   146	        d_t: int | None = None,
   147	        dropout: float = 0.1,
   148	    ) -> None:
   149	        super().__init__()
   150	        if n_layers % 2 == 0:
   151	            raise ValueError(
   152	                f"n_layers must be odd for the SALAD skip-transformer, got {n_layers}")
   153	        if code_dim % n_heads != 0:
   154	            raise ValueError(f"code_dim ({code_dim}) must divide n_heads ({n_heads})")
   155	        if d_ff is None:
   156	            d_ff = 4 * code_dim
   157	        if d_t is None:
   158	            d_t = code_dim
   159	        self.code_dim = code_dim
   160	        self.d_model = code_dim  # the token grid feature dim == RVQ code_dim
   161	        self.n_heads = n_heads
   162	        self.d_ff = d_ff
   163	        self.n_layers = n_layers
   164	        self.d_text = d_text
   165	        self.text_token_dim = text_token_dim
   166	        self.d_t = d_t
   167	
   168	        # Timestep embedding (sinusoidal -> MLP), shared across all FiLMs.
   169	        self.t_sin = SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding(d_t)
   170	        self.t_mlp = nn.Sequential(
   171	            nn.Linear(d_t, d_t * 4), nn.SiLU(), nn.Linear(d_t * 4, d_t))
   172	
   173	        # Dual-text projections (T5-768 -> code_dim): global mean + token level.
   174	        self.text_proj = nn.Linear(d_text, code_dim)
   175	        self.text_token_proj = nn.Linear(text_token_dim, code_dim)
   176	
   177	        # Input projection: z_t + additive skeleton-slot conditioning.
   178	        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   179	
   180	        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([
   181	            GraphCodeFlowLayer(code_dim, n_heads, d_ff, d_t, dropout=dropout)
   182	            for _ in range(n_layers)
   183	        ])
   184	        self.depth = (n_layers - 1) // 2
   185	        self.skip_mergers = nn.ModuleList(
   186	            [nn.Linear(2 * code_dim, code_dim) for _ in range(self.depth)])
   187	
   188	        # Output: pre-norm + zero-init linear -> initial v_pred ≈ 0.
   189	        self.output_norm = nn.LayerNorm(code_dim)
   190	        self.output_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   191	        nn.init.zeros_(self.output_proj.weight)
   192	        nn.init.zeros_(self.output_proj.bias)
   193	
   194	    def forward(
   195	        self,
   196	        z_t: torch.Tensor,                       # [B, T_lat, C, D]
   197	        timesteps: torch.Tensor,                 # [B] (float in [0,1])
   198	        text_global: torch.Tensor,               # [B, 768] mean caption
   199	        text_tokens: torch.Tensor,               # [B, L, 768] token caption
   200	        text_token_mask: torch.Tensor,           # [B, L] bool, True = valid token
   201	        has_text: torch.Tensor,                  # [B] bool (CFG gate)
   202	        pooled_adjacency: torch.Tensor,          # [B, C, C]
   203	        pooled_geodesic: torch.Tensor,           # [B, C, C]
   204	        pooled_skeleton_embeddings: torch.Tensor,  # [B, C, D]
   205	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,               # [B, C] bool
   206	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,            # [B, T_lat] bool
   207	        *,
   208	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
   209	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   210	        if z_t.dim() != 4 or z_t.shape[-1] != self.code_dim:
   211	            raise ValueError(
   212	                f"z_t must be [B,T_lat,C,D={self.code_dim}], got {tuple(z_t.shape)}")
   213	        B, T_lat, C, D = z_t.shape
   214	        ref_device = z_t.device
   215	        # ---- contract checks (mirror GraphSaladDenoiser; fail-loud) ----
   216	        if timesteps.shape != (B,):
   217	            raise ValueError(f"timesteps must be [B={B}], got {tuple(timesteps.shape)}")
   218	        if coarse_mask.shape != (B, C) or coarse_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   219	            raise ValueError(
   220	                f"coarse_mask must be [B={B},C={C}] bool, got "
   221	                f"{tuple(coarse_mask.shape)} {coarse_mask.dtype}")
   222	        if frame_mask_lat.shape != (B, T_lat) or frame_mask_lat.dtype != torch.bool:
   223	            raise ValueError(
   224	                f"frame_mask_lat must be [B={B},T_lat={T_lat}] bool, got "
   225	                f"{tuple(frame_mask_lat.shape)} {frame_mask_lat.dtype}")
   226	        if has_text.shape != (B,) or has_text.dtype != torch.bool:
   227	            raise ValueError(
   228	                f"has_text must be [B={B}] bool, got {tuple(has_text.shape)} "
   229	                f"{has_text.dtype}")
   230	        if pooled_adjacency.shape != (B, C, C) or pooled_geodesic.shape != (B, C, C):
   231	            raise ValueError(
   232	                f"pooled_adjacency/geodesic must be [B={B},C={C},C={C}], got "
   233	                f"{tuple(pooled_adjacency.shape)} / {tuple(pooled_geodesic.shape)}")
   234	        if pooled_skeleton_embeddings.shape != (B, C, D):
   235	            raise ValueError(
   236	                f"pooled_skeleton_embeddings must be [B={B},C={C},D={D}], got "
   237	                f"{tuple(pooled_skeleton_embeddings.shape)}")
   238	        if text_global.dim() != 2 or text_global.shape != (B, self.d_text):
   239	            raise ValueError(
   240	                f"text_global must be [B={B},{self.d_text}], got {tuple(text_global.shape)}")
   241	        if (text_tokens.dim() != 3 or text_tokens.shape[0] != B
   242	                or text_tokens.shape[2] != self.text_token_dim):
   243	            raise ValueError(
   244	                f"text_tokens must be [B={B},L,{self.text_token_dim}], got "
   245	                f"{tuple(text_tokens.shape)}")
   246	        L = text_tokens.shape[1]
   247	        if text_token_mask.shape != (B, L) or text_token_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   248	            raise ValueError(
   249	                f"text_token_mask must be [B={B},L={L}] bool, got "
   250	                f"{tuple(text_token_mask.shape)} {text_token_mask.dtype}")
   251	        for name, t in (
   252	            ("timesteps", timesteps), ("coarse_mask", coarse_mask),
   253	            ("frame_mask_lat", frame_mask_lat), ("has_text", has_text),
   254	            ("pooled_adjacency", pooled_adjacency), ("pooled_geodesic", pooled_geodesic),
   255	            ("pooled_skeleton_embeddings", pooled_skeleton_embeddings),
   256	            ("text_global", text_global), ("text_tokens", text_tokens),
   257	            ("text_token_mask", text_token_mask),
   258	        ):
   259	            if t.device != ref_device:
   260	                raise ValueError(
   261	                    f"GraphStructuredCodeFlow: {name}.device {t.device} != "
   262	                    f"z_t.device {ref_device}")
   263	        # Float conditioning tensors must match z_t.dtype on the fp32 path (same
   264	        # contract as GraphSaladDenoiser / GraphAttentionBlock).
   265	        if z_t.dtype in (torch.float32, torch.float64):
   266	            for name, t in (
   267	                ("pooled_adjacency", pooled_adjacency),
   268	                ("pooled_geodesic", pooled_geodesic),
   269	                ("pooled_skeleton_embeddings", pooled_skeleton_embeddings),
   270	                ("text_global", text_global), ("text_tokens", text_tokens),
   271	            ):
   272	                if t.dtype != z_t.dtype:
   273	                    raise ValueError(
   274	                        f"GraphStructuredCodeFlow: {name}.dtype {t.dtype} != "
   275	                        f"z_t.dtype {z_t.dtype}")
   276	
   277	        # ---- timestep embedding ----
   278	        t_emb = self.t_mlp(self.t_sin(timesteps))         # [B, D_t]
   279	
   280	        # ---- dual-text prep (project + build the shared key-padding mask) ----
   281	        text_global_proj = self.text_proj(text_global)    # [B, D]
   282	        tok_emb = self.text_token_proj(text_tokens)        # [B, L, D]
   283	        # valid key = token present AND has_text=True; key_padding_mask is the
   284	        # inverse (True = mask). has_text=False -> whole row masked -> cross-attn
   285	        # output zeroed in TextCrossAttention (CFG-uncond contributes 0).
   286	        valid_key = text_token_mask & has_text[:, None]    # [B, L]
   287	        text_key_padding_mask = ~valid_key                 # [B, L] True = mask
   288	
   289	        # ---- input projection + additive skeleton conditioning ----
   290	        x = self.input_proj(z_t)                           # [B, T_lat, C, D]
   291	        x = x + pooled_skeleton_embeddings.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, T_lat, -1, -1)
   292	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(x.dtype)
   293	        fm = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(x.dtype)
   294	        x = x * cm * fm
   295	
   296	        # ---- skip-transformer: enc -> mid -> dec with symmetric skips ----
   297	        def _run(layer, h):
   298	            return layer(
   299	                h, t_emb, text_global_proj, has_text, tok_emb, text_key_padding_mask,
   300	                pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic, coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat,
   301	                validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   302	
   303	        enc_outputs: list[torch.Tensor] = []
   304	        for i in range(self.depth):
   305	            x = _run(self.layers[i], x)
   306	            enc_outputs.append(x)
   307	        x = _run(self.layers[self.depth], x)
   308	        for i in range(self.depth):
   309	            dec_layer = self.layers[self.depth + 1 + i]
   310	            skip = enc_outputs[self.depth - 1 - i]
   311	            x = torch.cat([x, skip], dim=-1)               # [B, T_lat, C, 2D]
   312	            x = self.skip_mergers[i](x)                     # [B, T_lat, C, D]
   313	            x = _run(dec_layer, x)
   314	
   315	        # ---- output: pre-norm + zero-init linear + final re-mask ----
   316	        x = self.output_norm(x)
   317	        v_pred = self.output_proj(x)
   318	        return v_pred * cm * fm

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""TokenCacheDataset — reads the offline RVQ token cache produced by
     2	scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py for Graph-CodeFlow training.
     3	
     4	Each item is one exported clip: the post-RVQ z_q target + graph metadata + dual
     5	text caption tensors. The CodeFlow trainer reads these instead of running the
     6	frozen tokenizer encoder online every step (handoff §5.1).
     7	
     8	Padding is along the C (coarse-slot) and T_lat axes and is ALREADY baked into the
     9	export (token_mask/coarse_mask/frame_mask_lat). All exported clips share the same
    10	[T_lat, C_max, D, Q] padded shape (from the frozen tokenizer's max_coarse /
    11	temporal_stride), so the default collate stacks them directly — no ragged collate.
    12	The pooled_geodesic sentinel (export GEO_INF_SENTINEL for +inf) is mapped back to
    13	+inf here so GraphAttentionBlock sees its real unreachable-pair contract.
    14	"""
    15	
    16	from __future__ import annotations
    17	
    18	import json
    19	from pathlib import Path
    20	
    21	import numpy as np
    22	import torch
    23	from torch.utils.data import Dataset
    24	
    25	GEO_INF_SENTINEL = 30000.0
    26	
    27	
    28	class TokenCacheDataset(Dataset):
    29	    def __init__(self, cache_dir: str, split: str,
    30	                 geo_inf_sentinel: float = GEO_INF_SENTINEL) -> None:
    31	        self.split_dir = Path(cache_dir) / split
    32	        idx_path = self.split_dir / "index.jsonl"
    33	        if not idx_path.exists():
    34	            raise FileNotFoundError(
    35	                f"TokenCacheDataset: {idx_path} not found (run "
    36	                f"scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py first)")
    37	        self.rows = [json.loads(l) for l in idx_path.read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
    38	        if not self.rows:
    39	            raise RuntimeError(f"TokenCacheDataset: empty index {idx_path}")
    40	        self.geo_inf_sentinel = float(geo_inf_sentinel)
    41	
    42	    def __len__(self) -> int:
    43	        return len(self.rows)
    44	
    45	    def __getitem__(self, i: int) -> dict:
    46	        row = self.rows[i]
    47	        d = np.load(self.split_dir / row["file"], allow_pickle=False)
    48	        geo = d["pooled_geodesic"].astype(np.float32)
    49	        geo[geo >= self.geo_inf_sentinel] = np.inf
    50	        return {
    51	            "z_q": torch.from_numpy(d["z_q"].astype(np.float32)),           # [T_lat,C,D]
    52	            "indices": torch.from_numpy(d["indices"].astype(np.int64)),     # [T_lat,C,Q]
    53	            "token_mask": torch.from_numpy(d["token_mask"].astype(np.bool_)),
    54	            "coarse_mask": torch.from_numpy(d["coarse_mask"].astype(np.bool_)),
    55	            "frame_mask_lat": torch.from_numpy(d["frame_mask_lat"].astype(np.bool_)),
    56	            "pooled_adjacency": torch.from_numpy(d["pooled_adjacency"].astype(np.float32)),
    57	            "pooled_geodesic": torch.from_numpy(geo),
    58	            "pooled_skeleton_embeddings": torch.from_numpy(
    59	                d["pooled_skeleton_embeddings"].astype(np.float32)),
    60	            "assignment": torch.from_numpy(d["assignment"].astype(np.float32)),  # [J,C]
    61	            "s_j": torch.from_numpy(d["s_j"].astype(np.float32)),               # [J,D]
    62	            "joint_mask": torch.from_numpy(d["joint_mask"].astype(np.bool_)),
    63	            "rest_offsets": torch.from_numpy(d["rest_offsets"].astype(np.float32)),
    64	            "anytop_mean": torch.from_numpy(d["anytop_mean"].astype(np.float32)),
    65	            "anytop_std": torch.from_numpy(d["anytop_std"].astype(np.float32)),
    66	            "parent_indices": [int(p) for p in d["parent_indices"].tolist()],
    67	            "num_joints": int(d["num_joints"]),
    68	            "caption_emb": torch.from_numpy(d["caption_emb"].astype(np.float32)),  # [768]
    69	            "caption_token_emb": torch.from_numpy(
    70	                d["caption_token_emb"].astype(np.float32)),                  # [L,768]
    71	            "caption_token_mask": torch.from_numpy(d["caption_token_mask"].astype(np.bool_)),
    72	            "has_text": bool(d["has_text"]),
    73	            "object_type": row["object_type"],
    74	            "text": row.get("text", ""),
    75	        }
    76	
    77	
    78	def token_collate(batch: list[dict]) -> dict:
    79	    out: dict = {}
    80	    keys = batch[0].keys()
    81	    for k in keys:
    82	        v0 = batch[0][k]
    83	        if isinstance(v0, torch.Tensor):
    84	            out[k] = torch.stack([b[k] for b in batch])
    85	        elif isinstance(v0, bool):
    86	            out[k] = torch.tensor([b[k] for b in batch], dtype=torch.bool)
    87	        elif isinstance(v0, int):
    88	            out[k] = torch.tensor([b[k] for b in batch], dtype=torch.int64)
    89	        else:
    90	            out[k] = [b[k] for b in batch]
    91	    return out

     1	"""GraphPSCFFlowNet — graph-aware PSCF / FLUX-style double-stream + single-stream
     2	rectified-flow backbone over the FROZEN Graph-VQVAE post-RVQ z_q grid.
     3	
     4	Spec (LOCKED): handoff/20260609_1650_graph_pscf_final_executor_spec.md
     5	Plan §4:      handoff/20260609_graph_codeflow_pscf_double_single_impl_plan.md
     6	
     7	This is the formal backbone replacing the Level-A `GraphStructuredCodeFlow` probe.
     8	It is PURE ADDITIVE: it only imports (never copies) the shared audited ingredients
     9	and does NOT touch the Gaussian VAE / latent diffusion / Graph-VQVAE training /
    10	graph_salad behavior.
    11	
    12	Three internal streams (plan §3):
    13	  slot stream:  h_slot  [B, T_lat, C, D]   graph-pooled RVQ latent grid
    14	  frame stream: h_frame [B, T_lat, D]      graph-aware frame holder tokens (persistent, seeded once — Q4)
    15	  text stream:  h_text  [B, L, D]          T5 token stream
    16	
    17	Conditioning is two-path text (Q2):
    18	  - pooled_text -> cond (= timestep_emb + pooled_text, CFG-gated) -> every AdaLN/FiLM
    19	  - token_text  -> h_text stream -> double/single DiT joint attention
    20	
    21	The three new modules:
    22	  - GraphSlotTemporalBlock   keeps the slot stream graph-aware (GraphAttentionBlock
    23	                              over C with pooled adjacency/geodesic) + temporal
    24	                              (TemporalSelfAttention over T_lat), with DenseFiLM
    25	                              modulation. Mirrors graph_codeflow.py:94-122.
    26	  - GraphFrameSlotCoupling   NON-GRAPH (Q1) bridge: the frame holder reads slots
    27	                              via masked cross-attention; slots receive the updated
    28	                              frame via a FiLM-gated additive injection. Does NOT
    29	                              build a [1+C] extended graph (Floyd hub collapse).
    30	  - GraphPSCFFlowNet         6 double-stream + 12 single-stream stack with graph
    31	                              slot stream + couplings, zero-init velocity output.
    32	
    33	bf16 discipline: fp32 softmax everywhere; the ported DiT uses a bf16 -1e4 / fp32
    34	-1e9 mask sentinel. Graph tensors (adjacency / geodesic / skeleton) stay fp32 for
    35	GraphAttentionBlock. Strict 2D `[T_lat,C]` re-mask after every sub-block.
    36	"""
    37	
    38	from __future__ import annotations
    39

    41

    49	    SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding,
