 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2	"""Offline RVQ token export for Graph-CodeFlow (handoff/20260609_graph_codeflow_
     3	rvq_backbone_plan.md §5.1 + M1).
     4	
     5	Runs the FROZEN Graph-VQVAE tokenizer over the AnyTop L5 dataset and caches, per
     6	clip, the post-RVQ targets + graph metadata + caption refs the CodeFlow backbone
     7	needs so training never re-runs the encoder online:
     8	
     9	  z_q                         [T_lat,C,D] fp16   (RAW frozen RVQ z_q target)
    10	  indices                     [T_lat,C,Q] int16  (-1 on padded tokens)
    11	  token_mask                  [T_lat,C]   bool
    12	  coarse_mask                 [C]         bool
    13	  frame_mask_lat              [T_lat]     bool
    14	  pooled_adjacency            [C,C]       fp16
    15	  pooled_geodesic             [C,C]       fp16  (+inf -> large sentinel on save)
    16	  pooled_skeleton_embeddings  [C,D]       fp16
    17	  assignment                  [J,C]       fp16
    18	  parent_indices, rest_offsets, anytop_mean/std, joint_mask  (decode metadata)
    19	  caption_emb [768] + caption_token_emb [L,768] + caption_token_mask [L] + text
    20	
    21	HARD export invariants (review verdict gaps #3/#4 + M1 gates):
    22	  - tokenizer.eval()  (training-mode RVQ has quantizer-dropout that truncates depth)
    23	  - allow_collectives=False  (single-process; no cross-rank collectives)
    24	  - FULL Q (eval => no dropout)  asserted: every VALID token has all-Q indices >= 0
    25	  - captions ON (load_captions=True + caption caches), unlike VQ training
    26	  - strict shape audit + padded IDs exactly -1 + valid IDs in [0,K-1]
    27	  - ids_to_embeddings(indices) ~= cached z_q within tol  (per-clip)
    28	  - train/val split mirrors the VQVAE source split (val_frac/seed from the ckpt)
    29	
    30	For the SMOKE, pass --max_clips to export only a few clips to /tmp; the FULL export
    31	waits for the ep300 frozen ckpt.
    32	"""
    33	from __future__ import annotations
    34	
    35	import argparse
    36	import json
    37	import math
    38	import sys
    39	from pathlib import Path
    40	
    41	import numpy as np
    42	import torch
    43	
    44	ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
    45	sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
    46	
    47	from src.data.anytop_dataset import AnyTopDataset, collate_fn as anytop_collate_fn
    48	from src.models.graph_salad.batch import GraphMotionBatch
    49	from src.models.vq_model import GraphVQTokenizer
    50	
    51	# Sentinel for +inf in pooled_geodesic on disk (unreachable pairs). The CodeFlow
    52	# loader maps it back to +inf before feeding GraphAttentionBlock. Chosen well
    53	# above any real hop count (<= C-1 <= 49) and within fp16 range.
    54	GEO_INF_SENTINEL = 30000.0
    55	
    56	
    57	def load_frozen_tokenizer(ckpt_path: str, dev: torch.device):
    58	    """Rebuild GraphVQTokenizer from a train_graph_vqvae.py ckpt (saved `args`),
    59	    load weights strict=True, eval(). Mirrors animate_vqvae_recon.load_vq_tokenizer."""
    60	    ck = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    61	    ta = ck.get("args", {})
    62	    if not ta:
    63	        raise SystemExit(f"export: ckpt {ckpt_path} missing 'args' "
    64	                         f"(not from train_graph_vqvae.py?)")
    65	    model = GraphVQTokenizer(
    66	        d_model=ta["d_model"], n_heads=ta["n_heads"], d_ff=ta["d_ff"],
    67	        n_graph_layers=ta["n_graph_layers"],
    68	        n_enc_temporal_layers=ta["n_enc_temporal_layers"],
    69	        n_pre_vq_layers=ta["n_pre_vq_layers"],
    70	        n_post_vq_layers=ta["n_post_vq_layers"],
    71	        n_cross_layers=ta["n_cross_layers"],
    72	        n_dec_temporal_layers=ta["n_dec_temporal_layers"],
    73	        max_coarse=ta["max_coarse"], temporal_stride=ta["temporal_stride"],
    74	        temporal_kernel=ta["temporal_kernel"], dropout=ta["dropout"],
    75	        code_dim=ta["code_dim"], num_codes=ta["num_codes"],
    76	        num_quantizers=ta["num_quantizers"], ema_mu=ta["ema_mu"],
    77	        quantize_dropout_prob=ta["quantize_dropout_prob"],
    78	        dead_code_threshold=ta["dead_code_threshold"],
    79	    ).to(dev)
    80	    model.load_state_dict(ck["model_state_dict"], strict=True)
    81	    model.eval()
    82	    return model, ta, ck
    83	
    84	
    85	def main() -> int:
    86	    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    87	    ap.add_argument("--frozen_vqvae_ckpt", required=True)
    88	    ap.add_argument("--out", required=True, help="output dir for per-split token caches")
    89	    ap.add_argument("--splits", default="train,val")
    90	    ap.add_argument("--anytop_root", type=str, default=None,
    91	                    help="defaults to the ckpt's training root")
    92	    ap.add_argument("--caption_emb_cache", type=str,
    93	                    default="data/anytop_caption_t5_cleanL5_multi.npz")
    94	    ap.add_argument("--caption_token_cache", type=str,
    95	                    default="data/anytop_caption_t5_cleanL5_multi",
    96	                    help="prefix for .tokens.npy/.token_mask.npy/.keys.json")
    97	    ap.add_argument("--caption_token_max_len", type=int, default=64)
    98	    ap.add_argument("--num_frames", type=int, default=None,
    99	                    help="override the frozen tokenizer ckpt's max_frames; None = "
   100	                         "use ckpt's max_frames. Full-length backbone training uses "
   101	                         "300 -> T_lat up to 75")
   102	    ap.add_argument("--min_text_coverage", type=float, default=0.99,
   103	                    help="PREFLIGHT: minimum fraction of exported clips that must "
   104	                         "carry non-empty text (non-zero caption_emb AND "
   105	                         "caption_token_mask.sum()>0). Default 0.99 fails loud on a "
   106	                         "text-less cache; lower it explicitly for a deliberate "
   107	                         "unconditional export.")
   108	    ap.add_argument("--max_clips", type=int, default=0,
   109	                    help="SMOKE: cap clips per split (0 = all)")
   110	    ap.add_argument("--shard_idx", type=int, default=0,
   111	                    help="SHARD: this process exports the disjoint subset of clips "
   112	                         "with (dataset_index %% num_shards == shard_idx)")
   113	    ap.add_argument("--num_shards", type=int, default=1,
   114	                    help="SHARD: total parallel processes. >1 enables sharded mode: "
   115	                         "each shard writes index_shard{idx:03d}.jsonl (not the shared "
   116	                         "index.jsonl/manifest.json; the merge step writes those). "
   117	                         "1 (default) = single-process path, unchanged.")
   118	    ap.add_argument("--amp_dtype", choices=["fp32", "bf16"], default=None,
   119	                    help="autocast dtype; defaults to the ckpt's training amp_dtype")
   120	    ap.add_argument("--identity_tol", type=float, default=1e-2,
   121	                    help="max |ids_to_embeddings(indices) - z_q| tolerated on valid "
   122	                         "tokens (fp16 storage round-trip; the fp32 audit before "
   123	                         "casting must be ~1e-5)")
   124	    ap.add_argument("--device", default="cuda")
   125	    args = ap.parse_args()
   126	
   127	    if args.num_shards < 1:
   128	        raise SystemExit(f"export: --num_shards must be >= 1 (got {args.num_shards})")
   129	    if not (0 <= args.shard_idx < args.num_shards):
   130	        raise SystemExit(f"export: --shard_idx must be in [0, num_shards) "
   131	                         f"(got shard_idx={args.shard_idx}, num_shards={args.num_shards})")
   132	    sharded = args.num_shards > 1
   133	
   134	    if args.device == "cuda" and not torch.cuda.is_available():
   135	        print("  [INFO] CUDA unavailable -> CPU")
   136	        args.device = "cpu"
   137	    dev = torch.device(args.device)
   138	    out_dir = Path(args.out)
   139	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   140	
   141	    model, ta, ck = load_frozen_tokenizer(args.frozen_vqvae_ckpt, dev)
   142	    K = ta["num_codes"]
   143	    Q = ta["num_quantizers"]
   144	    D = ta["d_model"]
   145	    print(f"Frozen tokenizer: d_model={D} max_coarse={ta['max_coarse']} Q={Q} "
   146	          f"K={K} stride={ta['temporal_stride']} | ckpt epoch={ck.get('epoch')} "
   147	          f"val_total={ck.get('val_total')}")
   148	
   149	    amp_dtype = args.amp_dtype or ta.get("amp_dtype", "bf16")
   150	    amp_enabled = (amp_dtype == "bf16") and dev.type == "cuda"
   151	    anytop_root = args.anytop_root or ta.get("anytop_root") or ck.get("data_root")
   152	
   153	    # Resolve clip frame length: override the frozen ckpt's max_frames when
   154	    # --num_frames is given (full-length backbone export uses 300). T_lat is the
   155	    # latent frame count at the tokenizer's temporal_stride (ceil for the partial
   156	    # tail window the conv stack keeps).
   157	    num_frames = args.num_frames if args.num_frames is not None else ta.get("max_frames", 64)
   158	    temporal_stride = ta["temporal_stride"]
   159	    T_lat = math.ceil(num_frames / temporal_stride)
   160	    print(f"Export frame length: num_frames={num_frames} "
   161	          f"(ckpt max_frames={ta.get('max_frames', 64)}) -> T_lat={T_lat} "
   162	          f"@ stride={temporal_stride}")
   163	
   164	    # Caption caches ON (gap #3): VQ training used load_captions=False; the
   165	    # CodeFlow backbone needs T5 mean + token captions, so the exporter must open
   166	    # them. dual_text => return_caption_tokens=True (mean + token caches).
   167	    ds_common = dict(
   168	        num_frames=num_frames, max_joints=ta.get("max_joints", 64),
   169	        val_frac=ta.get("val_frac", 0.05), seed=ta.get("seed", 42),
   170	        data_root=anytop_root, load_captions=True,
   171	        caption_emb_cache=args.caption_emb_cache,
   172	        caption_token_cache=args.caption_token_cache,
   173	        return_caption_tokens=True,
   174	        caption_token_max_len=args.caption_token_max_len,
   175	        random_caption=False,  # deterministic primary caption for the export
   176	    )
   177	
   178	    manifest = {"frozen_vqvae_ckpt": str(args.frozen_vqvae_ckpt),
   179	                "ckpt_epoch": ck.get("epoch"), "D": D, "Q": Q, "K": K,
   180	                "max_coarse": ta["max_coarse"], "temporal_stride": temporal_stride,
   181	                "num_frames": num_frames, "T_lat": T_lat,
   182	                "ckpt_max_frames": ta.get("max_frames", 64),
   183	                "anytop_root": str(anytop_root), "amp_dtype": amp_dtype,
   184	                "geo_inf_sentinel": GEO_INF_SENTINEL, "splits": {}}
   185	
   186	    for split in [s.strip() for s in args.splits.split(",") if s.strip()]:
   187	        ds = AnyTopDataset(split=split, **ds_common)
   188	        n = len(ds) if args.max_clips <= 0 else min(args.max_clips, len(ds))
   189	        # Disjoint 1/num_shards subset of clips by dataset index (==1 -> all clips).
   190	        # The per-clip npz keeps its dataset-index filename {i:06d}.npz, so shards
   191	        # write disjoint filenames into the SAME split dir with no collision.
   192	        shard_indices = [i for i in range(n) if i % args.num_shards == args.shard_idx]
   193	        if sharded:
   194	            print(f"\n[{split}] {len(ds)} clips; shard {args.shard_idx}/{args.num_shards} "
   195	                  f"-> {len(shard_indices)} clips")
   196	        else:
   197	            print(f"\n[{split}] {len(ds)} clips (exporting {n})")
   198	        split_dir = out_dir / split
   199	        split_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   200	
   201	        # ---- PREFLIGHT (fail-loud text-coverage gate) ----
   202	        # Before any export, verify the clips actually carry text. A clip "has
   203	        # text" iff caption_emb is not all-zero AND caption_token_mask.sum()>0.
   204	        # Coverage below --min_text_coverage (or any required text field all-zero
   205	        # across every clip) aborts loudly: never silently emit a text-less cache.
   206	        n_with_text = 0
   207	        any_emb_nonzero = False
   208	        any_tokmask_nonzero = False
   209	        for i in shard_indices:
   210	            item = ds[i]
   211	            emb_nonzero = bool(item["caption_emb"].abs().sum().item() > 0)
   212	            tokmask_sum = int(item["caption_token_mask"].sum().item())
   213	            any_emb_nonzero = any_emb_nonzero or emb_nonzero
   214	            any_tokmask_nonzero = any_tokmask_nonzero or (tokmask_sum > 0)
   215	            if emb_nonzero and tokmask_sum > 0:
   216	                n_with_text += 1
   217	        n_shard = len(shard_indices)
   218	        coverage = (n_with_text / n_shard) if n_shard > 0 else 0.0
   219	        print(f"[{split}] PREFLIGHT text-coverage={coverage:.4f} "
   220	              f"({n_with_text}/{n_shard}) >= min {args.min_text_coverage}")
   221	        if (not any_emb_nonzero) or (not any_tokmask_nonzero):
   222	            raise RuntimeError(
   223	                f"[PREFLIGHT FAIL] split={split}: required text field is ALL-ZERO "
   224	                f"across every clip "
   225	                f"(caption_emb nonzero={any_emb_nonzero}, "
   226	                f"caption_token_mask nonzero={any_tokmask_nonzero}). "
   227	                f"emb cache={args.caption_emb_cache} | "
   228	                f"token cache={args.caption_token_cache}. "
   229	                f"Refusing to export a text-less cache.")
   230	        if coverage < args.min_text_coverage:
   231	            raise RuntimeError(
   232	                f"[PREFLIGHT FAIL] split={split}: text-coverage {coverage:.4f} "
   233	                f"({n_with_text}/{n_shard}) < min {args.min_text_coverage}. "
   234	                f"emb cache={args.caption_emb_cache} | "
   235	                f"token cache={args.caption_token_cache}. "
   236	                f"Refusing to silently export a text-less/under-captioned cache; "
   237	                f"lower --min_text_coverage explicitly for a deliberate "
   238	                f"unconditional run.")
   239	
   240	        index_rows = []
   241	        max_id_err_fp32 = 0.0
   242	        with torch.no_grad():
   243	            for i in shard_indices:
   244	                item = ds[i]
   245	                raw = anytop_collate_fn([item])
   246	                raw = {k: v.to(dev) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v
   247	                       for k, v in raw.items()}
   248	                batch = GraphMotionBatch.from_collate_dict(raw)
   249	
   250	                if amp_enabled:
   251	                    with torch.autocast(device_type="cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16):
   252	                        enc = model.encode(batch)
   253	                        vq = model.quantizer(enc["h_lat"], enc["token_mask"],
   254	                                             allow_collectives=False)
   255	                else:
   256	                    enc = model.encode(batch)
   257	                    vq = model.quantizer(enc["h_lat"], enc["token_mask"],
   258	                                         allow_collectives=False)
   259	
   260	                z_q = vq["quantized"].float()[0]            # [T_lat,C,D]
   261	                indices = vq["indices"][0]                  # [T_lat,C,Q] long
   262	                token_mask = enc["token_mask"][0]           # [T_lat,C] bool
   263	                coarse_mask = enc["coarse_mask"][0]         # [C]
   264	                frame_mask_lat = enc["frame_mask_lat"][0]   # [T_lat]
   265	                pooled_adj = enc["pooled_adjacency"].float()[0]   # [C,C]
   266	                pooled_geo = enc["pooled_geodesic"].float()[0]    # [C,C]
   267	                pooled_skel = enc["pooled_skeleton_embeddings"].float()[0]  # [C,D]
   268	                assignment = enc["assignment"].float()[0]   # [J,C]
   269	                s_j = enc["s_j"].float()[0]                  # [J,D] (decode needs it)
   270	
   271	                # ---- HARD audits (M1 gates) ----
   272	                Tlat, C = token_mask.shape
   273	                assert z_q.shape == (Tlat, C, D), f"z_q shape {tuple(z_q.shape)}"
   274	                assert indices.shape == (Tlat, C, Q), f"indices shape {tuple(indices.shape)}"
   275	                # padded IDs exactly -1 (all stages) on padded tokens.
   276	                pad = ~token_mask
   277	                if pad.any():
   278	                    assert (indices[pad] == -1).all(), "padded token has non(-1) id"
   279	                # valid tokens: FULL Q => every stage id in [0,K-1] (eval, no dropout).
   280	                val = token_mask
   281	                if val.any():
   282	                    vi = indices[val]                       # [n_valid, Q]
   283	                    assert (vi >= 0).all(), "valid token has -1 id (dropout leaked?)"
   284	                    assert (vi < K).all(), "valid id >= K"
   285	                # ids_to_embeddings(indices) ~= z_q on valid (fp32 audit).
   286	                z_from_ids = model.ids_to_embeddings(
   287	                    indices.unsqueeze(0), token_mask.unsqueeze(0))[0]  # [T_lat,C,D]
   288	                if val.any():
   289	                    err = (z_from_ids[val] - z_q[val]).abs().max().item()
   290	                    max_id_err_fp32 = max(max_id_err_fp32, err)
   291	
   292	                # ---- save (fp16 for the big tensors; +inf geo -> sentinel) ----
   293	                geo_save = pooled_geo.clone()
   294	                geo_save[torch.isinf(geo_save)] = GEO_INF_SENTINEL
   295	                stem = item["motion_id"]
   296	                npz_path = split_dir / f"{i:06d}.npz"
   297	                np.savez_compressed(
   298	                    npz_path,
   299	                    z_q=z_q.cpu().numpy().astype(np.float16),
   300	                    indices=indices.cpu().numpy().astype(np.int16),
   301	                    token_mask=token_mask.cpu().numpy(),
   302	                    coarse_mask=coarse_mask.cpu().numpy(),
   303	                    frame_mask_lat=frame_mask_lat.cpu().numpy(),
   304	                    pooled_adjacency=pooled_adj.cpu().numpy().astype(np.float16),
   305	                    pooled_geodesic=geo_save.cpu().numpy().astype(np.float16),
   306	                    pooled_skeleton_embeddings=pooled_skel.cpu().numpy().astype(np.float16),
   307	                    assignment=assignment.cpu().numpy().astype(np.float16),
   308	                    s_j=s_j.cpu().numpy().astype(np.float16),
   309	                    joint_mask=batch.joint_mask[0].cpu().numpy(),
   310	                    rest_offsets=batch.rest_offsets[0].cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32),
   311	                    anytop_mean=batch.anytop_mean[0].cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32),
   312	                    anytop_std=batch.anytop_std[0].cpu().numpy().astype(np.float32),
   313	                    parent_indices=np.asarray(item["parent_indices"], dtype=np.int64),
   314	                    num_joints=np.int64(item["num_joints"]),
   315	                    caption_emb=item["caption_emb"].numpy().astype(np.float16),
   316	                    caption_token_emb=item["caption_token_emb"].numpy().astype(np.float16),
   317	                    caption_token_mask=item["caption_token_mask"].numpy(),
   318	                    has_text=np.bool_(item["has_text"]),
   319	                )
   320	                index_rows.append({"idx": i, "file": npz_path.name,
   321	                                   "motion_id": stem,
   322	                                   "object_type": item["object_type"],
   323	                                   "text": item.get("caption", ""),
   324	                                   "num_joints": int(item["num_joints"]),
   325	                                   "n_valid_tokens": int(token_mask.sum().item())})
   326	                if (i + 1) % 50 == 0 or i == n - 1:
   327	                    print(f"  [{split}] {i + 1}/{n}  id_err_fp32(max)={max_id_err_fp32:.2e}")
   328	
   329	        if max_id_err_fp32 > args.identity_tol:
   330	            raise RuntimeError(
   331	                f"[{split}] ids_to_embeddings(indices) vs z_q fp32 max err "
   332	                f"{max_id_err_fp32:.3e} > tol {args.identity_tol} (RVQ identity broken)")
   333	        if sharded:
   334	            # Per-shard index file (disjoint name -> no race on the shared split dir);
   335	            # the merge step concatenates these into index.jsonl + writes manifest.json.
   336	            shard_index = split_dir / f"index_shard{args.shard_idx:03d}.jsonl"
   337	            shard_index.write_text(
   338	                "\n".join(json.dumps(r) for r in index_rows) + "\n")
   339	            # Per-shard manifest sidecar: global static fields + THIS shard's
   340	            # max_id_err_fp32. The merge step reads these to assemble the final
   341	            # manifest.json (CPU-only, no ckpt/torch reload). Same :03d zero-pad as
   342	            # the index sidecar so the merge can read both by f"...{s:03d}...".
   343	            shard_manifest = {k: v for k, v in manifest.items() if k != "splits"}
   344	            shard_manifest["max_id_err_fp32"] = max_id_err_fp32
   345	            (split_dir / f"manifest_shard{args.shard_idx:03d}.json").write_text(
   346	                json.dumps(shard_manifest, indent=2))
   347	            print(f"[shard {args.shard_idx}/{args.num_shards}] split={split} "
   348	                  f"wrote {len(index_rows)} npz")
   349	        else:
   350	            (split_dir / "index.jsonl").write_text(
   351	                "\n".join(json.dumps(r) for r in index_rows) + "\n")
   352	            manifest["splits"][split] = {"n": n, "max_id_err_fp32": max_id_err_fp32}
   353	            print(f"  [{split}] DONE {n} clips -> {split_dir} "
   354	                  f"(RVQ-identity fp32 max err {max_id_err_fp32:.2e})")
   355	
   356	    if sharded:
   357	        print(f"\n[shard {args.shard_idx}/{args.num_shards}] export complete -> {out_dir} "
   358	              f"(no manifest.json in sharded mode; the merge step writes it)")
   359	    else:
   360	        (out_dir / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2))
   361	        print(f"\nExport complete -> {out_dir}\nmanifest: {out_dir / 'manifest.json'}")
   362	    return 0
   363	
   364	
   365	if __name__ == "__main__":
   366	    raise SystemExit(main())

   280	    p.add_argument("--warmup_steps", type=int, default=2000)
   281	    p.add_argument("--eta_min_ratio", type=float, default=0.01)
   282	    p.add_argument("--weight_decay", type=float, default=0.01)
   283	    p.add_argument("--grad_clip", type=float, default=1.0)
   284	    p.add_argument("--cond_drop_prob", type=float, default=0.1)
   285	    p.add_argument("--flow_loss_weight", type=float, default=1.0)
   286	    p.add_argument("--terminal_loss_weight", type=float, default=0.0)
   287	    p.add_argument("--clean_loss_weight", type=float, default=0.0)
   288	    p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=42)
   289	    p.add_argument("--amp_dtype", choices=["fp32", "bf16"], default="bf16")
   290	    p.add_argument("--num_workers", type=int, default=8)
   291	    p.add_argument("--empirical_stats_max_clips", type=int, default=0,
   292	                   help="0 (default) = use ALL train clips for the empirical z_q "
   293	                        "norm (LOCKED: full train-set stats). A positive value caps "
   294	                        "to a PREFIX — smoke/debug only, NOT the real run.")
   295	    # eval / cfg
   296	    p.add_argument("--eval_cond_scale", type=float, default=4.0,
   297	                   help="CFG scale for sampling QA — SWEEP starting point, NOT a "
   298	                        "fixed default (project energy-overshoot history; recipe "
   299	                        "says do not hardcode 6.0).")
   300	    p.add_argument("--eval_steps", type=int, default=50)
   301	    # logging / ckpt
   302	    p.add_argument("--log_every", type=int, default=50)
   303	    p.add_argument("--qa_every", type=int, default=200,
   304	                   help="run the decode-based continuous-vs-snapped QA every N steps")
   305	    p.add_argument("--save_every", type=int, default=10)
   306	    p.add_argument("--out", required=True)
   307	    p.add_argument("--device", default="cuda")
   308	    p.add_argument("--overwrite", action="store_true")
   309	    p.add_argument("--resume", type=str, default=None)
   310	    p.add_argument("--smoke", action="store_true")
   311	    p.add_argument("--smoke_iters", type=int, default=4)
   312	    p.add_argument("--mem_profile", action="store_true",
   313	                   help="one fwd+bwd at --batch_size, report peak CUDA mem, exit")
   314	    args = p.parse_args()
   315	
   316	    is_ddp, rank, local_rank, world_size, is_main = _ddp_setup()
   317	    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)
   318	    np.random.seed(args.seed)
   319	    if torch.cuda.is_available():
   320	        torch.cuda.manual_seed_all(args.seed)
   321	    torch.backends.cuda.matmul.allow_tf32 = True
   322	    torch.backends.cudnn.allow_tf32 = True
   323	    torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = True
   324	
   325	    if is_ddp:
   326	        dev = torch.device("cuda", local_rank)
   327	    else:
   328	        if args.device == "cuda" and not torch.cuda.is_available():
   329	            raise RuntimeError("[DEVICE FAIL] --device cuda but CUDA unavailable.")
   330	        dev = torch.device(args.device)
   331	
   332	    out_dir = Path(args.out)
   333	    resume_in_place = (args.resume is not None
   334	                       and out_dir.resolve() == Path(args.resume).resolve().parent)
   335	    if (out_dir.exists() and any(out_dir.iterdir())
   336	            and not args.overwrite and not resume_in_place):
   337	        raise RuntimeError(f"[OUT FAIL] {out_dir} non-empty. Use --overwrite or "
   338	                           f"--resume to continue in place.")
   339	    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   340	    log_path = out_dir / "train.log"
   341	    metrics_path = out_dir / "metrics.jsonl"
   342	
   343	    def log(msg: str) -> None:
   344	        if not is_main:
   345	            return
   346	        print(msg, flush=True)
   347	        with open(log_path, "a") as f:
   348	            f.write(msg + "\n")
   349	
   350	    import subprocess
   351	    try:
   352	        git_sha = subprocess.check_output(["git", "rev-parse", "HEAD"], cwd=ROOT).decode().strip()
   353	    except Exception:
   354	        git_sha = "unknown"
   355	    log(f"=== Graph-CodeFlow [{args.model_variant}] rectified flow over post-RVQ z_q ===")
   356	    log(f"git_sha: {git_sha}  device: {dev}  world_size: {world_size}")
   357	    log(f"args: {vars(args)}")
   358	
   359	    # ---- Frozen tokenizer (for snapped-decode QA + projection) ----
   360	    tokenizer, ta = load_frozen_tokenizer(args.frozen_vqvae_ckpt, dev)
   361	    D = ta["d_model"]
   362	    if D != args.code_dim:
   363	        raise RuntimeError(f"[CFG FAIL] tokenizer code_dim {D} != --code_dim {args.code_dim}")
   364	    log(f"frozen tokenizer: code_dim={D} Q={ta['num_quantizers']} K={ta['num_codes']} "
   365	        f"max_coarse={ta['max_coarse']}")
   366	
   367	    # ---- Data (offline token cache) ----
   368	    ds_train = TokenCacheDataset(args.token_cache, "train")
   369	    try:
   370	        ds_val = TokenCacheDataset(args.token_cache, "val")
   371	    except FileNotFoundError:
   372	        ds_val = None
   373	    log(f"token cache: train={len(ds_train)}" + (f" val={len(ds_val)}" if ds_val else " (no val)"))
   374	    if len(ds_train) < args.batch_size and not (args.smoke or args.mem_profile):
   375	        raise RuntimeError(f"[DATA FAIL] train {len(ds_train)} < batch {args.batch_size}")
   376	    # Preflight (P2): graph_pscf's frame_seed capacity is max_T_lat; the cache's T_lat
   377	    # must not exceed it, else GraphPSCFFlowNet.forward raises mid-run. Catch it HERE
   378	    # (right after cache load, before empirical stats / first forward) by reading the
   379	    # first clip's z_q[T_lat,C,D].
   380	    if args.model_variant == "graph_pscf" and len(ds_train) > 0:
   381	        _cache_T_lat = int(ds_train[0]["z_q"].shape[0])
   382	        if _cache_T_lat > args.max_T_lat:
   383	            raise RuntimeError(
   384	                f"[CFG FAIL] token cache T_lat={_cache_T_lat} > --max_T_lat={args.max_T_lat}: "
   385	                f"graph_pscf frame_seed capacity too small. Re-export with smaller "
   386	                f"num_frames, or raise --max_T_lat to >= {_cache_T_lat}.")
   387	        log(f"preflight: cache T_lat={_cache_T_lat} <= max_T_lat={args.max_T_lat} OK")
   388	
   389	    # ---- Resume: rebuild the model from the CKPT's saved architecture args ----
   390	    # The model is constructed BEFORE the resume state_dict load below, so when
   391	    # resuming we must build the SAME architecture the ckpt was trained with (its
   392	    # model_variant + depths), not the CLI default — otherwise load_state_dict
   393	    # (strict=True) would mismatch. Old ckpts (pre-model_variant) default to
   394	    # level_a so they still load. Only architecture-defining args are overridden;
   395	    # train/schedule args stay from the CLI.
   396	    if args.resume is not None and Path(args.resume).exists():
   397	        _rargs = torch.load(args.resume, map_location="cpu",
   398	                            weights_only=False).get("args", {})
   399	        for _k, _default in (("model_variant", "level_a"), ("code_dim", None),
   400	                             ("n_heads", None), ("d_ff", None), ("n_layers", None),
   401	                             ("depth_double", 6), ("depth_single", 12),
   402	                             ("hidden_size", None), ("mlp_ratio", 4.0),
   403	                             ("dropout", None), ("max_T_lat", 75)):
   404	            _v = _rargs.get(_k, _default) if isinstance(_rargs, dict) \
   405	                else getattr(_rargs, _k, _default)
   406	            if _v is not None:
   407	                setattr(args, _k, _v)
   408	        log(f"resume: rebuilding model from ckpt args model_variant={args.model_variant} "
   409	            f"depth_double={args.depth_double} depth_single={args.depth_single}")
   410	
   411	    # Resolve dropout per variant (spec §5.4: graph_pscf=0.05, level_a=0.1). None =
   412	    # not set on the CLI; an explicit --dropout wins, and resume above may have restored
   413	    # the ckpt's dropout (non-None -> takes precedence). Resolving HERE — before
   414	    # vars(args) is saved into the ckpt — records the real dropout so resume rebuilds it.
   415	    if args.dropout is None:
   416	        args.dropout = 0.05 if args.model_variant == "graph_pscf" else 0.1
   417	
   418	    # ---- Model ----
   419	    # spec A3: graph_pscf pins H==D==512 for v1 (no "H!=D is fine" speculation).
   420	    if args.hidden_size != args.code_dim:
   421	        raise RuntimeError(
   422	            f"[CFG FAIL] spec A3 requires hidden_size==code_dim (H==D==512 for v1), "
   423	            f"got hidden_size={args.hidden_size} code_dim={args.code_dim}")
   424	    flow = GraphCodeFlow(
   425	        code_dim=args.code_dim, n_heads=args.n_heads, d_ff=args.d_ff,
   426	        n_layers=args.n_layers, d_text=768, text_token_dim=768, dropout=args.dropout,
   427	        model_variant=args.model_variant, depth_double=args.depth_double,
   428	        depth_single=args.depth_single, mlp_ratio=args.mlp_ratio,
   429	        max_T_lat=args.max_T_lat,
   430	    ).to(dev)
   431	    # Empirical z_q normalization (LOCKED): mean/std over valid train tokens.
   432	    # Startup acceleration: disk-cache the stats (keyed by cache identity from the
   433	    # manifest, so a re-export invalidates it) + rank-0 scan + broadcast under DDP.
   434	    _cache_path = None
   435	    _cache_key = None
   436	    try:
   437	        import hashlib
   438	        _tc = Path(args.token_cache)
   439	        _man_text = (_tc / "manifest.json").read_text()
   440	        _idx_bytes = (_tc / "train" / "index.jsonl").read_bytes()
   441	        # Strong invalidation: full-manifest md5 (any export-config change) + a full
   442	        # CONTENT md5 of train/index.jsonl (any clip-set/order/content change, even at
   443	        # identical byte-size) + n/D/max_clips. A re-export rewrites manifest/index ->
   444	        # key changes -> cache auto-invalidates. (md5 of ~24MB index is one-time
   445	        # rank-0-only ~tens of ms, negligible vs the scan it guards.)
   446	        _cache_key = {"manifest_md5": hashlib.md5(_man_text.encode()).hexdigest(),
   447	                      "index_md5": hashlib.md5(_idx_bytes).hexdigest(),
   448	                      "n": len(ds_train), "D": D,
   449	                      "max_clips": args.empirical_stats_max_clips}
   450	        _cache_path = str(_tc / "empirical_stats.pt")
   451	    except Exception:
   452	        _cache_path = None  # no manifest/index -> skip cache, fall back to full scan
   453	    e_mean, e_std, n_stat = compute_empirical_stats(
   454	        ds_train, D, dev, max_clips=args.empirical_stats_max_clips,
   455	        cache_path=_cache_path, cache_key=_cache_key, is_ddp=is_ddp, rank=rank)
   456	    flow.set_latent_stats(e_mean, e_std)
   457	    log(f"empirical z_q norm over {n_stat} valid tokens: "
   458	        f"mean|.|avg={e_mean.abs().mean().item():.4f} std.avg={e_std.mean().item():.4f}")
   459	    n_params = sum(pp.numel() for pp in flow.parameters() if pp.requires_grad)
   460	    log(f"GraphCodeFlow trainable params: {n_params:,}")
   461	
   462	    amp_enabled = (args.amp_dtype == "bf16") and dev.type == "cuda"
   463	    fwd_dtype = torch.float32  # conditioning/z_q dtype the model validates on the fp32 path
   464	    amp_ctx = ((lambda: torch.autocast(device_type="cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16))
   465	               if amp_enabled else contextlib.nullcontext)
   466	    log(f"AMP: amp_dtype={args.amp_dtype} (autocast around fp32 flow math)")
   467	
   468	    # ---- Mem profile (one fwd+bwd, report peak, exit; NOT a real launch) ----
   469	    if args.mem_profile:
   470	        bs = min(args.batch_size, len(ds_train))
   471	        dl = DataLoader(ds_train, batch_size=bs, shuffle=True,
   472	                        collate_fn=token_collate, num_workers=0, drop_last=True)
   473	        b = next(iter(dl))
   474	        b = {k: v.to(dev) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v for k, v in b.items()}
   475	        cond = build_cond(b, args.cond_drop_prob, training=True, dtype=fwd_dtype)
   476	        torch.cuda.reset_peak_memory_stats(dev)
   477	        with amp_ctx():
   478	            r = flow.flow_loss(b["z_q"].to(fwd_dtype), b["token_mask"], cond,
   479	                               validate_inputs=True)
   480	        loss = args.flow_loss_weight * r["flow_loss"]
   481	        loss.backward()
   482	        peak = torch.cuda.max_memory_allocated(dev) / 1e9
   483	        log(f"[MEM PROFILE] batch_size={bs} flow_loss={r['flow_loss'].item():.4f} "
   484	            f"peak_cuda_mem={peak:.2f} GB  (NO real run launched)")
   485	        if is_ddp:
   486	            dist.destroy_process_group()
   487	        return 0
   488	
   489	    train_sampler = (DistributedSampler(ds_train, shuffle=True, drop_last=True)
   490	                     if is_ddp else None)
   491	    nw = max(0, args.num_workers)
   492	    dl_train = DataLoader(
   493	        ds_train, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=(train_sampler is None),
   494	        sampler=train_sampler, collate_fn=token_collate, num_workers=nw,
   495	        drop_last=True, pin_memory=True, persistent_workers=(nw > 0),
   496	        prefetch_factor=(4 if nw > 0 else None))
   497	    dl_val = (DataLoader(ds_val, batch_size=args.batch_size, shuffle=False,
   498	                         collate_fn=token_collate, num_workers=max(1, nw // 2),
   499	                         drop_last=False, pin_memory=True)
   500	              if ds_val is not None else None)
   501	
   502	    if is_ddp:
   503	        flow = DDP(flow, device_ids=[local_rank], find_unused_parameters=False)
   504	    raw_flow = flow.module if is_ddp else flow
   505	
   506	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(flow.parameters(), lr=args.lr, weight_decay=args.weight_decay)
   507	    steps_per_epoch = max(1, len(dl_train))
   508	    total_steps = max(1, args.epochs * steps_per_epoch)
   509	
   510	    def lr_at(step: int) -> float:
   511	        """Linear warmup -> half-cosine decay to eta_min_ratio*lr (CodeFlow recipe)."""
   512	        if args.warmup_steps > 0 and step < args.warmup_steps:
   513	            return args.lr * (step + 1) / args.warmup_steps
   514	        if args.lr_scheduler == "none":
   515	            return args.lr
   516	        prog = (step - args.warmup_steps) / max(1, total_steps - args.warmup_steps)
   517	        prog = min(1.0, max(0.0, prog))
   518	        cos = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * prog))
   519	        return args.lr * (args.eta_min_ratio + (1.0 - args.eta_min_ratio) * cos)
   520	
   521	    n_iter, start_epoch, best_val = 0, 0, float("inf")
   522	    if args.resume is not None:
   523	        if not Path(args.resume).exists():
   524	            raise RuntimeError(f"[RESUME FAIL] {args.resume} missing.")
   525	        rc = torch.load(args.resume, map_location=dev, weights_only=False)
   526	        raw_flow.load_state_dict(rc["model_state_dict"], strict=True)
   527	        opt.load_state_dict(rc["optimizer_state_dict"])
   528	        for st in opt.state.values():
   529	            for k, v in st.items():
   530	                if torch.is_tensor(v):
   531	                    st[k] = v.to(dev)
   532	        start_epoch = int(rc["epoch"]) + 1
   533	        n_iter = int(rc["global_step"])
   534	        best_val = float(rc.get("best_val", float("inf")))
   535	        log(f"resumed: start_epoch={start_epoch} n_iter={n_iter} best_val={best_val:.4f}")
   536	        del rc
   537	
   538	    n_epochs = 2 if args.smoke else args.epochs
   539	    smoke_cap = args.smoke_iters if args.smoke else None
   540	
   541	    for epoch in range(start_epoch, n_epochs):
   542	        if is_ddp:
   543	            train_sampler.set_epoch(epoch)
   544	        flow.train()
   545	        t0 = time.time()
   546	        run_sum, run_cnt = 0.0, 0
   547	        for it, b in enumerate(dl_train):
   548	            if smoke_cap is not None and it >= smoke_cap:
   549	                break
   550	            b = {k: v.to(dev) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v for k, v in b.items()}
   551	            cond = build_cond(b, args.cond_drop_prob, training=True, dtype=fwd_dtype)
   552	            with amp_ctx():
   553	                r = flow(b["z_q"].to(fwd_dtype), b["token_mask"], cond,
   554	                         validate_inputs=(it == 0 and epoch == start_epoch))
   555	            loss = args.flow_loss_weight * r["flow_loss"]
   556	
   557	            if it == 0 and epoch == start_epoch:
   558	                B, T_lat, C, Dd = b["z_q"].shape
   559	                Qd = b["indices"].shape[-1]
   560	                log(f"  [gate ok] z_q=[{B},{T_lat},{C},{Dd}] indices Q={Qd} "
   561	                    f"flow_loss={r['flow_loss'].item():.4f}")
   562	            if not torch.isfinite(loss):
   563	                log(f"[GATE FAIL] loss non-finite at iter {n_iter}")
   564	                return 1
   565	            opt.zero_grad()
   566	            loss.backward()
   567	            grad_norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(flow.parameters(), args.grad_clip)
   568	            if not torch.isfinite(grad_norm):
   569	                log(f"[GATE FAIL] non-finite grad norm at iter {n_iter}")
   570	                return 1
   571	            cur_lr = lr_at(n_iter)
   572	            for pg in opt.param_groups:
   573	                pg["lr"] = cur_lr
   574	            opt.step()
   575	
   576	            run_sum += float(r["flow_loss"].detach()); run_cnt += 1
   577	            do_log = (n_iter % args.log_every == 0) or (it == 0 and epoch == start_epoch)
   578	            do_qa = (n_iter % args.qa_every == 0) or (args.smoke and it == 0)
   579	            if do_log or do_qa:
   580	                qa = None
   581	                if do_qa and is_main:
   582	                    qa = projection_qa(raw_flow, tokenizer, b,
   583	                                       build_cond(b, 0.0, training=False, dtype=fwd_dtype),
   584	                                       dev, decode=do_qa)
   585	                if do_log:
   586	                    log(f"[ep{epoch} it{it} n_iter={n_iter}] flow_loss={r['flow_loss'].item():.5f} "
   587	                        f"grad_norm={grad_norm.item():.3f} lr={cur_lr:.3e}"
   588	                        + (f" | proj_err={qa['projection_error']:.4f} "
   589	                           f"code_usage/q={qa['code_usage_per_q']}" if qa else ""))
   590	                    if qa and "decode_cont_vs_snap_maxabs" in qa:
   591	                        log(f"           [QA decode] cont_finite={qa['decode_cont_finite']} "
   592	                            f"snap_finite={qa['decode_snap_finite']} "
   593	                            f"cont_vs_snap_maxabs={qa['decode_cont_vs_snap_maxabs']:.4f}")
   594	                    if is_main:
   595	                        row = {"epoch": epoch, "iter": it, "n_iter": n_iter,
   596	                               "flow_loss": r["flow_loss"].item(),
   597	                               "grad_norm": grad_norm.item(), "lr": cur_lr}
   598	                        if qa:
   599	                            row.update(qa)
   600	                        with open(metrics_path, "a") as f:
   601	                            f.write(json.dumps(row) + "\n")
   602	            n_iter += 1
   603	
   604	        train_flow = run_sum / max(1, run_cnt)
   605	        log(f"=== epoch {epoch} done in {time.time() - t0:.1f}s | train_flow={train_flow:.5f} ===")
   606	
   607	        do_val = ((epoch + 1) % args.save_every == 0 or epoch == n_epochs - 1 or args.smoke)
   608	        if do_val and is_main and dl_val is not None:
   609	            raw_flow.eval()
   610	            vlosses, vproj = [], []
   611	            with torch.no_grad():
   612	                for vit, vb in enumerate(dl_val):
   613	                    if args.smoke and vit >= 2:
   614	                        break
   615	                    vb = {k: v.to(dev) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v for k, v in vb.items()}
   616	                    vcond = build_cond(vb, 0.0, training=False, dtype=fwd_dtype)
   617	                    with amp_ctx():
   618	                        vr = raw_flow.flow_loss(vb["z_q"].to(fwd_dtype), vb["token_mask"], vcond)
   619	                    vlosses.append(vr["flow_loss"].item())
   620	                    vproj.append(projection_qa(raw_flow, tokenizer, vb, vcond, dev,
   621	                                               decode=False)["projection_error"])
   622	            val_flow = float(np.mean(vlosses)) if vlosses else float("nan")
   623	            val_proj = float(np.mean(vproj)) if vproj else float("nan")
   624	            log(f"  [val] flow_loss={val_flow:.5f} projection_error={val_proj:.4f}")
   625	            if not args.smoke:
   626	                hist_best = min(best_val, val_flow)
   627	                ckpt = {"model_state_dict": raw_flow.state_dict(),
   628	                        "optimizer_state_dict": opt.state_dict(),
   629	                        "epoch": epoch, "global_step": n_iter, "args": vars(args),
   630	                        "val_flow": val_flow, "val_proj": val_proj, "best_val": hist_best,
   631	                        "git_sha": git_sha, "frozen_vqvae_ckpt": args.frozen_vqvae_ckpt,
   632	                        "latent_mean": raw_flow.latent_mean.cpu(),
   633	                        "latent_std": raw_flow.latent_std.cpu()}
   634	                torch.save(ckpt, out_dir / "last_model.pt")
   635	                if val_flow < best_val:
   636	                    best_val = val_flow
   637	                    torch.save(ckpt, out_dir / "best_model.pt")
   638	                    log(f"  [ckpt] new best val_flow={val_flow:.5f}")
   639	            raw_flow.train()
   640	        if is_ddp:
   641	            dist.barrier()
   642	
   643	    log("=== training loop complete ===")
   644	    if is_ddp:
   645	        dist.destroy_process_group()
   646	    return 0
   647	
   648	
   649	if __name__ == "__main__":
   650	    raise SystemExit(main())

     2	"""Train Graph-CodeFlow (level_a probe / graph_pscf formal backbone — rectified-flow over the FROZEN Graph-VQVAE
     3	post-RVQ z_q) — handoff/20260609_graph_codeflow_rvq_backbone_plan.md +
     4	handoff/20260609_0530_graph_codeflow_locked_recipe_and_state.md (LOCKED recipe).
     5	
     6	SEPARATE from train_graph_vqvae.py / train_graph_vae.py / train_denoiser.py. Reads
     7	the offline RVQ token cache (scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py), trains
     8	GraphCodeFlow (flow-only loss), and logs the continuous-vs-snapped projection QA
     9	(THE key gate). Mirrors train_graph_vqvae.py's DDP / bf16-autocast / resume /
    10	logging patterns so the operational behavior is familiar.
    11	
    12	LOCKED recipe defaults: batch 64, lr 1e-4, half_cosine, warmup 2000,
    13	eta_min_ratio 0.01, wd 0.01, grad_clip 1.0, cond_drop_prob 0.1,
    14	flow_loss_weight 1.0, terminal/clean 0.0, seed 42, empirical z_q norm,
    15	terminal ID CE OFF.
    16	
    17	Modes:
    18	  --smoke        : a few train iters + 1 val pass (single proc OK).
    19	  --mem_profile  : one fwd+bwd at --batch_size, report peak CUDA mem, exit (does
    20	                   NOT launch the real run).
    21	  (default)      : full training loop. DO NOT launch the real run from this task
    22	                   (the frozen tokenizer is still training to ep300).
    23	
    24	Usage (smoke, single GPU):
    25	  python scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py --token_cache /tmp/cf_tokens \

    54	def _ddp_setup():
    55	    world_size = int(os.environ.get("WORLD_SIZE", "1"))
    56	    if world_size <= 1:
    57	        return False, 0, 0, 1, True
    58	    rank = int(os.environ["RANK"])
    59	    local_rank = int(os.environ["LOCAL_RANK"])
    60	    torch.cuda.set_device(local_rank)
    61	    dist.init_process_group(backend="nccl", device_id=torch.device("cuda", local_rank))
    62	    return True, rank, local_rank, world_size, rank == 0

    65	def load_frozen_tokenizer(ckpt_path: str, dev: torch.device) -> GraphVQTokenizer:
    66	    """Rebuild + freeze the Graph-VQVAE tokenizer (for the snapped-decode QA +
    67	    empirical-norm decode path). eval() + requires_grad_(False)."""
    68	    ck = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    69	    ta = ck["args"]
    70	    model = GraphVQTokenizer(
    71	        d_model=ta["d_model"], n_heads=ta["n_heads"], d_ff=ta["d_ff"],
    72	        n_graph_layers=ta["n_graph_layers"],
    73	        n_enc_temporal_layers=ta["n_enc_temporal_layers"],
    74	        n_pre_vq_layers=ta["n_pre_vq_layers"], n_post_vq_layers=ta["n_post_vq_layers"],
    75	        n_cross_layers=ta["n_cross_layers"],
    76	        n_dec_temporal_layers=ta["n_dec_temporal_layers"],
    77	        max_coarse=ta["max_coarse"], temporal_stride=ta["temporal_stride"],
    78	        temporal_kernel=ta["temporal_kernel"], dropout=ta["dropout"],
    79	        code_dim=ta["code_dim"], num_codes=ta["num_codes"],
    80	        num_quantizers=ta["num_quantizers"], ema_mu=ta["ema_mu"],
    81	        quantize_dropout_prob=ta["quantize_dropout_prob"],
    82	        dead_code_threshold=ta["dead_code_threshold"],
    83	    ).to(dev)
    84	    model.load_state_dict(ck["model_state_dict"], strict=True)
    85	    model.eval()
    86	    model.requires_grad_(False)
    87	    return model, ta

    89	
    90	def build_cond(b: dict, cond_drop_prob: float, training: bool,
    91	               dtype: torch.dtype):
    92	    """Assemble the GraphStructuredCodeFlow conditioning dict from a token batch.
    93	
    94	    has_text starts from the dataset flag; during training we additionally CFG-
    95	    drop (flip True->False) with cond_drop_prob (so the model learns the uncond
    96	    branch). Float conditioning is cast to `dtype` (fp32 unless bf16 autocast
    97	    wraps the forward — the model enforces dtype-match on the fp32 path).
    98	    """
    99	    has_text = b["has_text"].clone()
   100	    if training and cond_drop_prob > 0.0:
   101	        drop = torch.rand(has_text.shape[0], device=has_text.device) < cond_drop_prob
   102	        has_text = has_text & ~drop
   103	    return {
   104	        "text_global": b["caption_emb"].to(dtype),
   105	        "text_tokens": b["caption_token_emb"].to(dtype),
   106	        "text_token_mask": b["caption_token_mask"],
   107	        "has_text": has_text,
   108	        "pooled_adjacency": b["pooled_adjacency"].to(dtype),
   109	        "pooled_geodesic": b["pooled_geodesic"].to(dtype),
   110	        "pooled_skeleton_embeddings": b["pooled_skeleton_embeddings"].to(dtype),
   111	        "coarse_mask": b["coarse_mask"],
   112	        "frame_mask_lat": b["frame_mask_lat"],
   113	    }
   114	
   115	
   116	def compute_empirical_stats(ds: TokenCacheDataset, D: int, dev: torch.device,
   117	                            max_clips: int = 0, cache_path: str | None = None,
   118	                            cache_key: dict | None = None,
   119	                            is_ddp: bool = False, rank: int = 0):
   120	    """Empirical z_q mean/std over VALID tokens of the train cache (LOCKED:
   121	    empirical normalization over the FULL train set, not codebook-stat). Streamed
   122	    sum / sumsq. max_clips<=0 (default) uses ALL train clips; a positive cap is for
   123	    smoke/debug only (it would normalize on a PREFIX, not the full set).
   124	
   125	    Startup acceleration (2026-06-09): (1) DISK CACHE — if cache_path exists and its
   126	    stored cache_key matches, load mean/std instantly (skips the full scan; survives
   127	    resume/restart/new experiments on the same cache). cache_key encodes the cache
   128	    identity (manifest_md5 + full-content index_md5 + n/D/max_clips) so any re-export
   129	    or clip-set/content change INVALIDATES it (a content hash, not a byte-size).
   130	    (2) rank-0-only under DDP — only rank 0 scans (or loads) + writes the cache, then
   131	    broadcasts mean/std to all ranks. Avoids every rank re-decompressing the full cache
   132	    (the un-optimized path made a 6-rank/full-length scan take ~30min; this makes a cold
   133	    scan ~6x faster and a warm/cached start instant)."""
   134	    n = len(ds) if max_clips <= 0 else min(len(ds), max_clips)
   135	    mean_t = std_t = None
   136	    count = 0
   137	    # Only rank 0 (or a single process) scans/loads + writes the cache.
   138	    if not is_ddp or rank == 0:
   139	        loaded = False
   140	        if cache_path and Path(cache_path).exists():
   141	            try:
   142	                c = torch.load(cache_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
   143	                if c.get("D") == D and c.get("cache_key") == cache_key:
   144	                    mean_t, std_t = c["mean"].float(), c["std"].float()
   145	                    count = int(c["count"])
   146	                    # Explicit raise (not assert: survives `python -O`, which would
   147	                    # strip an assert and silently feed a bad-shape mean/std). A bad
   148	                    # shape here is caught by the enclosing except -> loaded=False ->
   149	                    # full re-scan + cache rewrite (self-heals instead of using it).
   150	                    if tuple(mean_t.shape) != (D,) or tuple(std_t.shape) != (D,):
   151	                        raise RuntimeError(
   152	                            f"cached empirical stats shape mismatch: "
   153	                            f"mean{tuple(mean_t.shape)} std{tuple(std_t.shape)} != ({D},)")
   154	                    loaded = True
   155	            except Exception:
   156	                loaded = False
   157	        if not loaded:
   158	            count = 0
   159	            s = torch.zeros(D, dtype=torch.float64)
   160	            s2 = torch.zeros(D, dtype=torch.float64)
   161	            for i in range(n):
   162	                it = ds[i]
   163	                z = it["z_q"].reshape(-1, D).double()       # [T_lat*C, D]
   164	                m = it["token_mask"].reshape(-1)
   165	                zv = z[m]
   166	                count += zv.shape[0]
   167	                s += zv.sum(dim=0)
   168	                s2 += zv.pow(2).sum(dim=0)
   169	            if count == 0:
   170	                raise RuntimeError("compute_empirical_stats: zero valid tokens in cache")
   171	            mean_t = (s / count).float()
   172	            std_t = ((s2 / count) - (s / count).pow(2)).clamp_min(1e-12).sqrt().float()
   173	            if cache_path:
   174	                try:
   175	                    torch.save({"mean": mean_t, "std": std_t, "count": count,
   176	                                "D": D, "cache_key": cache_key}, cache_path)
   177	                except Exception:
   178	                    pass
   179	        mean_t, std_t = mean_t.to(dev), std_t.to(dev)
   180	    else:
   181	        mean_t = torch.zeros(D, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)
   182	        std_t = torch.ones(D, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)
   183	    # Broadcast rank-0's stats to all ranks (DDP); every rank ends with identical norm.
   184	    if is_ddp:
   185	        dist.broadcast(mean_t, src=0)
   186	        dist.broadcast(std_t, src=0)
   187	        cnt = torch.tensor([count], dtype=torch.long, device=dev)
   188	        dist.broadcast(cnt, src=0)
   189	        count = int(cnt.item())
   190	    return mean_t, std_t, count
   191	
   192	
   193	@torch.no_grad()
   194	def projection_qa(flow, tokenizer, b: dict, cond: dict, dev: torch.device,
   195	                  decode: bool = False):
   196	    """THE key gate: compare continuous decode(z_hat) vs snapped decode(z_snap)
   197	    and report projection_error = mse(z_hat, z_snap) over valid tokens.
   198	
   199	    Here z_hat is the model's predicted CLEAN latent from a single flow eval at a
   200	    fixed t (denormalized to raw RVQ space), NOT a full ODE sample (cheap, per-step
   201	    diagnostic). Returns projection_error, per-q generated-code usage, and (if
   202	    decode=True) the max abs decoded-motion gap continuous-vs-snapped.
   203	    """
   204	    z_q = b["z_q"].to(dev)
   205	    token_mask = b["token_mask"].to(dev)
   206	    B, T_lat, C, D = z_q.shape
   207	    # One flow eval at t~U: predict v -> clean (in normalized space) -> denorm.
   208	    x = flow.normalize(z_q) * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   209	    noise = torch.randn_like(x) * flow.noise_scale * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   210	    t = torch.rand(B, device=dev)
   211	    t_view = t[:, None, None, None]
   212	    z_t = (t_view * x + (1.0 - t_view) * noise) * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   213	    v = flow.predict_velocity(z_t, t, cond)
   214	    clean = flow.predict_clean_from_velocity(z_t, t, v)
   215	    z_hat = flow.denormalize(clean) * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   216	
   217	    proj = tokenizer.nearest_residual_ids(z_hat, token_mask)
   218	    indices_hat = proj["indices_hat"]
   219	    # per-q generated code usage (#unique codes used on valid tokens).
   220	    usage = []
   221	    for qi in range(indices_hat.shape[-1]):
   222	        ids_q = indices_hat[..., qi][token_mask]
   223	        usage.append(int(torch.unique(ids_q[ids_q >= 0]).numel()))
   224	    out = {"projection_error": float(proj["projection_error"].item()),
   225	           "code_usage_per_q": usage}
   226	    if decode:
   227	        skel_meta = {
   228	            "s_j": b["s_j"].to(dev), "assignment": b["assignment"].to(dev),
   229	            "coarse_mask": b["coarse_mask"].to(dev),
   230	            "frame_mask_lat": b["frame_mask_lat"].to(dev),
   231	            "pooled_adjacency": b["pooled_adjacency"].to(dev),
   232	            "pooled_geodesic": b["pooled_geodesic"].to(dev),
   233	        }
   234	        fake_batch = SimpleNamespace(joint_mask=b["joint_mask"].to(dev))
   235	        cont = tokenizer.decode(z_hat, skel_meta, fake_batch)["pred_motion"]
   236	        snap = tokenizer.decode_from_indices(indices_hat, skel_meta, fake_batch)["pred_motion"]
   237	        out["decode_cont_finite"] = bool(torch.isfinite(cont).all())
   238	        out["decode_snap_finite"] = bool(torch.isfinite(snap).all())
   239	        out["decode_cont_vs_snap_maxabs"] = float((cont - snap).abs().max().item())
   240	    return out
   241

   245	    # data / tokenizer

   247	    p.add_argument("--frozen_vqvae_ckpt", required=True)

   259	    p.add_argument("--depth_double", type=int, default=6,
   260	                   help="graph_pscf double-stream depth (spec §5.3)")

     2	"""Train the Graph-VQVAE (coarse-slot structured RVQ) tokenizer on AniMo4D

    52	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    53	def _ddp_setup():
    54	    world_size = int(os.environ.get("WORLD_SIZE", "1"))
    55	    if world_size <= 1:
    56	        return False, 0, 0, 1, True
    57	    rank = int(os.environ["RANK"])
    58	    local_rank = int(os.environ["LOCAL_RANK"])
    59	    torch.cuda.set_device(local_rank)
    60	    dist.init_process_group(
    61	        backend="nccl", device_id=torch.device("cuda", local_rank))
    62	    return True, rank, local_rank, world_size, rank == 0

    65	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    66	# Loss runner                                                                 #
    67	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    68	def run_vq_loss(out, batch, loss_weights, effective_frame_mask, code_dim,
    69	                allow_collectives=True):
    70	    gt_motion = batch.anytop_x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()  # [B,T,J,13]
    71	    return compute_vq_loss_13ch(
    72	        pred_motion=out["pred_motion"],
    73	        gt_motion=gt_motion,
    74	        foot_contact_per_joint=batch.foot_contact_per_joint,
    75	        anytop_mean=batch.anytop_mean,
    76	        anytop_std=batch.anytop_std,
    77	        parent_indices=batch.parent_indices,
    78	        rest_offsets=batch.rest_offsets,
    79	        joint_mask=batch.joint_mask,
    80	        frame_mask=effective_frame_mask,
    81	        code_dim=code_dim,
    82	        commit_sq_sum=out["commit_sq_sum"],
    83	        n_valid_tokens=out["n_valid_tokens"],
    84	        weights=loss_weights,
    85	        allow_collectives=allow_collectives,
    86	    )

    88	
    89	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    90	# M3 unit checks (the 4 amendment-critical invariants)                        #
    91	# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
    92	def run_unit_checks(dev) -> int:
    93	    """(a) padded slots -> ~0 decoder attention weight (F1)
    94	       (b) STE mask applied post-detach (1c)
    95	       (c) commit loss applied exactly once (F4)
    96	       (d) EMA denominators exclude padded tokens (1b)
    97	    Returns 0 on all-pass, 1 on any failure."""
    98	    import torch.nn.functional as F
    99	    from src.models.vq_model.masked_motion_decoder import MaskedSlotToJointCrossAttention
   100	    from src.models.vq_model.quantizer import MaskedResidualVQ, _EMACodebook
   101	    from src.models.vq_model.losses import _DEFAULT_VQ_WEIGHTS
   102	
   103	    torch.manual_seed(0)
   104	    ok = True
   105	
   106	    # ---- (a) F1: padded slot keys get EXACTLY zero attention weight ----
   107	    # Probe the cross-attn directly: build assignment that (via clamp(1e-8).log())
   108	    # would otherwise leak onto padded slots, set a padded slot, assert 0 weight.
   109	    D, H, B, J, K = 32, 4, 2, 5, 6
   110	    attn_mod = MaskedSlotToJointCrossAttention(D, H, dropout=0.0).to(dev).eval()
   111	    jq = torch.randn(B, J, D, device=dev)
   112	    sf = torch.randn(B, K, D, device=dev)
   113	    assign = torch.zeros(B, J, K, device=dev)
   114	    # give every joint mass on slot 0 (valid); leave others 0 (-> log clamp leak)
   115	    assign[:, :, 0] = 1.0
   116	    coarse_mask = torch.ones(B, K, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   117	    coarse_mask[:, -1] = False   # last slot padded
   118	    coarse_mask[1, -2] = False   # sample 1 has two padded slots
   119	
   120	    # Monkey-inspect the softmax weights by reconstructing them (the module does
   121	    # not return attn; replicate its score path to read the weights).
   122	    import math
   123	    with torch.no_grad():
   124	        q = attn_mod.q_proj(attn_mod.norm_q(jq)).view(B, J, H, D // H).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
   125	        kv = attn_mod.norm_kv(sf)
   126	        k = attn_mod.k_proj(kv).view(B, K, H, D // H).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
   127	        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(D // H)
   128	        ab = assign.unsqueeze(1).clamp(min=1e-8).log()
   129	        scores = scores + attn_mod.assign_bias_scale * ab
   130	        key_mask = coarse_mask.bool().unsqueeze(1).unsqueeze(2)
   131	        scores = scores.float().masked_fill(~key_mask, float("-inf"))
   132	        w = F.softmax(scores, dim=-1).nan_to_num(0.0)   # [B,H,J,K]
   133	    # weight on padded slot keys must be EXACTLY 0
   134	    padded_w = w[:, :, :, ~coarse_mask.any(dim=0)]  # cols padded in ALL samples (slot -1)
   135	    leak_global = padded_w.abs().max().item()
   136	    # per-sample padded check (sample 1 slot -2)
   137	    leak_s1 = w[1, :, :, -2].abs().max().item()
   138	    if leak_global > 0.0 or leak_s1 > 0.0:
   139	        print(f"[UNIT a FAIL] F1 padded-slot attention weight != 0 "
   140	              f"(global={leak_global:.3e} s1={leak_s1:.3e})")
   141	        ok = False
   142	    else:
   143	        print(f"[UNIT a OK] F1: padded-slot-key attention weight == 0 "
   144	              f"(global={leak_global:.1e}, s1_extra_pad={leak_s1:.1e})")
   145	
   146	    # ---- (b) 1c: STE mask applied post-detach (padded -> 0 value AND 0 grad) ----
   147	    vq = MaskedResidualVQ(code_dim=D, num_codes=16, num_quantizers=2,
   148	                          quantize_dropout_prob=0.0).to(dev).eval()
   149	    Tl, C = 3, 4
   150	    x = torch.randn(B, Tl, C, D, device=dev, requires_grad=True)
   151	    tok_mask = torch.ones(B, Tl, C, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   152	    tok_mask[:, :, -1] = False  # last slot padded everywhere
   153	    # allow_collectives=False: unit checks may run rank-0-only under torchrun
   154	    # (process group initialized), so the fresh VQ modules here must NOT call
   155	    # cross-rank collectives the other ranks are not making.
   156	    o = vq(x, tok_mask, allow_collectives=False)
   157	    zq = o["quantized"]
   158	    # value: padded slot quantized output must be exactly 0
   159	    pad_val = zq[:, :, -1, :].abs().max().item()
   160	    # gradient: a loss on zq must give EXACTLY zero grad to padded x entries
   161	    zq.sum().backward()
   162	    pad_grad = x.grad[:, :, -1, :].abs().max().item()
   163	    valid_grad = x.grad[:, :, 0, :].abs().max().item()
   164	    if pad_val > 0.0 or pad_grad > 0.0:
   165	        print(f"[UNIT b FAIL] STE padded value={pad_val:.3e} grad={pad_grad:.3e} "
   166	              f"(both must be 0)")
   167	        ok = False
   168	    else:
   169	        print(f"[UNIT b OK] 1c STE-post-detach: padded value=0 grad=0; "
   170	              f"valid grad nonzero={valid_grad:.3e}")
   171	
   172	    # ---- (c) F4: commit weight applied EXACTLY once ----
   173	    # commit term in total == w_commit * raw_commit (not squared, not double).
   174	    w_commit = _DEFAULT_VQ_WEIGHTS["commit"]
   175	    vq2 = MaskedResidualVQ(code_dim=D, num_codes=16, num_quantizers=2,
   176	                           quantize_dropout_prob=0.0).to(dev).eval()
   177	    x2 = torch.randn(B, Tl, C, D, device=dev)
   178	    m2 = torch.ones(B, Tl, C, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   179	    m2[:, :, -1] = False
   180	    o2 = vq2(x2, m2, allow_collectives=False)
   181	    raw_commit = o2["commit_loss"].item()
   182	    # rebuild commit exactly as the loss wrapper does (single-rank => no allreduce)
   183	    n_valid = o2["n_valid_tokens"].item()
   184	    wrapper_commit = (o2["commit_sq_sum"].item() * 1.0) / (max(n_valid, 1.0) * D)
   185	    contrib = w_commit * wrapper_commit
   186	    double = w_commit * w_commit * wrapper_commit  # the WRONG (squared) value
   187	    if abs(wrapper_commit - raw_commit) > 1e-5:
   188	        print(f"[UNIT c FAIL] wrapper commit {wrapper_commit:.6e} != "
   189	              f"quantizer raw commit {raw_commit:.6e}")
   190	        ok = False
   191	    elif abs(contrib - double) < 1e-12:
   192	        print(f"[UNIT c FAIL] cannot distinguish single vs squared (w too close to 1)")
   193	        ok = False
   194	    else:
   195	        print(f"[UNIT c OK] F4 commit applied ONCE: raw={raw_commit:.4e} "
   196	              f"contrib={contrib:.4e} (w={w_commit}); squared-would-be={double:.4e}")
   197	
   198	    # ---- (d) 1b: EMA denominators exclude padded tokens ----
   199	    # Feed a codebook a batch where MANY tokens are padded zero-vectors; the EMA
   200	    # cluster_size SUM must reflect the #VALID tokens, never the total. With a
   201	    # fresh codebook (cluster_size starts at 0), one EMA update gives
   202	    #   cluster_size = mu*0 + (1-mu)*batch_count  ->  sum = (1-mu)*n_valid.
   203	    # So sum/(1-mu) must equal n_valid (37), independent of the 63 padded tokens.
   204	    mu_d = 0.01
   205	    cb = _EMACodebook(num_codes=8, code_dim=D, ema_mu=mu_d).to(dev)
   206	    Ntot = 100
   207	    xx = torch.randn(Ntot, D, device=dev)
   208	    valid = torch.zeros(Ntot, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   209	    valid[:37] = True  # exactly 37 valid, 63 padded
   210	    xx[~valid] = 0.0    # padded are zero-vectors (the leak risk)
   211	    codes, _ = cb.quantize(xx)
   212	    cb.ema_update(x_fp32=xx, codes=codes, valid=valid, allow_collectives=False)
   213	    recovered = cb.cluster_size.sum().item() / (1.0 - mu_d)  # undo EMA decay
   214	    if abs(recovered - 37.0) > 1e-3:
   215	        print(f"[UNIT d FAIL] EMA counted {recovered:.4f} tokens != 37 valid "
   216	              f"(padded tokens leaked into the denominator)")
   217	        ok = False
   218	    else:
   219	        print(f"[UNIT d OK] 1b EMA excludes padded: counted {recovered:.2f} "
   220	              f"== {int(valid.sum())} valid (of {Ntot} total, 63 padded zero-vecs)")

exec
/usr/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py | sed -n '220,520p'" in /iridisfs/scratch/ts1v23/workspace/noKslot_clean
exec
/usr/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py | sed -n '520,859p'" in /iridisfs/scratch/ts1v23/workspace/noKslot_clean
 succeeded in 0ms:
   220	              f"== {int(valid.sum())} valid (of {Ntot} total, 63 padded zero-vecs)")
   221	
   222	    # ---- (e) decoder padded-FRAME isolation. Two probes:
   223	    #   (e1) padded-frame OUTPUT is exactly 0, and perturbing padded-frame INPUT
   224	    #        by 100x does not change valid-frame output (input-dependent leak).
   225	    #   (e2) the INTRA-temporal-block leak (codex 3rd-pass): conv biases make
   226	    #        padded positions nonzero independent of input, and conv2 could spread
   227	    #        them into valid frames. Catch it by comparing valid-frame output of a
   228	    #        clip WITH trailing padded frames vs the all-valid PREFIX clip — they
   229	    #        must match. We force LARGE conv biases so the bug, if present, leaks
   230	    #        loudly (a small default bias could hide under the 1e-4 tol).
   231	    from src.models.vq_model.masked_motion_decoder import MaskedMotionDecoder
   232	    dec = MaskedMotionDecoder(d_model=D, n_heads=H, n_cross_layers=2,
   233	                              n_temporal_layers=2, temporal_kernel=9,
   234	                              dropout=0.0).to(dev).eval()
   235	    with torch.no_grad():
   236	        for tl in dec.temporal_layers:
   237	            tl.conv1.bias.fill_(5.0)   # large biases -> loud leak if unmasked
   238	            tl.conv2.bias.fill_(5.0)
   239	    Td = 8
   240	    skel = torch.randn(B, J, D, device=dev)
   241	    assign_d = torch.zeros(B, J, K, device=dev)
   242	    assign_d[:, :, 0] = 1.0
   243	    cmask = torch.ones(B, K, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   244	    cmask[:, -1] = False
   245	    jmask = torch.ones(B, J, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   246	    fmask = torch.ones(B, Td, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   247	    fmask[:, -2:] = False  # last 2 frames padded
   248	    base_slots = torch.randn(B, Td, K, D, device=dev)
   249	    # all-valid PREFIX clip (Td-2 frames, no padding) — same valid content.
   250	    fmask_pre = torch.ones(B, Td - 2, dtype=torch.bool, device=dev)
   251	    slots_pre = base_slots[:, : Td - 2, :, :].contiguous()
   252	    with torch.no_grad():
   253	        out1 = dec(base_slots, skel, assign_d, cmask, jmask, fmask)
   254	        perturbed = base_slots.clone()
   255	        perturbed[:, -2:, :, :] += 100.0 * torch.randn(B, 2, K, D, device=dev)
   256	        out2 = dec(perturbed, skel, assign_d, cmask, jmask, fmask)
   257	        out_pre = dec(slots_pre, skel, assign_d, cmask, jmask, fmask_pre)
   258	    pad_frame_out = out1[:, -2:, :, :].abs().max().item()
   259	    valid_leak_input = (out1[:, :-2, :, :] - out2[:, :-2, :, :]).abs().max().item()
   260	    valid_leak_bias = (out1[:, :-2, :, :] - out_pre).abs().max().item()
   261	    if pad_frame_out > 0.0 or valid_leak_input > 1e-4 or valid_leak_bias > 1e-4:
   262	        print(f"[UNIT e FAIL] decoder padded-frame out={pad_frame_out:.3e} "
   263	              f"input-leak={valid_leak_input:.3e} bias-leak={valid_leak_bias:.3e} "
   264	              f"(all must be ~0)")
   265	        ok = False
   266	    else:
   267	        print(f"[UNIT e OK] decoder padded-frame isolation: padded out=0, "
   268	              f"input-leak={valid_leak_input:.1e}, intra-block bias-leak="
   269	              f"{valid_leak_bias:.1e} (valid output identical with/without trailing "
   270	              f"padded frames, even with conv bias=5.0)")
   271	
   272	    print(f"\n[UNIT CHECKS] {'ALL PASS' if ok else 'FAILURE'}")
   273	    return 0 if ok else 1

   499	    # so the codebook buffers (embed/cluster_size/embed_avg/initted) become identical
   500	    # across ranks here; the broadcast just below then operates on already-identical

   502	    # invariant that the per-step EMA all_reduce relies on, whether or not resuming.

   512	        # `model` is still the RAW module here (DDP wrap is below).
