   514	        start_epoch = int(resume_ckpt["epoch"]) + 1
   515	        log(f"  resumed model weights (strict=True); start_epoch={start_epoch} "
   516	            f"(ckpt epoch={resume_ckpt['epoch']}, global_step={resume_ckpt['global_step']}, "
   517	            f"val_total={resume_ckpt.get('val_total')})")
   518	
   519	    if is_ddp:
   520	        # ONE-TIME codebook buffer sync: each rank built its _EMACodebook with an

 succeeded in 0ms:
   520	        # ONE-TIME codebook buffer sync: each rank built its _EMACodebook with an
   521	        # independent torch.randn init, so the codebooks (embed / cluster_size /
   522	        # embed_avg buffers) DIFFER across ranks at construction. The EMA update's
   523	        # all_reduce keeps codebooks identical ACROSS STEPS only if they START
   524	        # identical, so we broadcast rank-0's codebook buffers to all ranks once,
   525	        # here, before training. After this they stay bit-identical (EMA stats are
   526	        # all_reduced; dead-code replacements are broadcast from rank 0).
   527	        # (model is still the raw module here — DDP wrap happens just below.)
   528	        for cb in model.quantizer.codebooks:
   529	            dist.broadcast(cb.embed, src=0)
   530	            dist.broadcast(cb.cluster_size, src=0)
   531	            dist.broadcast(cb.embed_avg, src=0)
   532	            dist.broadcast(cb.initted, src=0)
   533	        # EMA codebook buffers are updated in-place inside forward; DDP must NOT
   534	        # re-broadcast buffers each iter (it would clobber the per-step EMA state).
   535	        # We keep codebooks synced explicitly (one-time broadcast above + the EMA
   536	        # all_reduce each step), so broadcast_buffers=False. find_unused_parameters=
   537	        # True: under quantizer dropout dropped stages' codebooks are buffers (no
   538	        # params) and some graph-attn bias params on edge-free pooled graphs may go
   539	        # unused — keep it True defensively.
   540	        model = DDP(model, device_ids=[local_rank],
   541	                    find_unused_parameters=True, broadcast_buffers=False)
   542	    raw_model = model.module if is_ddp else model
   543	
   544	    loss_weights = {
   545	        "pos": args.w_pos, "rot": args.w_rot, "vel": args.w_vel,
   546	        "contact": args.w_contact, "world": args.w_world, "fk": args.w_fk,
   547	        "traj": args.w_traj, "commit": args.w_commit,
   548	    }
   549	    log(f"loss_weights: {loss_weights}")
   550	    expected_C = args.max_coarse
   551	
   552	    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=args.lr)
   553	
   554	    # --resume (part 2): restore optimizer state + step/epoch/best-val bookkeeping
   555	    # (model weights + start_epoch were restored above). The optimizer is built on
   556	    # the same model.parameters() order as the original run (both built AFTER DDP
   557	    # wrap), so param identity matches. load_state_dict may leave the exp_avg /
   558	    # exp_avg_sq tensors on CPU — move them onto `dev` (AdamW would otherwise error
   559	    # mixing CPU state with CUDA params). n_iter continues from the saved global_step
   560	    # so n_iter % log_every / qa_every phase is preserved; best_val carried forward so
   561	    # the first post-resume val does not clobber a better earlier best_model.pt.
   562	    n_iter = 0
   563	    best_val = float("inf")
   564	    # Local optimizer-step counter for THIS process only — starts at 0 every launch
   565	    # and is DELIBERATELY NOT restored from the ckpt (unlike n_iter). The LR warmup
   566	    # below is keyed to this, so on --resume the warmup re-ramps from 0 at this
   567	    # launch (re-warm after a global-batch change), independent of the large resumed
   568	    # n_iter. warmup_steps=0 leaves this unused (no lr writes, flat-lr preserved).
   569	    steps_this_run = 0
   570	    if resume_ckpt is not None:
   571	        opt.load_state_dict(resume_ckpt["optimizer_state_dict"])
   572	        for state in opt.state.values():
   573	            for k, v in state.items():
   574	                if torch.is_tensor(v):
   575	                    state[k] = v.to(dev)
   576	        n_iter = int(resume_ckpt["global_step"])
   577	        # best_val = the HISTORICAL best, NOT the resumed ckpt's own current val_total
   578	        # (which is just the checkpointed epoch's value — resuming from last_model.pt /
   579	        # epN_model.pt of a worse epoch would otherwise overwrite a better earlier
   580	        # best_model.pt on the first post-resume val). Prefer the persisted
   581	        # best_val_total; for a legacy ckpt without it, fall back to the sibling
   582	        # best_model.pt's val_total (that IS the historical best); else inf
   583	        # (codex 019ea63f #1, mirrors train_graph_vae.py best_val_loss logic).
   584	        if "best_val_total" in resume_ckpt:
   585	            best_val = float(resume_ckpt["best_val_total"])
   586	        else:
   587	            _bm = Path(args.resume).parent / "best_model.pt"
   588	            best_val = (float(torch.load(_bm, map_location="cpu",
   589	                                         weights_only=False)["val_total"])
   590	                        if _bm.exists() else float("inf"))
   591	            log("  [resume] legacy ckpt (no best_val_total) → best_val from "
   592	                "sibling best_model.pt")
   593	        log(f"  resumed optimizer state ({len(opt.state)} param groups on {dev}); "
   594	            f"n_iter={n_iter} best_val={best_val:.4f}")
   595	        del resume_ckpt
   596	
   597	    amp_enabled = (args.amp_dtype == "bf16")
   598	    amp_ctx = ((lambda: torch.autocast(device_type="cuda", dtype=torch.bfloat16))
   599	               if amp_enabled else contextlib.nullcontext)
   600	    log(f"AMP: amp_dtype={args.amp_dtype}")
   601	
   602	    smoke_iter_cap = args.smoke_iters if args.smoke else None
   603	    # Smoke caps the epoch count too (2 epochs is enough to exercise
   604	    # train -> rank-0 val -> DDP barrier -> next-epoch reshuffle without churning
   605	    # the full 300-epoch loop).
   606	    n_epochs = 2 if args.smoke else args.epochs
   607	    # n_iter / best_val were initialized (and possibly restored from --resume) above;
   608	    # do NOT reset them here. start_epoch is 0 for a fresh run, ckpt.epoch+1 on resume.
   609	
   610	    for epoch in range(start_epoch, n_epochs):
   611	        if is_ddp:
   612	            train_sampler.set_epoch(epoch)
   613	        model.train()
   614	        t0 = time.time()
   615	        # GPU-side detached running sums (per loss key) + step counter for the EXACT
   616	        # epoch-mean (one .item() per key at epoch end), replacing per-step .item().
   617	        loss_running_sum: dict = {}
   618	        loss_step_count = 0
   619	        for it, raw in enumerate(dl_train):
   620	            if smoke_iter_cap is not None and it >= smoke_iter_cap:
   621	                break
   622	            batch = GraphMotionBatch.from_collate_dict({
   623	                k: v.to(dev) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v for k, v in raw.items()
   624	            })
   625	            with amp_ctx():
   626	                out = model(batch)
   627	
   628	            if it == 0:
   629	                B, T_lat, C, D = out["z_q"].shape
   630	                assert C == expected_C, f"[GATE FAIL] z C={C} != {expected_C}"
   631	                assert D == args.d_model, f"[GATE FAIL] z D={D} != {args.d_model}"
   632	                Q = out["indices"].shape[-1]
   633	                assert Q == args.num_quantizers, f"[GATE FAIL] Q={Q}"
   634	                _allowed = (torch.float32, torch.bfloat16) if amp_enabled else (torch.float32,)
   635	                assert out["z_q"].dtype in _allowed, f"[DTYPE FAIL] z_q={out['z_q'].dtype}"
   636	                log(f"  [gate ok] z_q=[{B},{T_lat},{C},{D}] dtype={out['z_q'].dtype} "
   637	                    f"indices=[{B},{T_lat},{C},{Q}]")
   638	
   639	            effective_frame_mask = out["frame_mask_recovered"]
   640	            losses = run_vq_loss(out, batch, loss_weights,
   641	                                 effective_frame_mask, args.code_dim)
   642	
   643	            # ---- EVERY-step non-finite LOSS gate (1 sync, was ~8) ----
   644	            # `total` is the weighted SUM of all loss terms, so any NaN/Inf in any
   645	            # component propagates into it (NaN through addition; Inf, and the Inf-Inf
   646	            # -> NaN edge, are all caught). Checking total finiteness every step thus
   647	            # detects a non-finite loss every step with ONE sync. The full per-key
   648	            # breakdown (which term blew up) is diagnostic-only and is checked on
   649	            # log-steps below — never weakening the every-step abort.
   650	            if not torch.isfinite(losses["total"]):
   651	                log(f"[GATE FAIL] loss[total]={losses['total'].item()} non-finite "
   652	                    f"at iter {n_iter}")
   653	                return 1
   654	            opt.zero_grad()
   655	            losses["total"].backward()
   656	            # ---- EVERY-step non-finite GRAD gate (1 sync, was hundreds) ----
   657	            # clip_grad_norm_ (max_norm=10.0, unchanged behavior) ALREADY computes the
   658	            # total grad L2 norm over all params and clips in place; we reuse its
   659	            # returned norm for the finiteness check instead of a per-parameter
   660	            # isfinite/abs-max Python loop (which forced hundreds of GPU syncs/step and
   661	            # was redundant with this same reduction). NO error_if_nonfinite=True: we
   662	            # want a GRACEFUL gate-fail exit (return 1) BEFORE opt.step(), matching the
   663	            # original behavior, not an exception. Health metric changed from
   664	            # per-element grad_max -> total L2 grad_norm (both standard diagnostics);
   665	            # its .item() is GATED to log-steps below.
   666	            grad_norm = torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(
   667	                model.parameters(), max_norm=10.0)
   668	            if not torch.isfinite(grad_norm):
   669	                log(f"[GATE FAIL] non-finite grad norm at iter {n_iter}")
   670	                return 1
   671	            # ---- linear LR warmup (OFF by default; flat-lr otherwise untouched) ----
   672	            # Keyed to steps_this_run (steps since THIS launch), NOT n_iter, so on
   673	            # --resume the ramp restarts from 0 here (re-warm after a batch change).
   674	            # Ramp lr 0 -> args.lr over the first warmup_steps steps, then hold at
   675	            # args.lr; (steps_this_run+1)/warmup_steps reaches 1.0 on the warmup_steps-th
   676	            # step and min(1.0, .) holds thereafter. Applied to every param group right
   677	            # before opt.step(); does NOT touch the optimizer construction, grad clip,
   678	            # or the NaN/finite gates above. warmup_steps==0 -> no lr writes at all
   679	            # (behavior byte-identical to no warmup).
   680	            if args.warmup_steps > 0:
   681	                cur_lr = args.lr * min(1.0, (steps_this_run + 1) / args.warmup_steps)
   682	                for pg in opt.param_groups:
   683	                    pg["lr"] = cur_lr
   684	            steps_this_run += 1
   685	            opt.step()
   686	
   687	            # Defer the per-key loss .item() syncs (~8/step): accumulate detached
   688	            # GPU-side running sums + a step counter, and materialize the EXACT epoch
   689	            # mean with a single .item() per key at epoch end (below). On log-steps the
   690	            # current-step losses are .item()'d for the console/metrics line (gated,
   691	            # cheap). No approximation: epoch mean = sum / count over the steps run.
   692	            for k, v in losses.items():
   693	                loss_running_sum[k] = loss_running_sum.get(k, 0) + v.detach()
   694	            loss_step_count += 1
   695	
   696	            # ---- logging / QA frequency gates (300ep L5 is >1.3M steps) ----
   697	            # do_log gates the per-iter console/file block + the metrics.jsonl row.
   698	            # do_qa gates the expensive full-world-recovery root QA. The finite/grad
   699	            # safety gates above are NOT gated (they fire EVERY step). Always log the
   700	            # very first observed step so the gate-check line is emitted (fresh run:
   701	            # n_iter==0; resume: first iter of the resumed start_epoch).
   702	            do_log = (n_iter % args.log_every == 0) or (n_iter == 0) \
   703	                or (it == 0 and epoch == start_epoch)
   704	            do_qa = (n_iter % args.qa_every == 0)
   705	
   706	            qa = None
   707	            if do_qa:
   708	                with torch.no_grad():
   709	                    qa = root_drift_jitter_qa(
   710	                        out["pred_motion"],
   711	                        batch.anytop_x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous(),
   712	                        batch.anytop_mean, batch.anytop_std, effective_frame_mask,
   713	                    )
   714	
   715	            if do_log:
   716	                # GATED .item() of the current-step per-key losses (cheap: log-steps
   717	                # only). These per-key values ARE the full per-key health view on
   718	                # log-steps (the every-step total-finiteness gate above already aborts
   719	                # on any non-finite component before opt.step, so by here all keys are
   720	                # finite). grad_norm.item() is likewise gated here (the only place its
   721	                # value is materialized to host).
   722	                cur_losses = {k: v.item() for k, v in losses.items()}
   723	                grad_norm_v = grad_norm.item()
   724	                # current live LR read straight off the optimizer for the log row:
   725	                # the warmup-ramped value while --warmup_steps>0 is ramping, else the
   726	                # held flat value (the constructed args.lr on a fresh run, or the lr
   727	                # carried in by opt.load_state_dict on --resume — equal under the
   728	                # fixed-lr same-experiment resume contract).
   729	                cur_lr_log = opt.param_groups[0]["lr"]
   730	                # codebook health (ppl/active/dead/n_valid_tok) — these GPU->CPU reads
   731	                # are GATED to log-steps (consumed only by the console log + the
   732	                # metrics.jsonl row just below). On non-log steps they are not computed,
   733	                # avoiding a per-step sync. NOTE: the commit-loss normalization uses the
   734	                # TENSOR out["n_valid_tokens"] (passed into run_vq_loss above), not this
   735	                # scalar, and the epoch-end ckpt reads out["active_codes"] fresh from the
   736	                # tensor — both independent of these gated host scalars.
   737	                ppl = out["perplexity"].tolist()
   738	                active = out["active_codes"].tolist()
   739	                dead = out["dead_codes"].tolist()
   740	                n_valid_tok = out["n_valid_tokens"].item()
   741	                # mean / p95 valid coarse slot count (only needed for the log row)
   742	                cm = out["coarse_mask"]
   743	                ccount = cm.sum(dim=-1).float()
   744	                valid_c_mean = ccount.mean().item()
   745	                valid_c_p95 = (torch.quantile(ccount, 0.95).item()
   746	                               if ccount.numel() else 0.0)
   747	
   748	                log(f"[ep{epoch} it{it} n_iter={n_iter}] total={cur_losses['total']:.4f} "
   749	                    f"pos={cur_losses['pos']:.4f} rot={cur_losses['rot']:.4f} "
   750	                    f"vel={cur_losses['vel']:.4f} world={cur_losses['world']:.4f} "
   751	                    f"fk={cur_losses['fk']:.4f} traj={cur_losses['traj']:.4f} "
   752	                    f"commit={cur_losses['commit']:.5f} grad_norm={grad_norm_v:.3f} "
   753	                    f"lr={cur_lr_log:.3e}")
   754	                log(f"           ppl={[f'{x:.1f}' for x in ppl]} "
   755	                    f"active={active} dead={dead} n_valid_tok={int(n_valid_tok)} "
   756	                    f"validC_mean={valid_c_mean:.1f} validC_p95={valid_c_p95:.1f}")
   757	                if qa is not None:
   758	                    log(f"           [ROOT-QA] drift_mean={qa['root_drift_mean']:.4f} "
   759	                        f"drift_max={qa['root_drift_max']:.4f} "
   760	                        f"jitter_pred={qa['root_jitter_pred']:.4f} "
   761	                        f"jitter_gt={qa['root_jitter_gt']:.4f} "
   762	                        f"jitter_ratio={qa['root_jitter_ratio']:.2f}")
   763	                if is_main:
   764	                    row = {
   765	                        "epoch": epoch, "iter": it, "n_iter": n_iter,
   766	                        "loss": cur_losses,
   767	                        "perplexity": ppl, "active_codes": active, "dead_codes": dead,
   768	                        "n_valid_tokens": n_valid_tok,
   769	                        "valid_c_mean": valid_c_mean, "valid_c_p95": valid_c_p95,
   770	                        "grad_norm": grad_norm_v, "lr": cur_lr_log,
   771	                    }
   772	                    if qa is not None:
   773	                        row.update({f"rootqa_{k}": v for k, v in qa.items()})
   774	                    with open(metrics_path, "a") as f:
   775	                        f.write(json.dumps(row) + "\n")
   776	            n_iter += 1
   777	
   778	        epoch_dt = time.time() - t0
   779	        # EXACT epoch-mean total via the deferred GPU-side running sum (one .item()).
   780	        train_total = ((loss_running_sum["total"] / loss_step_count).item()
   781	                       if loss_step_count > 0 else float("nan"))
   782	        log(f"=== epoch {epoch} done in {epoch_dt:.1f}s | train_total={train_total:.4f} ===")
   783	
   784	        # Periodic PRESERVED snapshot: every periodic_save_every epochs AND at the
   785	        # final epoch (mirrors train_graph_vae.py). We fold do_periodic into do_val so
   786	        # the snapshot carries a FRESH val_total and is byte-identical to last_model.pt
   787	        # ("same contents as last_model.pt"), rather than reusing a stale best_val.
   788	        do_periodic = (args.periodic_save_every > 0 and not args.smoke
   789	                       and ((epoch + 1) % args.periodic_save_every == 0
   790	                            or epoch == n_epochs - 1))
   791	        do_val = ((epoch + 1) % args.save_every == 0 or epoch == n_epochs - 1
   792	                  or do_periodic or args.smoke)
   793	        if do_val and is_main:
   794	            model.eval()
   795	            val_losses = defaultdict(list)
   796	            with torch.no_grad():
   797	                for vit, raw in enumerate(dl_val):
   798	                    if args.smoke and vit >= 2:
   799	                        break
   800	                    vb = GraphMotionBatch.from_collate_dict({
   801	                        k: v.to(dev) if torch.is_tensor(v) else v for k, v in raw.items()
   802	                    })
   803	                    with amp_ctx():
   804	                        # rank-0-only val: NO cross-rank collectives (the other
   805	                        # ranks are waiting at the post-val barrier, not in here).
   806	                        vo = raw_model(vb, allow_collectives=False)
   807	                    vl = run_vq_loss(vo, vb, loss_weights,
   808	                                     vo["frame_mask_recovered"], args.code_dim,
   809	                                     allow_collectives=False)
   810	                    for k, v in vl.items():
   811	                        val_losses[k].append(v.item())
   812	            val_total = float(np.mean(val_losses["total"]))
   813	            val_recon = float(np.mean(val_losses["pos"])) + float(np.mean(val_losses["rot"]))
   814	            log(f"  [val] total={val_total:.4f} pos={np.mean(val_losses['pos']):.4f} "
   815	                f"rot={np.mean(val_losses['rot']):.4f} commit={np.mean(val_losses['commit']):.5f}")
   816	            # checkpoint (rank-0 only, is_main already guards this block)
   817	            if not args.smoke:
   818	                # best_val_total = the HISTORICAL best (incl. this epoch). Persisted so
   819	                # --resume restores the right best-val bookkeeping and does NOT clobber
   820	                # a better earlier best_model.pt on the first post-resume validation,
   821	                # even when resuming from last_model.pt / epN_model.pt of a worse epoch
   822	                # (codex 019ea63f #1, mirrors train_graph_vae.py best_val_loss).
   823	                hist_best_val = min(best_val, val_total)
   824	                ckpt = {
   825	                    "model_state_dict": raw_model.state_dict(),
   826	                    "optimizer_state_dict": opt.state_dict(),
   827	                    "epoch": epoch, "global_step": n_iter,
   828	                    "args": vars(args), "val_total": val_total, "val_recon": val_recon,
   829	                    "best_val_total": hist_best_val,
   830	                    "git_sha": git_sha,
   831	                    "data_root": args.anytop_root,
   832	                    "max_joints": args.max_joints, "max_coarse": args.max_coarse,
   833	                    "codebook_active": [int(x) for x in out["active_codes"].tolist()],
   834	                }
   835	                last_path = out_dir / "last_model.pt"
   836	                torch.save(ckpt, last_path)
   837	                if val_total < best_val:
   838	                    best_val = val_total
   839	                    torch.save(ckpt, out_dir / "best_model.pt")
   840	                    log(f"  [ckpt] new best_model val_total={val_total:.4f}")
   841	                # Retained periodic snapshot: a byte-identical copy of last_model.pt
   842	                # (copyfile, not a 2nd torch.save — avoids re-serializing the ~666MB
   843	                # ckpt and guarantees identical bytes; codex 019ea63f #2). Not
   844	                # overwritten next val, so CV visual-QA can re-render mid-run ckpts.
   845	                if do_periodic:
   846	                    periodic_path = out_dir / f"ep{epoch + 1}_model.pt"
   847	                    shutil.copyfile(last_path, periodic_path)
   848	                    log(f"  [ckpt] saved periodic snapshot → {periodic_path}")
   849	        if is_ddp:
   850	            dist.barrier()
   851	
   852	    log("=== training loop complete ===")
   853	    if is_ddp:
   854	        dist.destroy_process_group()
   855	    return 0
   856	
   857	
   858	if __name__ == "__main__":
   859	    raise SystemExit(main())

name: repo-audit
description: "Independent repo-truth verification via codex exec. Codex reads the repo directly — Codex does not control what it sees. Use when user says \"audit\", \"verify claims\", \"check the repo\", \"hostile review\", \"repo audit\", or wants independent evidence-first verification."

# Repo Audit: Independent Repo-Truth Verification

Verify claims against repo evidence for: **$ARGUMENTS**

> **Routing**: This skill always uses `codex exec` (review_intent=verify, reviewer_backend=exec).
> See `docs/REVIEWER_ROUTING_POLICY.md` for the full routing policy.

## Overview

This is the reusable hostile-audit primitive for ARIS. It invokes `codex exec` so that the external reviewer (GPT) reads the repository directly. Codex does not curate or filter context — the reviewer decides what to inspect.

This skill is designed to be:
- **Reusable** — callable from any workflow or standalone
- **Evidence-bound** — output separates verified from inferred from unsupported
- **Honest** — blind spots are explicit, not hidden

## Constants

- **AUDIT_MODEL** = uses `codex exec` default model (configured in `~/.codex/config.toml`, typically `gpt-5.4`)
- **AUDIT_TIMEOUT** = 300 seconds (5 minutes)

## Inputs

1. **Claims to verify** — from user argument, `CLAIMS_FROM_RESULTS.md`, `AUTO_REVIEW.md`, or `NARRATIVE_REPORT.md`
2. **Scope** — which files, directories, or artifact types to prioritize (optional — reviewer can explore freely)

If no specific claims are provided, the reviewer audits the repo holistically: reading code, results, logs, and narrative docs, then checking for internal consistency.

## Workflow

### Step 1: Prepare Claims List

If the user provides specific claims, format them as a numbered list. If not, extract claims from available narrative docs.

### Step 2: Check exec Availability

```bash
command -v codex && echo "AVAILABLE" || echo "UNAVAILABLE"
```

If unavailable:
- **Do NOT fall back to MCP silently**
- Report to user:
  ```
  ⚠️ DEGRADED REVIEW: Repo-truth verification was requested via codex exec,
  but exec was unavailable. Used Codex MCP fallback on curated context.
  This is NOT equivalent to an independent repo audit.
  ```
- If user accepts degraded mode, proceed with MCP and label all outputs as DEGRADED

### Step 3: Execute Hostile Audit

```bash
codex exec "$(cat <<'PROMPT'
You are an independent auditor. Your job is to verify claims against this
repository's actual code, data, logs, and artifacts. Trust NOTHING the
author (Codex) tells you — verify everything yourself.

## Claims to verify:
[numbered list of claims]

## Instructions:
1. Read the experiment code, training scripts, and evaluation scripts
2. Read result files (JSON, CSV, logs) and verify reported numbers
3. Check if evaluation metrics are computed correctly
4. Look for cherry-picked results, missing seeds, or suspicious config choices
5. Read narrative docs (NARRATIVE_REPORT.md, AUTO_REVIEW.md) and cross-check
   each factual statement against the actual repo artifacts
6. Check for discrepancies between what the code does and what the docs claim

## Output — use this EXACT structure:

### Verification Report

- **Review intent**: verify
- **Backend requested**: exec
- **Backend used**: exec
- **Status**: FULL

### Evidence Inspected
- Files read: [list every file you opened]
- Logs inspected: [list]
- Artifact types: [JSON, CSV, .tar, wandb, etc.]
- Commands executed: [list shell commands you ran]

### Verified Findings
[Claims you independently confirmed — cite the file and line/value]

### Inferred Findings
[Claims consistent with evidence but not independently recomputed]

### Unsupported or Uncertain
[Claims lacking evidence or contradicting evidence — cite the discrepancy]

### Not Checked / Blind Spots
[What you did NOT inspect — be explicit]

### Overall Assessment
- Trustworthiness: [HIGH / MEDIUM / LOW]
- Critical issues: [list if any]
- Recommendation: [proceed / investigate further / stop and fix]

Be adversarial. Trust nothing. Cite everything.
PROMPT
)" --skip-git-repo-check 2>&1
```

### Step 4: Parse and Save Output

1. Save the full raw output to `REPO_AUDIT.md` in the project root
2. Extract structured fields: trustworthiness, critical issues, verified/unsupported claims
3. If critical issues found, present them prominently to the user

### Step 5: Report

Present results to user with:
- Number of claims verified / inferred / unsupported / not checked
- Any critical discrepancies
- The full `REPO_AUDIT.md` path for reference

## Degraded Mode (MCP Fallback)

If exec is unavailable and user accepts degraded mode:

```
mcp__codex__codex:
  config: {"model_reasoning_effort": "xhigh"}
  prompt: |
    [same prompt as above, but prefixed with:]

    NOTE: This review is running in DEGRADED mode. You are receiving
    curated context from Codex, not reading the repo directly.
    Flag any claims you cannot independently verify.

    [Codex pastes relevant file contents, logs, results]
```

All outputs must include:
```
⚠️ DEGRADED: This audit used Codex MCP on curated context, not codex exec.
It is NOT equivalent to an independent repo audit.
```

## Key Rules

- **Never present MCP fallback as equivalent to exec audit** — always label DEGRADED
- **Save full raw output** — do not summarize or truncate the reviewer's response
- **Cite evidence** — every finding must reference specific files, lines, or values
- **Explicit blind spots** — state what was NOT checked
- **No silent downgrade** — if exec fails, report it loudly

     1	# Graph-RVQ + Graph-CodeFlow 管线说明
     2	
     3	本文档解释当前代码里“Graph-VQVAE tokenizer + Graph-CodeFlow backbone”的核心信息流，重点回答：
     4	
     5	1. RVQ 是怎么量化的。
     6	2. backbone 训练时学的是什么。
     7	3. 推理时连续预测怎么一步步 nearest/snap 成 RVQ indices。
     8	4. 最后怎么用冻结 tokenizer decode 回动作。
     9	
    10	这条线和前面的 Gaussian VAE / latent diffusion 是分开的。它复用 graph attention / temporal attention / AnyTop dataset 这些模块，但模型和训练脚本在 `vq_model/` 与 `CodeFlow_Model/` 下独立维护。
    11	
    12	---
    13	
    14	## 0. 符号
    15	
    16	常用 shape：
    17	
    18	- `B`: batch size
    19	- `T`: fine motion 帧数，当前全长训练一般是 `300`
    20	- `T_lat`: latent 时间长度，`T / temporal_stride`，stride=4 时 `300 -> 75`
    21	- `J`: fine joints，任意拓扑，每个样本不同，batch 内 pad 到 `max_joints`
    22	- `C`: coarse slots，EdgeSegmentPool 后的有效 slot 数，batch 内 pad 到 `max_coarse`
    23	- `D`: latent/channel dim，当前通常 `512`
    24	- `Q`: RVQ residual stages，当前通常 `4`
    25	- `K`: 每个 codebook 的 code 数，当前通常 `512`
    26	- `L`: caption token 长度，T5 token cache 通常 `64`
    27	
    28	关键 tensor：
    29	
    30	```text
    31	motion              [B, J, 13, T]       AnyTopDataset 输出
    32	motion_in           [B, T, J, 13]       送进 tokenizer encoder
    33	h_lat               [B, T_lat, C, D]    RVQ 之前的连续 coarse-slot latent
    34	z_q                 [B, T_lat, C, D]    RVQ 之后的 summed code embedding
    35	indices             [B, T_lat, C, Q]    每个 token 的 Q 个 code id
    36	token_mask          [B, T_lat, C]       有效 latent token
    37	pooled_adjacency    [B, C, C]           coarse graph 邻接
    38	pooled_geodesic     [B, C, C]           coarse graph hop distance
    39	caption_emb         [B, 768]            T5 mean pooled 文本
    40	caption_token_emb   [B, L, 768]         T5 token-level 文本
    41	```
    42	
    43	---
    44	
    45	## 1. Graph-VQVAE Tokenizer 总体流程
    46	
    47	核心文件：
    48	
    49	- `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py`
    50	- `src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py`
    51	- `src/models/vq_model/masked_motion_decoder.py`
    52	- `scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py`
    53	- `src/models/vq_model/losses.py`
    54	
    55	`GraphVQTokenizer` 顶部 docstring 已经把主流程写得很清楚：
    56	
    57	代码位置：
    58	
    59	- `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:1-30`
    60	
    61	实际信息流：
    62	
    63	```text
    64	AnyTop 13ch motion
    65	  [B, J, 13, T]
    66	    -> permute
    67	  [B, T, J, 13]
    68	    -> SkeletonEncoder + SlotNorm
    69	  [B, T, J, D]
    70	    -> EdgeSegmentPool
    71	  h_lat [B, T_lat, C, D]
    72	    -> optional pre-VQ graph-temporal layers
    73	  h_lat [B, T_lat, C, D]
    74	    -> MaskedResidualVQ
    75	  z_q [B, T_lat, C, D], indices [B, T_lat, C, Q]
    76	    -> optional post-VQ graph-temporal layers
    77	    -> temporal repeat_interleave
    78	  [B, T, C, D]
    79	    -> MaskedMotionDecoder slot-to-joint
    80	  feats [B, T, J, D]
    81	    -> anytop13 head
    82	  pred_motion [B, T, J, 13]
    83	```
    84	
    85	核心代码：
    86	
    87	- tokenizer 构造 encoder/pool/RVQ/decoder：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:98-184`
    88	- encoder + EdgeSegmentPool：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:186-257`
    89	- decode：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:259-293`
    90	- full forward：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:486-513`
    91	
    92	人话解释：
    93	
    94	Tokenizer 的作用是把任意拓扑骨架动作先变成规则的 coarse-slot latent grid，再用 RVQ 变成离散 code。它不是在 fine joint 上直接做 VQ，而是在我们图池化后的 `T_lat x C` token map 上做 VQ。
    95	
    96	---
    97	
    98	## 2. RVQ 之前：图感知 coarse latent 怎么来
    99	
   100	入口在：
   101	
   102	- `GraphVQTokenizer.encode()`：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:186-257`
   103	
   104	关键步骤：
   105	
   106	1. Dataset 给出 `batch.anytop_x [B,J,13,T]`。
   107	2. 先转成 `[B,T,J,13]`：
   108	   - `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:199`
   109	3. `SkeletonEncoder` 同时编码 motion 和 skeleton：
   110	   - motion path 得到 `h0 [B,T,J,D]`
   111	   - skeleton-only path 得到 `s_j [B,J,D]`
   112	   - `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:202-213`
   113	4. `SlotNorm` 后进入 `EdgeSegmentPool`：
   114	   - `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:214-225`
   115	5. Pool 输出：
   116	   - `h_lat / pooled_features [B,T_lat,C,D]`
   117	   - `assignment [B,J,C]`
   118	   - `coarse_mask [B,C]`
   119	   - `frame_mask_lat [B,T_lat]`
   120	   - `pooled_adjacency [B,C,C]`
   121	   - `pooled_geodesic [B,C,C]`
   122	   - `pooled_skeleton_embeddings [B,C,D]`
   123	   - `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:226-236`
   124	
   125	然后可选的 pre-VQ graph-temporal refine：
   126	
   127	- `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:241-243`
   128	
   129	这里的 `CoarseGraphTemporalLayer` 是：
   130	
   131	- spatial: 对每个 latent frame，在 `C` 个 coarse slots 上做 graph attention。
   132	- temporal: 对每个 coarse slot，在 `T_lat` 上做 temporal attention。
   133	
   134	代码位置：
   135	
   136	- `src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:49-95`
   137	
   138	---
   139	
   140	## 3. RVQ 量化：一个 token 变成 Q 个 code id
   141	
   142	核心类：
   143	
   144	- `MaskedResidualVQ`
   145	- `src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:231-420`
   146	
   147	每个有效 token 是一个 `D` 维向量：
   148	
   149	```text
   150	x = h_lat[b, t, c]   [D]
   151	```
   152	
   153	RVQ 不是只查一次 codebook，而是 residual quantization：
   154	
   155	```text
   156	residual_0 = x
   157	
   158	for q in 0..Q-1:
   159	    id_q = nearest_codebook_q(residual_q)
   160	    e_q  = codebook_q[id_q]
   161	    residual_{q+1} = residual_q - e_q
   162	
   163	z_q_token = e_0 + e_1 + ... + e_{Q-1}
   164	indices_token = [id_0, id_1, ..., id_{Q-1}]
   165	```
   166	
   167	代码对应：
   168	
   169	- nearest lookup `quantize()`：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:164-178`
   170	- residual loop：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:342-367`
   171	- accumulated `q_total`：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:307-308,365`
   172	- output `indices [B,T_lat,C,Q]`：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:345-349`
   173	- straight-through estimator：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:411-417`
   174	
   175	mask 规则很重要：
   176	
   177	- `token_mask=False` 的 padded token 不参与 codebook EMA。
   178	- padded token 的 `indices=-1`。
   179	- padded token 的 `z_q=0`。
   180	
   181	代码位置：
   182	
   183	- mask-aware contract：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:1-35`
   184	- padded indices 置 `-1`：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:345-349`
   185	- padded token zero + zero grad：`src/models/vq_model/quantizer.py:411-417`
   186	
   187	---
   188	
   189	## 4. Graph-VQVAE Tokenizer 训练目标
   190	
   191	训练脚本：
   192	
   193	- `scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py`
   194	
   195	模型构造参数：
   196	
   197	- `scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py:276-366`
   198	- `scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py:476-495`
   199	
   200	训练 step：
   201	
   202	- forward：`scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py:619-627`
   203	- gate 检查 `z_q / indices` shape：`scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py:628-637`
   204	- loss：`scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py:639-641`
   205	- backward/step：`scripts/train_graph_vqvae.py:650-685`
   206	
   207	VQ loss 文件：
   208	
   209	- `src/models/vq_model/losses.py`
   210	
   211	总 loss：
   212	
   213	```text
   214	L_total =
   215	    w_pos     * pos
   216	  + w_rot     * rot
   217	  + w_vel     * vel
   218	  + w_contact * contact
   219	  + w_world   * world
   220	  + w_fk      * fk
   221	  + w_traj    * traj
   222	  + w_commit  * commit
   223	```
   224	
   225	代码位置：
   226	
   227	- loss 公式注释：`src/models/vq_model/losses.py:1-21`
   228	- pos/rot/vel/contact：`src/models/vq_model/losses.py:103-111`
   229	- world/fk/traj：`src/models/vq_model/losses.py:113-123`
   230	- commit loss：`src/models/vq_model/losses.py:125-148`
   231	- total assembly：`src/models/vq_model/losses.py:149-155`
   232	
   233	注意：
   234	
   235	- 这里没有 KL，因为 VQ 替代了 Gaussian latent。
   236	- `commit` 是 RVQ 的 commitment loss，用来让 encoder output 靠近选中的 code。
   237	- `w_commit` 只在 loss wrapper 里乘一次。
   238	
   239	---
   240	
   241	## 5. Token Export：为什么 backbone 不在线跑 tokenizer encoder
   242	
   243	训练 CodeFlow backbone 前，先用冻结的 Graph-VQVAE tokenizer 离线导出 token cache。
   244	
   245	脚本：
   246	
   247	- `scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py`
   248	
   249	它对每条 motion 保存：
   250	
   251	```text
   252	z_q                         [T_lat,C,D]
   253	indices                     [T_lat,C,Q]
   254	token_mask                  [T_lat,C]
   255	coarse_mask                 [C]
   256	frame_mask_lat              [T_lat]
   257	pooled_adjacency            [C,C]
   258	pooled_geodesic             [C,C]
   259	pooled_skeleton_embeddings  [C,D]
   260	assignment                  [J,C]

   241	## 5. Token Export：为什么 backbone 不在线跑 tokenizer encoder
   242	
   243	训练 CodeFlow backbone 前，先用冻结的 Graph-VQVAE tokenizer 离线导出 token cache。
   244	
   245	脚本：
   246	
   247	- `scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py`
   248	
   249	它对每条 motion 保存：
   250	
   251	```text
   252	z_q                         [T_lat,C,D]
   253	indices                     [T_lat,C,Q]
   254	token_mask                  [T_lat,C]
   255	coarse_mask                 [C]
   256	frame_mask_lat              [T_lat]
   257	pooled_adjacency            [C,C]
   258	pooled_geodesic             [C,C]
   259	pooled_skeleton_embeddings  [C,D]
   260	assignment                  [J,C]
   261	s_j                         [J,D]
   262	joint_mask                  [J]
   263	caption_emb                 [768]
   264	caption_token_emb           [L,768]
   265	caption_token_mask          [L]
   266	```
   267	
   268	代码位置：
   269	
   270	- export schema 注释：`scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py:1-31`
   271	- load frozen tokenizer：`scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py:57-82`

   275	- RVQ identity audit：`scripts/export_graph_vq_tokens.py:285-332`
   276	
   277	这里有一个关键自检：
   278	
   279	```text
   280	ids_to_embeddings(indices) ≈ z_q
   281	```
   282	
   283	也就是从导出的 indices 重新查 codebook，应该能还原导出的 `z_q`。这是保证后续 snap/decode 正确的核心 gate。

   287	## 6. CodeFlow 训练数据怎么读
   288	
   289	Dataset：
   290	
   291	- `src/models/CodeFlow_Model/token_dataset.py`
   292	
   293	`TokenCacheDataset` 不读原始 motion，它读 export 出来的 `.npz`：
   294	
   295	- `z_q [T_lat,C,D]`
   296	- `indices [T_lat,C,Q]`
   297	- graph metadata
   298	- text embedding
   299	- decode metadata
   300	
   301	代码位置：
   302	
   303	- dataset schema：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/token_dataset.py:1-14`
   304	- `__getitem__` 读 `.npz`：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/token_dataset.py:45-75`
   305	- collate：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/token_dataset.py:78-90`
   306	
   307	所以 backbone 训练阶段的 target 是冻结 tokenizer 的 `z_q`，不是原始 motion，也不是 pre-RVQ `h_lat`。
   308	
   309	---
   310	
   311	## 7. CodeFlow Backbone 学什么
   312	
   313	核心 wrapper：
   314	
   315	- `src/models/CodeFlow_Model/flow.py`
   316	
   317	正式 backbone：
   318	
   319	- `src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py`
   320	
   321	旧 Level-A probe：
   322	
   323	- `src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_codeflow.py`
   324	
   325	当前正式配置默认走：
   326	
   327	```text
   328	GraphCodeFlow(model_variant="graph_pscf")
   329	```
   330	
   331	代码位置：
   332	
   333	- variant selector：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/flow.py:55-99`
   334	- train script 默认 `graph_pscf`：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:243-253`
   335	
   336	训练目标是 rectified flow velocity，不是直接分类 code id：
   337	
   338	```text
   339	x = normalize(z_q)
   340	noise ~ N(0, I)
   341	t ~ Uniform(0, 1)
   342	
   343	z_t = t * x + (1 - t) * noise
   344	v_target = x - noise
   345	
   346	v_pred = backbone(z_t, t, text, graph)
   347	loss = MSE(v_pred, v_target) over valid token_mask
   348	```
   349	
   350	代码位置：
   351	
   352	- flow math 注释：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/flow.py:1-30`
   353	- empirical norm：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/flow.py:108-126`
   354	- flow loss：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/flow.py:170-236`
   355	- train step：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:547-574`
   356	
   357	训练时的 batch cond：
   358	
   359	- `caption_emb [B,768]`
   360	- `caption_token_emb [B,L,768]`
   361	- `caption_token_mask [B,L]`
   362	- `has_text [B]`，训练时会按 `cond_drop_prob` 做 CFG dropout
   363	- `pooled_adjacency / pooled_geodesic`
   364	- `pooled_skeleton_embeddings`
   365	- `coarse_mask / frame_mask_lat`
   366	
   367	代码位置：
   368	
   369	- `build_cond()`：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:90-113`
   370	- CFG text drop：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:99-102`
   371	
   372	---
   373	
   374	## 8. Graph-PSCF Backbone 的结构
   375	
   376	核心文件：
   377	
   378	- `src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py`
   379	- `src/models/CodeFlow_Model/dit_blocks.py`
   380	
   381	Graph-PSCF 有三条流：
   382	
   383	```text
   384	slot stream   h_slot  [B, T_lat, C, D]   RVQ latent grid，保留 coarse graph 结构
   385	frame stream  h_frame [B, T_lat, D]      每个 latent frame 一个 holder token
   386	text stream   h_text  [B, L, D]          T5 token stream
   387	```
   388	
   389	代码位置：
   390	
   391	- 三流说明：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:12-31`
   392	- forward 输入检查：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:336-421`
   393	
   394	### 8.1 slot stream：图感知
   395	
   396	slot stream 每层会做：
   397	
   398	1. graph-spatial attention over `C`
   399	2. temporal attention over `T_lat`
   400	3. strict mask
   401	
   402	代码位置：
   403	
   404	- `GraphSlotTemporalBlock`：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:66-125`
   405	- graph attention over coarse slots：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:99-113`
   406	- temporal attention：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:115-125`
   407	
   408	这说明 backbone 是图感知的：它直接使用 `pooled_adjacency` 和 `pooled_geodesic`。
   409	
   410	### 8.2 frame holder：压缩时间级全局上下文
   411	
   412	`h_frame [B,T_lat,D]` 不是替代 `h_slot [B,T_lat,C,D]`，而是每一帧一个全局 holder。
   413	
   414	交互方式：
   415	
   416	1. holder 读同一帧的所有 coarse slots。
   417	2. holder 更新后再 broadcast/add 回该帧所有 slots。
   418	
   419	代码位置：
   420	
   421	- `GraphFrameSlotCoupling`：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:128-220`
   422	- holder cross-attn 读 slots：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:184-206`
   423	- holder 注回 slots：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:208-219`
   424	
   425	### 8.3 text stream：对齐 CodeFlow/FLUX 式 double/single stream
   426	
   427	两路文本：
   428	
   429	1. `text_global [B,768] -> pooled_text -> cond`
   430	   - 用于 timestep/AdaLN/FiLM 调制。
   431	2. `text_tokens [B,L,768] -> h_text`
   432	   - 和 frame stream 做 double/single stream attention。
   433	
   434	代码位置：
   435	
   436	- text pooled/token projection：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:279-282`
   437	- `cond = timestep_emb + pooled_text`：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:425-430`
   438	- token stream：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/graph_pscf.py:444-447`
   439

   464	## 9. Backbone 训练循环
   465	
   466	训练脚本：
   467	
   468	- `scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py`
   469	
   470	流程：
   471	
   472	```text
   473	TokenCacheDataset
   474	  -> batch:
   475	      z_q, token_mask, graph meta, text
   476	  -> build_cond()
   477	  -> GraphCodeFlow.flow_loss()
   478	  -> loss.backward()
   479	  -> optimizer.step()
   480	```
   481	
   482	代码位置：
   483	
   484	- load frozen tokenizer：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:65-87`

   490	- train step：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:547-574`
   491	- projection QA：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:193-240`
   492	- validation + checkpoint：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:607-638`
   493	
   494	训练 loss 只有 flow MSE：
   495	
   496	```text
   497	loss = flow_loss_weight * flow_loss
   498	```
   499	
   500	代码位置：
   501	
   502	- `scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:552-555`
   503	
   504	`terminal_loss_weight` 和 `clean_loss_weight` 参数存在，但当前代码没有把它们接入 loss；正式训练是 flow-only。

   508	## 10. 推理：从 skeleton + text 生成 motion
   509	
   510	推理脚本：
   511	
   512	- `scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py`
   513	
   514	脚本顶部 docstring 写了完整推理路径：
   515	
   516	- `scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:1-17`
   517	
   518	实际步骤：
   519	
   520	### Step 1. 读目标 skeleton 和 prompt

exec
/usr/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba handoff/20260611_0954_rvq_codeflow_pipeline_explainer.md | sed -n '521,760p'" in /iridisfs/scratch/ts1v23/workspace/noKslot_clean
 succeeded in 0ms:
   521	
   522	Dataset 取一个样本，作为目标骨架；motion 本身不作为生成输入，只用来拿 skeleton / caption / 可选 GT QA。
   523	
   524	代码位置：
   525	
   526	- dataset：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:159-166`
   527	- batch：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:180-187`
   528	
   529	### Step 2. skeleton-only pool，得到 coarse graph meta
   530	
   531	```text
   532	tokenizer.prepare_skeleton_only(batch, T_lat_i)
   533	```
   534	
   535	输出：
   536	
   537	```text
   538	s_j
   539	assignment [B,J,C]
   540	coarse_mask [B,C]
   541	frame_mask_lat [B,T_lat]
   542	token_mask [B,T_lat,C]
   543	pooled_adjacency [B,C,C]
   544	pooled_geodesic [B,C,C]
   545	pooled_skeleton_embeddings [B,C,D]
   546	```
   547	
   548	代码位置：
   549	
   550	- prepare_skeleton_only 实现：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:414-465`
   551	- animate 调用：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:204-216`
   552	
   553	### Step 3. ODE + CFG 采样连续 latent `z_hat`
   554	
   555	```text
   556	z_hat = flow.sample(cond, token_mask, T_lat, C, steps, cfg_scale)
   557	```
   558	
   559	这里从高斯噪声开始，用 learned velocity field 走 ODE：
   560	
   561	```text
   562	z_0 ~ noise
   563	for i in 0..steps-1:
   564	    v_cond   = backbone(z_i, t_i, cond)
   565	    v_uncond = backbone(z_i, t_i, cond with text dropped)
   566	    v = v_uncond + cfg_scale * (v_cond - v_uncond)
   567	    z_{i+1} = z_i + dt * v
   568	
   569	z_hat = denormalize(z_final)
   570	```
   571	
   572	代码位置：
   573	
   574	- sampler：`src/models/CodeFlow_Model/flow.py:241-291`
   575	- animate 调用：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:217-221`
   576	
   577	注意：此时 `z_hat [B,T_lat,C,D]` 是连续向量，还不是合法的 RVQ code sequence。
   578	
   579	### Step 4. residual nearest snap：连续 `z_hat` 变回 RVQ indices
   580	
   581	```text
   582	proj = tokenizer.nearest_residual_ids(z_hat, token_mask)
   583	indices_hat = proj["indices_hat"]   [B,T_lat,C,Q]
   584	z_snap      = proj["z_snap"]         [B,T_lat,C,D]
   585	```
   586	
   587	算法和训练时 RVQ residual loop 一样：
   588	
   589	```text
   590	residual = z_hat
   591	for q in 0..Q-1:
   592	    id_q = nearest_codebook_q(residual)
   593	    e_q  = codebook_q[id_q]
   594	    residual = residual - e_q
   595	z_snap = sum_q e_q
   596	indices_hat = stack(id_q)
   597	```
   598	
   599	代码位置：
   600	
   601	- nearest residual API：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:346-412`
   602	- animate 调用：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:217-225`
   603	- training QA 里也用它：`scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py:193-240`
   604	
   605	这一步的 `projection_error` 是关键诊断：
   606	
   607	```text
   608	projection_error = MSE(z_hat, z_snap) over valid tokens
   609	```
   610	
   611	如果 continuous decode 很好、snapped decode 很差，说明 flow 预测到了 codebook 流形附近但 snap 质量不够；如果两者都差，则是 flow/backbone 本身没学好。
   612	
   613	### Step 5. 从 indices decode 回 motion
   614	
   615	```text
   616	pred = tokenizer.decode_from_indices(indices_hat, meta, batch)
   617	```
   618	
   619	内部做两步：
   620	
   621	1. `ids_to_embeddings(indices_hat)`：
   622	   - 每个 stage 查 codebook embedding。
   623	   - 把 Q 个 residual embedding 相加。
   624	   - 得到 `z_q / z_snap [B,T_lat,C,D]`。
   625	2. 调 `decode(z_q, meta, batch)`：
   626	   - post-VQ refine
   627	   - temporal upsample
   628	   - slot-to-joint decoder
   629	   - anytop13 head
   630	
   631	代码位置：
   632	
   633	- indices -> embeddings：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:306-344`
   634	- decode_from_indices：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:467-484`
   635	- decode 主体：`src/models/vq_model/graph_vq_tokenizer.py:259-293`
   636	- animate 调用：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:222-226`
   637	
   638	### Step 6. anytop13 motion -> world positions -> GIF
   639	
   640	输出 `pred_motion [B,T,J,13]` 还是 normalized AnyTop 13ch。渲染前要反归一化，然后走 rot6d FK 恢复世界坐标：
   641	
   642	```text
   643	raw = pred_norm * (std + floor) + mean
   644	world = recover_from_bvh_rot_np(raw, parents, offsets)
   645	```
   646	
   647	代码位置：
   648	
   649	- de-normalize + FK recovery：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:228-241`
   650	- GIF 渲染：`scripts/animate_graph_codeflow.py:256-270`
   651	
   652	---
   653	
   654	## 11. 一句话版信息流
   655	
   656	### Tokenizer 训练
   657	
   658	```text
   659	motion [B,J,13,T]
   660	  -> graph encoder
   661	  -> EdgeSegmentPool
   662	  -> h_lat [B,T_lat,C,D]
   663	  -> RVQ
   664	  -> z_q + indices
   665	  -> frozen-style decoder during training
   666	  -> pred_motion [B,T,J,13]
   667	  -> reconstruction + geometry + commit loss
   668	```
   669	
   670	### Backbone 训练
   671	
   672	```text
   673	frozen tokenizer export:
   674	  motion -> z_q [T_lat,C,D] + indices [T_lat,C,Q] + graph/text metadata
   675	
   676	CodeFlow training:
   677	  z_q + noise + t -> z_t
   678	  graph/text-conditioned backbone predicts v
   679	  MSE(v_pred, z_q - noise)
   680	```
   681	
   682	### 推理
   683	
   684	```text
   685	skeleton + text
   686	  -> skeleton-only EdgeSegmentPool meta
   687	  -> ODE sample continuous z_hat [B,T_lat,C,D]
   688	  -> nearest_residual_ids(z_hat)
   689	  -> indices_hat [B,T_lat,C,Q]
   690	  -> ids_to_embeddings(indices_hat) = z_snap
   691	  -> frozen tokenizer decode
   692	  -> pred_motion [B,T,J,13]
   693	  -> FK/world recovery
   694	  -> GIF
   695	```
   696	
   697	---
   698	
   699	## 12. 给同事讲时的重点
   700	
   701	1. 我们的 VQ token 不是人体固定 joint token，而是任意拓扑骨架经过 `EdgeSegmentPool` 后的 coarse-slot token。
   702	2. RVQ 的离散 id 是 `[T_lat, C, Q]`，每个 `(t,c)` 有 `Q` 个 residual code。
   703	3. Backbone 不直接预测 id，也不是 latent diffusion；它在 post-RVQ 的连续 `z_q` 空间做 rectified flow。
   704	4. 推理时先生成连续 `z_hat`，再逐 stage residual nearest 到 codebook，得到完整 `indices_hat [T_lat,C,Q]`。
   705	5. 最终动作不是 backbone 直接输出的，而是冻结 Graph-VQVAE decoder 从 snapped RVQ indices 解出来的。
   706	6. 图感知来自两处：
   707	   - tokenizer encoder/pool/decode 使用 skeleton graph。
   708	   - Graph-PSCF backbone 的 slot stream 使用 `pooled_adjacency / pooled_geodesic` 做 graph attention。
   709	7. 文本也是两路：
   710	   - T5 mean pooled global text 进入 AdaLN/FiLM 条件。
   711	   - T5 token sequence 进入 double/single stream attention。
   712	

codex
The handoff’s major structure is now visible. I’m moving into source evidence: tokenizer/RVQ first, then token export and CodeFlow training/inference, because those are where misleading handoff errors would matter most.

     1	"""GraphVQTokenizer — coarse-slot structured Residual-VQ tokenizer for AnyTop
     2	arbitrary-topology motion (Graph-VQVAE v1.0).
     3	
     4	Pipeline (handoff/20260608_graph_vqvae_l5_pipeline_plan.md):
     5	
     6	  anytop13 [B,J,13,T] (from AnyTopDataset)
     7	    -> permute [B,T,J,13]
     8	    -> SkeletonEncoder(anytop13_split, graphormer)        [reused by IMPORT]
     9	    -> SlotNorm                                           [reused by IMPORT]
    10	    -> EdgeSegmentPool(edge_segment, max_coarse=C)        [reused by IMPORT]
    11	       => pooled_features [B,T_lat,C,D] + assignment P [B,J,C] + pooled graph meta
    12	    -> (optional) n_pre_vq coarse graph+temporal refine layers
    13	    -> MaskedResidualVQ (Q stages, mask-aware, padded slots excluded)
    14	       => z_q [B,T_lat,C,D], indices [B,T_lat,C,Q] (-1 on padded)
    15	    -> (optional) n_post_vq coarse graph+temporal refine layers
    16	    -> repeat_interleave(temporal_stride) -> [B,T,C,D]
    17	    -> MaskedMotionDecoder (F1: strict padded-slot-key mask)  [vq_model fork]
    18	    -> anytop13 root/non-root output heads
    19	    -> pred_motion [B,T,J,13]
    20	
    21	This module OWNS its own decoder + heads (under src/models/vq_model/) and reuses
    22	the encoder / pool / graph-attention by IMPORT — it does NOT subclass or call
    23	GraphMotionVAE, so the VQ checkpoint + training loop are fully independent of the
    24	Gaussian Graph-VAE (which keeps running unchanged).
    25	
    26	bf16-safe: the whole forward runs under the caller's autocast; the quantizer does
    27	its codebook distance/argmin/EMA in fp32 internally and the graph attention forces
    28	fp32 softmax. The pool requires fp32 inputs (joint_features / adjacency /
    29	geodesic), so the encoder output + graph tensors are cast to fp32 before the pool
    30	call (mirrors how the VAE feeds the pool — the pool is fp32-only by contract).
    31	"""
    32	
    33	from __future__ import annotations
    34	
    35	import torch
    36	import torch.nn as nn
    37	import torch.nn.functional as F
    38	
    39	from src.models.encoder import SkeletonEncoder
    40	from src.models.slot_norm import SlotNorm
    41	from src.models.graph_salad.pool_edge_segment import EdgeSegmentPool
    42	from src.models.graph_salad.attention import GraphAttentionBlock
    43	from src.models.motion_decoder import TemporalSelfAttention
    44	
    45	from .quantizer import MaskedResidualVQ
    46	from .masked_motion_decoder import MaskedMotionDecoder
    47	
    48	
    49	class CoarseGraphTemporalLayer(nn.Module):
    50	    """One coarse-latent refine layer: graph self-attention over coarse slots
    51	    (per latent frame) + temporal self-attention over latent frames (per slot).
    52	
    53	    Uses GraphAttentionBlock (scalar adj+geo bias — pooled_adjacency is binary
    54	    {0,1}, pooled_geodesic is its Floyd distance, exactly the block's contract)
    55	    for the spatial sub-block, and TemporalSelfAttention for the temporal one
    56	    (both bf16-safe / fp32-softmax). Each sub-block re-masks padded slots/frames
    57	    so the layer output is clean by construction (mirrors the shared
    58	    GraphTemporalDecoderLayer discipline, but at the coarse level with the pool's
    59	    adjacency/geodesic rather than graphormer edge types).
    60	    """
    61	
    62	    def __init__(self, d_model: int, n_heads: int, d_ff: int, dropout: float = 0.1):
    63	        super().__init__()
    64	        self.spatial = GraphAttentionBlock(d_model, n_heads, d_ff, dropout)
    65	        self.temporal = TemporalSelfAttention(d_model, n_heads, dropout)
    66	
    67	    def forward(
    68	        self,
    69	        x: torch.Tensor,                 # [B, T_lat, C, D]
    70	        pooled_adjacency: torch.Tensor,  # [B, C, C] fp32 binary
    71	        pooled_geodesic: torch.Tensor,   # [B, C, C] fp32 Floyd dist
    72	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,       # [B, C] bool
    73	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,    # [B, T_lat] bool
    74	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
    75	        B, T_lat, C, D = x.shape
    76	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(x.dtype)   # [B,1,C,1]
    77	        fm = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(x.dtype)  # [B,T_lat,1,1]
    78	
    79	        # --- spatial: graph attention over coarse slots, per latent frame ---
    80	        xs = x.reshape(B * T_lat, C, D)
    81	        adj_e = pooled_adjacency.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C, C)
    82	        geo_e = pooled_geodesic.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C, C)
    83	        cm_e = coarse_mask.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C)
    84	        # GraphAttentionBlock requires fp32 adjacency/geodesic; node_mask bool.
    85	        xs = self.spatial(xs, adj_e.float(), geo_e.float(), cm_e, validate_inputs=False)
    86	        x = xs.reshape(B, T_lat, C, D) * cm   # re-mask padded slots
    87	
    88	        # --- temporal: self-attention over latent frames, per coarse slot ---
    89	        xt = x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).reshape(B * C, T_lat, D)
    90	        fm_e = frame_mask_lat.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, C, T_lat).reshape(B * C, T_lat)
    91	        # A fully-padded slot has an all-False frame row → TemporalSelfAttention
    92	        # nan_to_num(0.0) keeps it zero; re-mask below removes any residue.
    93	        xt = self.temporal(xt, fm_e)
    94	        x = xt.reshape(B, C, T_lat, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
    95	        return x * cm * fm                    # re-mask padded slots + frames
    96	
    97	
    98	class GraphVQTokenizer(nn.Module):
    99	    """Graph-aware coarse-slot RVQ tokenizer. See module docstring for the pipeline."""
   100	
   101	    def __init__(
   102	        self,
   103	        d_model: int = 512,
   104	        n_heads: int = 8,
   105	        d_ff: int = 1536,
   106	        n_graph_layers: int = 4,
   107	        n_enc_temporal_layers: int = 2,
   108	        n_pre_vq_layers: int = 2,
   109	        n_post_vq_layers: int = 2,
   110	        n_cross_layers: int = 3,
   111	        n_dec_temporal_layers: int = 2,
   112	        max_coarse: int = 50,
   113	        temporal_stride: int = 4,
   114	        temporal_kernel: int = 9,
   115	        dropout: float = 0.1,
   116	        joint_feat_dim: int = 9,
   117	        # quantizer
   118	        code_dim: int = 512,
   119	        num_codes: int = 512,
   120	        num_quantizers: int = 4,

   178	            temporal_kernel=temporal_kernel,
   179	            dropout=dropout,
   180	        )
   181	        self.anytop13_head = nn.ModuleDict({
   182	            "out_root": nn.Linear(d_model, 13),
   183	            "out_nonroot": nn.Linear(d_model, 13),
   184	        })
   185	
   186	    def encode(self, batch) -> dict:
   187	        """anytop13 batch -> pooled coarse slots + pool graph metadata.
   188	
   189	        Returns the pre-VQ pooled features (graph-refined if n_pre_vq>0) plus all
   190	        masks / assignment / pooled graph tensors the decoder + VQ need.
   191	        """
   192	        if batch.anytop_x is None:
   193	            raise ValueError("GraphVQTokenizer requires batch.anytop_x "
   194	                             "(use --dataset anytop_truebones)")
   195	        if batch.anytop_graph_dist is None or batch.anytop_joint_relations is None:
   196	            raise ValueError("GraphVQTokenizer(graphormer) requires "
   197	                             "batch.anytop_graph_dist + batch.anytop_joint_relations")
   198	
   199	        motion_in = batch.anytop_x.permute(0, 3, 1, 2).contiguous()  # [B,T,J,13]
   200	        gd, jr = batch.anytop_graph_dist, batch.anytop_joint_relations
   201	
   202	        h0 = self.encoder(
   203	            motion_in, batch.skeleton_features,
   204	            batch.adjacency, batch.geodesic_dist,
   205	            batch.joint_mask, batch.frame_mask,
   206	            name_hashes=batch.name_hashes,
   207	            graph_dist=gd, joint_relations=jr,
   208	        )  # [B,T,J,D]
   209	        s_j = self.encoder.encode_skeleton(
   210	            batch.skeleton_features, batch.adjacency, batch.geodesic_dist,
   211	            batch.joint_mask, name_hashes=batch.name_hashes,
   212	            graph_dist=gd, joint_relations=jr,
   213	        )  # [B,J,D]
   214	        h0 = self.slot_norm(h0)
   215	
   216	        # Pool is fp32-only by contract: cast features + graph tensors to fp32.
   217	        pool_out = self.pool(
   218	            joint_features=h0.float(),
   219	            skeleton_embeddings=s_j.float(),
   220	            adjacency=batch.adjacency.float(),
   221	            geodesic_dist=batch.geodesic_dist.float(),
   222	            joint_mask=batch.joint_mask,
   223	            frame_mask=batch.frame_mask,
   224	            parent_indices=batch.parent_indices,
   225	        )
   226	        h_lat = pool_out["pooled_features"]            # [B,T_lat,C,D] fp32
   227	        coarse_mask = pool_out["pooled_mask"]          # [B,C] bool
   228	        frame_mask_lat = pool_out["frame_mask_down"]   # [B,T_lat] bool
   229	        assignment = pool_out["assignment"]            # [B,J,C] fp32
   230	        pooled_adjacency = pool_out["pooled_adjacency"]   # [B,C,C] fp32
   231	        pooled_geodesic = pool_out["pooled_geodesic"]     # [B,C,C] fp32
   232	        # gap #1 (Graph-CodeFlow): the pool computes per-segment skeleton
   233	        # embeddings but encode() dropped them. Expose ADDITIVELY (extra dict key,
   234	        # no existing key/value altered) for the CodeFlow backbone's skeleton
   235	        # conditioning + offline export. fp32, [B,C,D]; zero on padded slots.
   236	        pooled_skeleton_embeddings = pool_out["pooled_skeleton_embeddings"]  # [B,C,D] fp32
   237	
   238	        # Bring back to the autocast dtype for the refine layers (graph attention
   239	        # casts adjacency/geodesic to fp32 internally; features can be bf16).
   240	        h_lat = h_lat.to(h0.dtype)
   241	        for layer in self.pre_vq_layers:
   242	            h_lat = layer(h_lat, pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic,
   243	                          coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat)
   244	        # latent-slot validity = valid coarse slot AND valid latent frame.
   245	        token_mask = coarse_mask.unsqueeze(1) & frame_mask_lat.unsqueeze(-1)  # [B,T_lat,C]
   246	
   247	        return {
   248	            "h_lat": h_lat,
   249	            "s_j": s_j,
   250	            "assignment": assignment,
   251	            "coarse_mask": coarse_mask,
   252	            "frame_mask_lat": frame_mask_lat,
   253	            "token_mask": token_mask,
   254	            "pooled_adjacency": pooled_adjacency,
   255	            "pooled_geodesic": pooled_geodesic,
   256	            "pooled_skeleton_embeddings": pooled_skeleton_embeddings,
   257	        }
   258	
   259	    def decode(self, z_q, enc, batch) -> dict:
   260	        """Quantized coarse slots z_q [B,T_lat,C,D] -> pred_motion [B,T,J,13]."""
   261	        coarse_mask = enc["coarse_mask"]
   262	        frame_mask_lat = enc["frame_mask_lat"]
   263	
   264	        # Optional post-VQ refine (on the quantized slots).
   265	        z = z_q
   266	        for layer in self.post_vq_layers:
   267	            z = layer(z, enc["pooled_adjacency"], enc["pooled_geodesic"],
   268	                      coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat)
   269	
   270	        # Temporal upsample back to full resolution.
   271	        z_up = z.repeat_interleave(self.temporal_stride, dim=1)   # [B,T,C,D]
   272	        frame_mask_recovered = frame_mask_lat.repeat_interleave(
   273	            self.temporal_stride, dim=-1)                          # [B,T]
   274	
   275	        feats = self.decoder(
   276	            z_up,                       # slot_features [B,T,C,D]
   277	            enc["s_j"],                 # skeleton embeddings [B,J,D]
   278	            enc["assignment"],          # real pool assignment P [B,J,C]
   279	            coarse_mask,                # [B,C] bool — STRICT padded-slot-key mask (F1)
   280	            batch.joint_mask,
   281	            frame_mask_recovered,
   282	        )  # [B,T,J,D]
   283	
   284	        fm_b = frame_mask_recovered[:, :, None, None].to(feats.dtype)
   285	        jm_b = batch.joint_mask[:, None, :, None].to(feats.dtype)
   286	        out_root = self.anytop13_head["out_root"](feats[:, :, 0:1, :])      # [B,T,1,13]
   287	        out_nonroot = self.anytop13_head["out_nonroot"](feats[:, :, 1:, :])  # [B,T,J-1,13]
   288	        pred_motion = torch.cat([out_root, out_nonroot], dim=2)            # [B,T,J,13]
   289	        pred_motion = pred_motion * fm_b * jm_b
   290	        return {
   291	            "pred_motion": pred_motion,
   292	            "frame_mask_recovered": frame_mask_recovered,
   293	        }
   294	
   295	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   296	    # Graph-CodeFlow frozen-tokenizer utilities (ADDITIVE).               #
   297	    #                                                                     #
   298	    # These are READ-ONLY helpers for the post-RVQ Graph-CodeFlow         #
   299	    # backbone (handoff/20260609_graph_codeflow_rvq_backbone_plan.md §10).#
   300	    # They DO NOT touch encode/decode/quantizer forward behavior, never   #

   301	    # run EMA / dead-code reset / quantizer-dropout, and do all           #
   302	    # codebook-distance / argmin math in fp32 (bf16-safe). Padding        #
   303	    # contract identical to the quantizer: token_mask=False -> indices=-1,#
   304	    # z_q=0.                                                              #
   305	    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
   306	    @torch.no_grad()
   307	    def ids_to_embeddings(self, indices: torch.Tensor,
   308	                          token_mask: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
   309	        """RVQ indices [B,T_lat,C,Q] -> summed code embeddings z_q [B,T_lat,C,D].
   310	
   311	        Mirrors the quantizer's accumulation `q_total = sum_q embedding(codes_q,
   312	        codebook_q.embed)` (quantizer.py:365) and the final STE masking
   313	        (quantizer.py:416): a token's z_q is the SUM over the Q residual stages
   314	        of codebooks[q].embed[indices[...,q]], and padded tokens (token_mask
   315	        False, encoded as indices=-1) are exactly 0.
   316	
   317	        fp32 throughout (codebook embeds are fp32 buffers); returns fp32.
   318	        Per-stage: an index of -1 (padded token OR a dropped stage, though
   319	        export uses full depth) contributes 0 for that stage.
   320	        """
   321	        if indices.dim() != 4 or indices.shape[-1] != self.num_quantizers:
   322	            raise ValueError(
   323	                f"ids_to_embeddings: indices must be [B,T_lat,C,Q={self.num_quantizers}], "
   324	                f"got {tuple(indices.shape)}")
   325	        B, T_lat, C, Q = indices.shape
   326	        if token_mask.shape != (B, T_lat, C) or token_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   327	            raise ValueError(
   328	                f"ids_to_embeddings: token_mask must be [B,T_lat,C]={(B, T_lat, C)} bool, "
   329	                f"got {tuple(token_mask.shape)} dtype {token_mask.dtype}")
   330	        device = indices.device
   331	        D = self.d_model
   332	        z_q = torch.zeros(B, T_lat, C, D, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
   333	        for qi, cb in enumerate(self.quantizer.codebooks):
   334	            idx_q = indices[..., qi]                       # [B,T_lat,C] long, -1 = none
   335	            valid_q = idx_q >= 0                           # per-stage validity
   336	            # Gather embed for valid entries; clamp(min=0) keeps the gather index
   337	            # in-range for padded -1 slots (their contribution is masked to 0).
   338	            gathered = F.embedding(idx_q.clamp(min=0), cb.embed.float())  # [B,T_lat,C,D]
   339	            z_q = z_q + gathered * valid_q.unsqueeze(-1).to(z_q.dtype)
   340	        # Final STE-style mask: padded tokens are exactly 0 (defensive — per-stage
   341	        # -1 masking already zeroes a fully-padded token, but token_mask is the
   342	        # authoritative validity used by the quantizer's z_q masking).
   343	        z_q = z_q * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).to(z_q.dtype)
   344	        return z_q
   345	
   346	    @torch.no_grad()
   347	    def nearest_residual_ids(self, z_hat: torch.Tensor,
   348	                             token_mask: torch.Tensor) -> dict:
   349	        """Residual-nearest RVQ projection of a continuous latent z_hat
   350	        [B,T_lat,C,D] back onto the frozen codebooks.
   351	
   352	        Mirrors the quantizer's residual loop (quantizer.py:342-367) EXACTLY,
   353	        stage by stage, but with NO EMA update / NO dead-code reset / NO
   354	        quantizer dropout (this is inference-only projection):
   355	
   356	            r = z_hat
   357	            for q in range(Q):
   358	                idx_q = argmin_k || r - codebook_q[k] ||^2   (cb.quantize)
   359	                e_q   = codebook_q[idx_q]
   360	                r     = r - e_q
   361	            z_snap = sum_q e_q           (== ids_to_embeddings(indices_hat))
   362	
   363	        Returns dict:
   364	          indices_hat       [B,T_lat,C,Q] long  (-1 on padded tokens)
   365	          z_snap            [B,T_lat,C,D] fp32   (0 on padded tokens)
   366	          projection_error  scalar fp32  = mean over VALID tokens * D of
   367	                                           (z_hat - z_snap)^2
   368	
   369	        All math in fp32 (bf16-safe). z_hat is cast to fp32 internally.
   370	        """
   371	        if z_hat.dim() != 4 or z_hat.shape[-1] != self.d_model:
   372	            raise ValueError(
   373	                f"nearest_residual_ids: z_hat must be [B,T_lat,C,D={self.d_model}], "
   374	                f"got {tuple(z_hat.shape)}")
   375	        B, T_lat, C, D = z_hat.shape
   376	        if token_mask.shape != (B, T_lat, C) or token_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   377	            raise ValueError(
   378	                f"nearest_residual_ids: token_mask must be [B,T_lat,C]={(B, T_lat, C)} "
   379	                f"bool, got {tuple(token_mask.shape)} dtype {token_mask.dtype}")
   380	        device = z_hat.device
   381	        Q = self.num_quantizers
   382	        z_flat = z_hat.reshape(-1, D).float()              # [N,D] fp32
   383	        valid_flat = token_mask.reshape(-1)                # [N] bool
   384	        N = z_flat.shape[0]
   385	
   386	        residual = z_flat
   387	        z_snap_flat = torch.zeros_like(z_flat)             # accumulated quantized
   388	        indices_stages: list[torch.Tensor] = []
   389	        for cb in self.quantizer.codebooks:
   390	            codes, q = cb.quantize(residual)               # [N], [N,D] (fp32 argmin)
   391	            # Per-stage indices: -1 on padded tokens (mirrors quantizer.py:346-348).
   392	            stage_idx = torch.full((N,), -1, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
   393	            stage_idx[valid_flat] = codes[valid_flat]
   394	            indices_stages.append(stage_idx.reshape(B, T_lat, C))
   395	            # Accumulate / recurse on the SAME math as the quantizer (it leaves
   396	            # padded residuals in place; we zero padded z_snap at the end).
   397	            z_snap_flat = z_snap_flat + q
   398	            residual = residual - q
   399	        # Final masking: padded tokens -> z_snap exactly 0 (STE convention).
   400	        z_snap_flat = z_snap_flat * valid_flat.unsqueeze(-1).to(z_snap_flat.dtype)
   401	
   402	        indices_hat = torch.stack(indices_stages, dim=-1)  # [B,T_lat,C,Q]
   403	        z_snap = z_snap_flat.reshape(B, T_lat, C, D)
   404	        # projection_error = masked MSE over valid tokens * D.
   405	        n_valid = int(valid_flat.sum().item())
   406	        diff_sq = (z_flat - z_snap_flat).pow(2)            # [N,D] (padded rows now 0-0=0)
   407	        proj_err = diff_sq[valid_flat].sum() / (max(n_valid, 1) * D)
   408	        return {
   409	            "indices_hat": indices_hat,
   410	            "z_snap": z_snap,
   411	            "projection_error": proj_err,
   412	        }
   413	
   414	    @torch.no_grad()
   415	    def prepare_skeleton_only(self, batch, T_lat: int) -> dict:
   416	        """Motion-INDEPENDENT coarse-graph metadata for CodeFlow inference.
   417	
   418	        Computes everything the decoder + CodeFlow conditioning need WITHOUT
   419	        consuming motion frames (motion is the unknown being generated). Mirrors
   420	        GraphMotionVAE.encode_skeleton_only (vae.py:518): runs encoder.encode_
   421	        skeleton + pool.compute_assignment_and_graph (both motion-independent),
   422	        then synthesizes an all-True frame_mask_lat of length T_lat (every latent
   423	        frame is generated) and the matching token_mask.
   424	
   425	        Returns a schema-aligned subset of encode()'s keys (so decode() / the
   426	        CodeFlow backbone can consume it directly):
   427	          s_j, assignment, coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat, token_mask,
   428	          pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic, pooled_skeleton_embeddings

   430	        Caller owns the freeze contract (tokenizer.eval() + torch.no_grad()).

   432	        if batch.anytop_graph_dist is None or batch.anytop_joint_relations is None:
   433	            raise ValueError("prepare_skeleton_only (graphormer) requires "
   434	                             "batch.anytop_graph_dist + batch.anytop_joint_relations")
   435	        if T_lat <= 0:
   436	            raise ValueError(f"prepare_skeleton_only: T_lat must be > 0, got {T_lat}")
   437	        gd, jr = batch.anytop_graph_dist, batch.anytop_joint_relations
   438	        s_j = self.encoder.encode_skeleton(

   450	        coarse_mask = geom["pooled_mask"]                  # [B,C] bool
   451	        B, C = coarse_mask.shape
   452	        device = coarse_mask.device
   453	        # Every latent frame is to be generated -> all-True frame_mask_lat.
   454	        frame_mask_lat = torch.ones(B, T_lat, dtype=torch.bool, device=device)
   455	        token_mask = coarse_mask.unsqueeze(1) & frame_mask_lat.unsqueeze(-1)  # [B,T_lat,C]
   456	        return {
   457	            "s_j": s_j,
   458	            "assignment": geom["assignment"],
   459	            "coarse_mask": coarse_mask,
   460	            "frame_mask_lat": frame_mask_lat,
   461	            "token_mask": token_mask,
   462	            "pooled_adjacency": geom["pooled_adjacency"],
   463	            "pooled_geodesic": geom["pooled_geodesic"],
   464	            "pooled_skeleton_embeddings": geom["pooled_skeleton_embeddings"],
   465	        }
   466	
   467	    @torch.no_grad()
   468	    def decode_from_indices(self, indices: torch.Tensor, skeleton_meta: dict,
   469	                            batch) -> dict:
   470	        """RVQ indices [B,T_lat,C,Q] -> pred_motion [B,T,J,13] via the frozen
   471	        decoder.
   472	
   473	        Convenience glue (plan §8 step 6): ids_to_embeddings(indices) -> decode.
   474	        `skeleton_meta` must carry the decode metadata (from encode() OR
   475	        prepare_skeleton_only()): coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat, pooled_adjacency,
   476	        pooled_geodesic, s_j, assignment. Delegates to self.decode (post-VQ
   477	        refine + temporal upsample + masked decoder + anytop13 heads), unchanged.
   478	        """
   479	        token_mask = skeleton_meta["coarse_mask"].unsqueeze(1) \
   480	            & skeleton_meta["frame_mask_lat"].unsqueeze(-1)
   481	        z_q = self.ids_to_embeddings(indices, token_mask)  # [B,T_lat,C,D] fp32
   482	        # decode() reads the autocast dtype from z_q; keep fp32 (decoder casts
   483	        # graph tensors to fp32 internally and accepts fp32 features).

   486	    def forward(self, batch, allow_collectives: bool = True) -> dict:
   487	        """allow_collectives: True on the all-ranks DDP training step (the
   488	        quantizer's EMA all_reduce / dead-code + dropout broadcasts fire across
   489	        ranks — amendment 3). Pass False on a rank-0-only eval pass so the
   490	        quantizer does not block on collectives the other ranks skip."""

   492	        vq = self.quantizer(enc["h_lat"], enc["token_mask"],
   493	                            allow_collectives=allow_collectives)
   494	        dec = self.decode(vq["quantized"], enc, batch)

   498	            "frame_mask_recovered": dec["frame_mask_recovered"],

   507	            "dead_codes": vq["dead_codes"],

   510	            "frame_mask_lat": enc["frame_mask_lat"],
   511	            "token_mask": enc["token_mask"],
   512	            "assignment": enc["assignment"],
   513	        }

   126	        new_vecs = torch.zeros(n_dead, D, dtype=stage_input.dtype, device=stage_input.device)
   127	
   128	        # Only the sampling source (rank 0 under DDP, else this rank) populates
   129	        # new_vecs from its valid tokens; if it has none, leave zeros and skip the
   130	        # in-place overwrite below so a code is never replaced by a padded zero.
   131	        has_valid_source = False

   133	            src = stage_input[valid_mask]   # [M, D] valid tokens on the source rank
   134	            M = src.shape[0]
   135	            if M > 0:
   136	                idx = torch.randint(0, M, (n_dead,), device=src.device)
   137	                new_vecs = src[idx]
   138	                has_valid_source = True

   140	        if ddp:

   142	            # the source-has-valid flag and the sampled vectors from rank 0.
   143	            flag = torch.tensor([1 if has_valid_source else 0],
   144	                                device=stage_input.device)

   146	            has_valid_source = bool(int(flag.item()))
   147	            dist.broadcast(new_vecs, src=0)

   151	            # with zeros; report 0 resets.
   152	            return 0
