 succeeded in 0ms:
   140	      - strict re-mask both streams.
   141	
   142	    fp32 softmax (bf16-safe). No graph metadata is touched here.
   143	    """
   144	
   145	    def __init__(self, code_dim: int, n_heads: int, d_ff: int, dropout: float) -> None:
   146	        super().__init__()
   147	        if code_dim % n_heads != 0:
   148	            raise ValueError(
   149	                f"code_dim ({code_dim}) must be divisible by n_heads ({n_heads})")
   150	        self.n_heads = n_heads
   151	        self.d_head = code_dim // n_heads
   152	
   153	        # --- holder-reads-slots cross-attention (manual, masked, fp32 softmax) ---
   154	        self.norm_q = nn.LayerNorm(code_dim)
   155	        self.norm_kv = nn.LayerNorm(code_dim)
   156	        self.q_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   157	        self.k_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   158	        self.v_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   159	        self.o_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   160	        # zero-init output -> the holder-read is a no-op residual at init (stable).
   161	        nn.init.zeros_(self.o_proj.weight)
   162	        nn.init.zeros_(self.o_proj.bias)
   163	        self.dropout = nn.Dropout(dropout)
   164	        self.film_frame = DenseFiLM(code_dim, code_dim)
   165	
   166	        # --- slots-receive-frame additive injection (FiLM-gated) ---
   167	        self.norm_inject = nn.LayerNorm(code_dim)
   168	        self.inject_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   169	        # zero-init injection -> slots receive nothing at init (no-op residual).
   170	        nn.init.zeros_(self.inject_proj.weight)
   171	        nn.init.zeros_(self.inject_proj.bias)
   172	        self.film_slot = DenseFiLM(code_dim, code_dim)
   173	
   174	    def forward(
   175	        self,
   176	        h_frame: torch.Tensor,        # [B, T, D]
   177	        h_slot: torch.Tensor,         # [B, T, C, D]
   178	        cond: torch.Tensor,           # [B, D]
   179	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,    # [B, C] bool, True = valid
   180	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,  # [B, T] bool, True = valid
   181	    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   182	        B, T, C, D = h_slot.shape
   183	
   184	        # ---- 1. holder reads slots: per-frame [B*T,1,D] q over [B*T,C,D] kv ----
   185	        q_in = self.norm_q(h_frame).reshape(B * T, 1, D)         # [B*T,1,D]
   186	        kv_in = self.norm_kv(h_slot).reshape(B * T, C, D)        # [B*T,C,D]
   187	        q = self.q_proj(q_in).view(B * T, 1, self.n_heads, self.d_head).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
   188	        k = self.k_proj(kv_in).view(B * T, C, self.n_heads, self.d_head).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
   189	        v = self.v_proj(kv_in).view(B * T, C, self.n_heads, self.d_head).permute(0, 2, 1, 3)
   190	        scores = torch.matmul(q, k.transpose(-2, -1)) / math.sqrt(self.d_head)  # [B*T,H,1,C]
   191	        # key_padding_mask = ~coarse_mask expanded over T (True = ignore).
   192	        kpm = (~coarse_mask)[:, None, :].expand(B, T, C).reshape(B * T, C)       # [B*T,C]
   193	        scores = scores.masked_fill(kpm[:, None, None, :], -1e9)
   194	        # fp32 softmax (bf16-safe; fp32 path is a no-op). all-padded rows cannot
   195	        # occur (>=1 valid slot per sample guaranteed by coarse_mask), but
   196	        # nan_to_num guards defensively.
   197	        attn = F.softmax(scores.float(), dim=-1).to(scores.dtype)
   198	        attn = attn.nan_to_num(0.0)
   199	        attn = self.dropout(attn)
   200	        out = torch.matmul(attn, v)                                # [B*T,H,1,dh]
   201	        out = out.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().view(B * T, 1, D)
   202	        out = self.o_proj(out).reshape(B, T, D)
   203	        h_frame = h_frame + self.dropout(out)
   204	        h_frame = _film_frame(self.film_frame, h_frame, cond)
   205	        # re-mask frame stream (padded latent frames -> 0).
   206	        h_frame = h_frame * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None].to(h_frame.dtype)
   207	
   208	        # ---- 2. slots receive frame: FiLM-gated additive injection ----
   209	        inj = self.inject_proj(self.norm_inject(h_frame))          # [B,T,D]
   210	        # broadcast the per-frame holder context to all C slots, then FiLM-gate.
   211	        inj = inj[:, :, None, :].expand(B, T, C, D)                # [B,T,C,D]
   212	        inj = self.film_slot(inj, cond)
   213	        h_slot = h_slot + inj
   214	
   215	        # ---- 3. strict re-mask both streams ----
   216	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(h_slot.dtype)
   217	        fm4 = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(h_slot.dtype)
   218	        h_slot = h_slot * cm * fm4
   219	        h_frame = h_frame * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None].to(h_frame.dtype)
   220	        return h_frame, h_slot
   221	
   222	
   223	class GraphPSCFFlowNet(nn.Module):
   224	    """Graph-aware PSCF / FLUX-style rectified-flow velocity network over post-RVQ z_q.
   225	
   226	    6 double-stream + 12 single-stream DiT blocks (depth_double / depth_single)
   227	    with a graph-aware slot stream (GraphSlotTemporalBlock) bridged to the frame /
   228	    text DiT by GraphFrameSlotCoupling (NON-GRAPH, Q1). Conditioning is two-path
   229	    text (Q2): pooled_text -> cond (AdaLN/FiLM, CFG-gated); token_text -> h_text
   230	    stream. h_frame is persistent (Q4): seeded once and updated in place by every
   231	    double / single / coupling.
   232	
   233	    forward signature is the EXACT 11-positional-arg contract of
   234	    GraphStructuredCodeFlow.forward (graph_codeflow.py:194-209) so flow.py /
   235	    predict_velocity / CFG stay drop-in.
   236	    """
   237	
   238	    def __init__(
   239	        self,
   240	        code_dim: int = 512,
   241	        n_heads: int = 8,
   242	        d_ff: int = 2048,
   243	        depth_double: int = 6,
   244	        depth_single: int = 12,
   245	        d_text: int = 768,
   246	        text_token_dim: int = 768,
   247	        max_T_lat: int = 75,
   248	        mlp_ratio: float = 4.0,
   249	        dropout: float = 0.05,
   250	    ) -> None:
   251	        super().__init__()
   252	        if code_dim % n_heads != 0:
   253	            raise ValueError(
   254	                f"code_dim ({code_dim}) must be divisible by n_heads ({n_heads})")
   255	        if depth_double <= 0 or depth_single <= 0:
   256	            raise ValueError(
   257	                f"depth_double / depth_single must be > 0, got "
   258	                f"{depth_double} / {depth_single}")
   259	        self.code_dim = code_dim
   260	        self.d_model = code_dim

 succeeded in 0ms:
   260	        self.d_model = code_dim
   261	        self.n_heads = n_heads
   262	        self.head_dim = code_dim // n_heads

   264	        self.depth_double = depth_double
   265	        self.depth_single = depth_single
   266	        self.d_text = d_text
   267	        self.text_token_dim = text_token_dim
   268	        self.max_T_lat = max_T_lat
   269	        self.mlp_ratio = mlp_ratio
   270	        # RoPE over the single frame axis: one axis spanning the full head_dim.
   271	        self.rope_axes_dims = [self.head_dim]
   272	
   273	        # ---- timestep embedding (sinusoidal -> MLP) ----
   274	        self.t_sin = SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding(code_dim)
   275	        self.t_mlp = nn.Sequential(
   276	            nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim * 4), nn.SiLU(),

   300	                "coupling_pre": GraphFrameSlotCoupling(code_dim, n_heads, d_ff, dropout),
   301	                "dit": DoubleStreamBlock(code_dim, n_heads, mlp_ratio, dropout),
   302	                "coupling_post": GraphFrameSlotCoupling(code_dim, n_heads, d_ff, dropout),
   303	            })
   304	            for _ in range(depth_double)
   305	        ])
   306	
   307	        # ---- single-stream blocks ----

   309	            nn.ModuleDict({

   311	                "slot_temporal": GraphSlotTemporalBlock(code_dim, n_heads, d_ff, dropout),
   312	                "coupling": GraphFrameSlotCoupling(code_dim, n_heads, d_ff, dropout),
   313	            })

   315	        ])
   316

   318	        self.output_norm = nn.LayerNorm(code_dim)

   320	        nn.init.zeros_(self.output_proj.weight)
   321	        nn.init.zeros_(self.output_proj.bias)
   322	
   323	    @staticmethod
   324	    def _build_sinusoidal_pos(max_len: int, dim: int) -> torch.Tensor:
   325	        """Standard [max_len, dim] sinusoidal table (additive frame position)."""
   326	        if dim % 2 != 0:
   327	            raise ValueError(f"dim must be even, got {dim}")
   328	        pos = torch.arange(max_len, dtype=torch.float32).unsqueeze(1)      # [L,1]
   329	        div = torch.exp(
   330	            torch.arange(0, dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) * (-math.log(10000.0) / dim))
   331	        pe = torch.zeros(max_len, dim, dtype=torch.float32)
   332	        pe[:, 0::2] = torch.sin(pos * div)
   333	        pe[:, 1::2] = torch.cos(pos * div)
   334	        return pe
   335	
   336	    def forward(
   337	        self,
   338	        z_t: torch.Tensor,                       # [B, T_lat, C, D]
   339	        timesteps: torch.Tensor,                 # [B] (float in [0,1])
   340	        text_global: torch.Tensor,               # [B, 768] mean caption
   341	        text_tokens: torch.Tensor,               # [B, L, 768] token caption
   342	        text_token_mask: torch.Tensor,           # [B, L] bool, True = valid token
   343	        has_text: torch.Tensor,                  # [B] bool (CFG gate)
   344	        pooled_adjacency: torch.Tensor,          # [B, C, C]
   345	        pooled_geodesic: torch.Tensor,           # [B, C, C]
   346	        pooled_skeleton_embeddings: torch.Tensor,  # [B, C, D]
   347	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,               # [B, C] bool
   348	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,            # [B, T_lat] bool
   349	        *,
   350	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
   351	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   352	        if z_t.dim() != 4 or z_t.shape[-1] != self.code_dim:
   353	            raise ValueError(
   354	                f"z_t must be [B,T_lat,C,D={self.code_dim}], got {tuple(z_t.shape)}")
   355	        B, T_lat, C, D = z_t.shape
   356	        ref_device = z_t.device
   357	        # ---- contract checks (mirror GraphStructuredCodeFlow; fail-loud) ----
   358	        if timesteps.shape != (B,):
   359	            raise ValueError(f"timesteps must be [B={B}], got {tuple(timesteps.shape)}")
   360	        if coarse_mask.shape != (B, C) or coarse_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   361	            raise ValueError(
   362	                f"coarse_mask must be [B={B},C={C}] bool, got "
   363	                f"{tuple(coarse_mask.shape)} {coarse_mask.dtype}")
   364	        if frame_mask_lat.shape != (B, T_lat) or frame_mask_lat.dtype != torch.bool:
   365	            raise ValueError(
   366	                f"frame_mask_lat must be [B={B},T_lat={T_lat}] bool, got "
   367	                f"{tuple(frame_mask_lat.shape)} {frame_mask_lat.dtype}")
   368	        if has_text.shape != (B,) or has_text.dtype != torch.bool:
   369	            raise ValueError(
   370	                f"has_text must be [B={B}] bool, got {tuple(has_text.shape)} "
   371	                f"{has_text.dtype}")
   372	        if pooled_adjacency.shape != (B, C, C) or pooled_geodesic.shape != (B, C, C):
   373	            raise ValueError(
   374	                f"pooled_adjacency/geodesic must be [B={B},C={C},C={C}], got "
   375	                f"{tuple(pooled_adjacency.shape)} / {tuple(pooled_geodesic.shape)}")
   376	        if pooled_skeleton_embeddings.shape != (B, C, D):
   377	            raise ValueError(
   378	                f"pooled_skeleton_embeddings must be [B={B},C={C},D={D}], got "
   379	                f"{tuple(pooled_skeleton_embeddings.shape)}")
   380	        if text_global.dim() != 2 or text_global.shape != (B, self.d_text):
   381	            raise ValueError(
   382	                f"text_global must be [B={B},{self.d_text}], got {tuple(text_global.shape)}")
   383	        if (text_tokens.dim() != 3 or text_tokens.shape[0] != B
   384	                or text_tokens.shape[2] != self.text_token_dim):
   385	            raise ValueError(
   386	                f"text_tokens must be [B={B},L,{self.text_token_dim}], got "
   387	                f"{tuple(text_tokens.shape)}")
   388	        L = text_tokens.shape[1]
   389	        if text_token_mask.shape != (B, L) or text_token_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   390	            raise ValueError(
   391	                f"text_token_mask must be [B={B},L={L}] bool, got "
   392	                f"{tuple(text_token_mask.shape)} {text_token_mask.dtype}")
   393	        if T_lat > self.max_T_lat:
   394	            raise ValueError(
   395	                f"T_lat={T_lat} exceeds max_T_lat={self.max_T_lat} (frame_seed / "
   396	                f"time_pos_embedding cannot cover it)")
   397	        for name, t in (
   398	            ("timesteps", timesteps), ("coarse_mask", coarse_mask),
   399	            ("frame_mask_lat", frame_mask_lat), ("has_text", has_text),
   400	            ("pooled_adjacency", pooled_adjacency), ("pooled_geodesic", pooled_geodesic),
   401	            ("pooled_skeleton_embeddings", pooled_skeleton_embeddings),
   402	            ("text_global", text_global), ("text_tokens", text_tokens),
   403	            ("text_token_mask", text_token_mask),
   404	        ):
   405	            if t.device != ref_device:
   406	                raise ValueError(
   407	                    f"GraphPSCFFlowNet: {name}.device {t.device} != "
   408	                    f"z_t.device {ref_device}")
   409	        # Float conditioning tensors must match z_t.dtype on the fp32 path (same
   410	        # contract as GraphStructuredCodeFlow / GraphAttentionBlock).
   411	        if z_t.dtype in (torch.float32, torch.float64):
   412	            for name, t in (
   413	                ("pooled_adjacency", pooled_adjacency),
   414	                ("pooled_geodesic", pooled_geodesic),
   415	                ("pooled_skeleton_embeddings", pooled_skeleton_embeddings),
   416	                ("text_global", text_global), ("text_tokens", text_tokens),
   417	            ):
   418	                if t.dtype != z_t.dtype:
   419	                    raise ValueError(
   420	                        f"GraphPSCFFlowNet: {name}.dtype {t.dtype} != "
   421	                        f"z_t.dtype {z_t.dtype}")
   422	
   423	        dtype = z_t.dtype
   424	
   425	        # ---- conditioning (Q2): cond = timestep_emb + pooled_text (CFG-gated) ----
   426	        t_emb = self.t_mlp(self.t_sin(timesteps)).to(dtype)        # [B, D]
   427	        text_pooled = self.text_pooled_proj(text_global)           # [B, D]
   428	        text_pooled = text_pooled * has_text[:, None].to(dtype)    # CFG gate
   429	        cond = t_emb + text_pooled                                 # [B, D]
   430	
   431	        # ---- slot stream: input_proj(z_t) + pooled skeleton (I2) ----
   432	        h_slot = self.input_proj(z_t)
   433	        h_slot = h_slot + pooled_skeleton_embeddings.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, T_lat, -1, -1)
   434	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(dtype)
   435	        fm4 = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(dtype)
   436	        h_slot = h_slot * cm * fm4
   437	
   438	        # ---- frame stream (Q4): persistent seed + sinusoidal frame pos ----
   439	        h_frame = self.frame_seed[:, :T_lat, :].expand(B, T_lat, D).to(dtype)
   440	        time_pos = self.time_pos_embedding[:T_lat].to(device=ref_device, dtype=dtype)
   441	        h_frame = h_frame + time_pos[None, :, :]
   442	        h_frame = h_frame * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   443	
   444	        # ---- text stream: token projection (I3: True = valid) ----
   445	        h_text = self.text_token_proj(text_tokens)                 # [B, L, D]
   446	        text_valid = text_token_mask & has_text[:, None]           # [B, L] True = valid
   447	        h_text = h_text * text_valid[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   448	
   449	        # ---- frame motion pos_ids over T_lat: [B,T_lat,1] (single RoPE axis) ----
   450	        frame_pos = torch.arange(T_lat, device=ref_device, dtype=torch.long)
   451	        motion_pos_ids = frame_pos[None, :, None].expand(B, T_lat, 1)  # [B,T_lat,1]
   452	
   453	        # ============================ DOUBLE STAGE ============================
   454	        # per block (plan §4.5):
   455	        #   h_slot = slot_temporal(h_slot)
   456	        #   (h_frame,h_slot) = coupling_pre(h_frame,h_slot)
   457	        #   (h_frame,h_text) = DoubleStreamBlock(h_frame,h_text,cond)
   458	        #   (h_frame,h_slot) = coupling_post(h_frame,h_slot)
   459	        #   strict mask all
   460	        for blk in self.double_blocks:
   461	            h_slot = blk["slot_temporal"](
   462	                h_slot, cond, pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic,
   463	                coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat, validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   464	            h_frame, h_slot = blk["coupling_pre"](
   465	                h_frame, h_slot, cond, coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat)
   466	            # DiT boundary: ported DoubleStreamBlock takes True=valid directly.
   467	            h_frame, h_text = blk["dit"](
   468	                h_frame, h_text, cond,
   469	                frame_mask_lat, text_valid,
   470	                motion_pos_ids, self.rope_axes_dims)
   471	            # ported DiT only key-masks; re-mask the padded frame/text rows.
   472	            h_frame = h_frame * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   473	            h_text = h_text * text_valid[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   474	            h_frame, h_slot = blk["coupling_post"](
   475	                h_frame, h_slot, cond, coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat)
   476	            # strict mask all
   477	            h_slot = h_slot * cm * fm4
   478	            h_frame = h_frame * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   479	            h_text = h_text * text_valid[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   480	
   481	        # ============================ SINGLE STAGE ===========================
   482	        # text PERSISTS across single blocks (mirrors FrameMotionTextDiT). per
   483	        # block (plan §4.6):
   484	        #   joint = concat(h_frame, h_text)
   485	        #   joint = SingleStreamBlock(joint, cond, valid, joint_pos)
   486	        #   split -> h_frame, h_text
   487	        #   h_slot = slot_temporal(h_slot)
   488	        #   (h_frame,h_slot) = coupling(h_frame,h_slot)
   489	        #   strict mask
   490	        text_pos = torch.zeros(B, L, 1, device=ref_device, dtype=torch.long)
   491	        joint_pos = torch.cat([motion_pos_ids, text_pos], dim=1)   # [B,T_lat+L,1]
   492	        joint_valid = torch.cat([frame_mask_lat, text_valid], dim=1)  # [B,T_lat+L]
   493	        for blk in self.single_blocks:
   494	            joint = torch.cat([h_frame, h_text], dim=1)            # [B,T_lat+L,D]
   495	            joint = blk["dit"](
   496	                joint, cond, joint_valid, joint_pos, self.rope_axes_dims)
   497	            h_frame, h_text = joint.split([T_lat, L], dim=1)
   498	            # ported DiT only key-masks; re-mask padded frame/text rows.
   499	            h_frame = h_frame * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   500	            h_text = h_text * text_valid[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   501	            h_slot = blk["slot_temporal"](
   502	                h_slot, cond, pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic,
   503	                coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat, validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   504	            h_frame, h_slot = blk["coupling"](
   505	                h_frame, h_slot, cond, coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat)
   506	            # strict mask
   507	            h_slot = h_slot * cm * fm4
   508	            h_frame = h_frame * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   509	            h_text = h_text * text_valid[:, :, None].to(dtype)
   510	
   511	        # ---- output: pre-norm + zero-init linear + final strict re-mask ----
   512	        v_pred = self.output_proj(self.output_norm(h_slot))
   513	        return v_pred * cm * fm4

     9	and does NOT touch the Gaussian VAE / latent diffusion / Graph-VQVAE training /
    10	graph_salad behavior.
    11	
    12	Three internal streams (plan §3):
    13	  slot stream:  h_slot  [B, T_lat, C, D]   graph-pooled RVQ latent grid
    14	  frame stream: h_frame [B, T_lat, D]      graph-aware frame holder tokens (persistent, seeded once — Q4)
    15	  text stream:  h_text  [B, L, D]          T5 token stream
    16

    18	  - pooled_text -> cond (= timestep_emb + pooled_text, CFG-gated) -> every AdaLN/FiLM
    19	  - token_text  -> h_text stream -> double/single DiT joint attention
    20	
    21	The three new modules:
    22	  - GraphSlotTemporalBlock   keeps the slot stream graph-aware (GraphAttentionBlock
    23	                              over C with pooled adjacency/geodesic) + temporal
    24	                              (TemporalSelfAttention over T_lat), with DenseFiLM
    25	                              modulation. Mirrors graph_codeflow.py:94-122.
    26	  - GraphFrameSlotCoupling   NON-GRAPH (Q1) bridge: the frame holder reads slots
    27	                              via masked cross-attention; slots receive the updated
    28	                              frame via a FiLM-gated additive injection. Does NOT
    29	                              build a [1+C] extended graph (Floyd hub collapse).
    30	  - GraphPSCFFlowNet         6 double-stream + 12 single-stream stack with graph

    58	def _film_frame(film: DenseFiLM, h_frame: torch.Tensor, cond: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    59	    """Apply a 4D DenseFiLM to a [B,T,D] frame stream by treating it as a single
    60	    coarse-slot grid [B,T,1,D]. DenseFiLM broadcasts (scale,shift) as [B,1,1,D],
    61	    so on [B,T,1,D] this is the identical affine the slot stream gets — we reuse
    62	    the exact audited DenseFiLM rather than introducing a parallel 3D variant."""
    63	    return film(h_frame.unsqueeze(2), cond).squeeze(2)
    64	
    65	
    66	class GraphSlotTemporalBlock(nn.Module):
    67	    """Graph-spatial (over C) + temporal (over T_lat) update of the slot stream.
    68	
    69	    Mirrors graph_codeflow.py:94-109 EXACTLY for the reshape / expand / mask
    70	    patterns:
    71	      1. graph-spatial: reshape [B*T,C,D], expand adj/geo/coarse_mask over T,
    72	         GraphAttentionBlock(..., validate_inputs), reshape back, FiLM(cond),
    73	         re-mask by coarse_mask.
    74	      2. temporal: permute to [B*C,T,D], expand frame_mask over C,
    75	         TemporalSelfAttention, permute back, FiLM(cond).
    76	      3. strict re-mask by coarse_mask AND frame_mask_lat.
    77	    """
    78	
    79	    def __init__(self, code_dim: int, n_heads: int, d_ff: int, dropout: float) -> None:
    80	        super().__init__()
    81	        self.spatial = GraphAttentionBlock(code_dim, n_heads, d_ff, dropout=dropout)
    82	        self.temporal = TemporalSelfAttention(code_dim, n_heads, dropout=dropout)
    83	        self.film_after_spatial = DenseFiLM(code_dim, code_dim)
    84	        self.film_after_temporal = DenseFiLM(code_dim, code_dim)
    85	
    86	    def forward(
    87	        self,
    88	        h_slot: torch.Tensor,         # [B, T, C, D]
    89	        cond: torch.Tensor,           # [B, D]
    90	        pooled_adj: torch.Tensor,     # [B, C, C] fp32
    91	        pooled_geo: torch.Tensor,     # [B, C, C] fp32
    92	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,    # [B, C] bool
    93	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,  # [B, T] bool
    94	        *,
    95	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
    96	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
    97	        B, T, C, D = h_slot.shape
    98	
    99	        # --- 1. Graph-spatial self-attn (per latent frame, over C slots) ---
   100	        x_sp_in = h_slot.reshape(B * T, C, D)
   101	        adj_exp = pooled_adj.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T, C, C).reshape(B * T, C, C)
   102	        geo_exp = pooled_geo.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T, C, C).reshape(B * T, C, C)
   103	        cm_exp = coarse_mask.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T, C).reshape(B * T, C)
   104	        x_sp = self.spatial(x_sp_in, adj_exp, geo_exp, cm_exp,
   105	                            validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   106	        h_slot = x_sp.reshape(B, T, C, D)
   107	        h_slot = self.film_after_spatial(h_slot, cond)
   108	        # re-mask by coarse_mask AND frame_mask_lat after the spatial sub-block:
   109	        # padded query slots/frames still attend valid keys -> non-zero output, and
   110	        # I7 strict padded-zero requires clearing BOTH after every sub-block (else
   111	        # padded frame rows leak as non-zero queries into the temporal sub-block).
   112	        h_slot = (h_slot * coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(h_slot.dtype)
   113	                  * frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(h_slot.dtype))
   114	
   115	        # --- 2. Temporal self-attn (per slot, over T_lat frames) ---
   116	        x_t_in = h_slot.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().reshape(B * C, T, D)
   117	        fm_exp = frame_mask_lat.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, C, T).reshape(B * C, T)
   118	        x_t = self.temporal(x_t_in, fm_exp)
   119	        h_slot = x_t.reshape(B, C, T, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
   120	        h_slot = self.film_after_temporal(h_slot, cond)
   121	
   122	        # --- 3. Strict padded re-mask (coarse_mask AND frame_mask_lat) ---
   123	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(h_slot.dtype)
   124	        fm = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(h_slot.dtype)
   125	        return h_slot * cm * fm
   126	
   127	
   128	class GraphFrameSlotCoupling(nn.Module):
   129	    """NON-GRAPH (Q1) bridge between the per-frame holder and the C graph slots.
   130	
   131	    Per Q1 the holder is NOT inserted into the GraphAttentionBlock adjacency /
   132	    geodesic (a holder hub collapses slot-slot geodesics to <=2 hops and trips the
   133	    Floyd strict validation). Instead:
   134	
   135	      - holder reads slots: per latent frame, h_frame[:,t] (1 query) cross-attends
   136	        over h_slot[:,t] (C keys), keys masked by coarse_mask (True=valid). FiLM,
   137	        residual.
   138	      - slots receive frame: a FiLM-gated additive injection of a Linear-projected
   139	        h_frame broadcast over the C slots. residual.
   140	      - strict re-mask both streams.

codex
Graph-PSCF also matches the three-stream account. The important nuance is that frame-slot coupling is explicitly non-graph and FiLM-gated/zero-initialized; the doc says “broadcast/add,” which is broadly right but loses that safety detail.

   240	
   241	class DoubleStreamBlock(nn.Module):
   242	    """Joint text-motion attention with separate stream updates."""
   243	
   244	    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, num_heads: int, mlp_ratio: float, dropout: float) -> None:
   245	        super().__init__()
   246	        mlp_hidden = int(hidden_size * mlp_ratio)
   247	        self.motion_mod = AdaLNModulation(hidden_size, num=2)
   248	        self.text_mod = AdaLNModulation(hidden_size, num=2)
   249	        self.motion_norm = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_size, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
   250	        self.text_norm = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_size, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
   251	        self.joint_attn = MultiHeadAttention(hidden_size, num_heads, dropout)
   252	        self.motion_ffn_norm = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_size, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
   253	        self.text_ffn_norm = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_size, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
   254	        self.motion_ffn = nn.Sequential(SwiGLU(hidden_size, mlp_hidden), nn.Dropout(dropout))
   255	        self.text_ffn = nn.Sequential(SwiGLU(hidden_size, mlp_hidden), nn.Dropout(dropout))
   256	
   257	    def forward(
   258	        self,
   259	        motion: torch.Tensor,
   260	        text: torch.Tensor,
   261	        cond: torch.Tensor,
   262	        motion_valid: torch.Tensor,
   263	        text_valid: torch.Tensor,
   264	        pos_ids: torch.Tensor,
   265	        rope_axes_dims: List[int],
   266	    ) -> Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
   267	        m_attn, m_ff = self.motion_mod(cond)
   268	        t_attn, t_ff = self.text_mod(cond)
   269	
   270	        motion_in = self.motion_norm(motion)
   271	        motion_in = (1 + m_attn.scale) * motion_in + m_attn.shift
   272	        text_in = self.text_norm(text)
   273	        text_in = (1 + t_attn.scale) * text_in + t_attn.shift
   274	
   275	        joint = torch.cat([motion_in, text_in], dim=1)
   276	        joint_valid = torch.cat([motion_valid, text_valid], dim=1)
   277	        text_pos = torch.zeros(
   278	            text.shape[0], text.shape[1], pos_ids.shape[-1],
   279	            device=pos_ids.device,
   280	            dtype=pos_ids.dtype,
   281	        )
   282	        joint_pos = torch.cat([pos_ids, text_pos], dim=1)
   283	        joint_out = self.joint_attn(
   284	            joint,
   285	            joint,
   286	            key_valid=joint_valid,
   287	            query_valid=joint_valid,
   288	            query_pos=joint_pos,
   289	            memory_pos=joint_pos,
   290	            rope_axes_dims=rope_axes_dims,
   291	        )
   292	        motion_out, text_out = joint_out.split([motion.shape[1], text.shape[1]], dim=1)
   293	        motion = motion + m_attn.gate * motion_out
   294	        text = text + t_attn.gate * text_out
   295	
   296	        motion_ff = self.motion_ffn_norm(motion)
   297	        motion_ff = (1 + m_ff.scale) * motion_ff + m_ff.shift
   298	        motion = motion + m_ff.gate * self.motion_ffn(motion_ff)
   299	
   300	        text_ff = self.text_ffn_norm(text)
   301	        text_ff = (1 + t_ff.scale) * text_ff + t_ff.shift
   302	        text = text + t_ff.gate * self.text_ffn(text_ff)
   303	        return motion, text
   304	
   305	
   306	class SingleStreamBlock(nn.Module):
   307	    """Single-stream DiT block over concatenated motion and text tokens."""
   308	
   309	    def __init__(self, hidden_size: int, num_heads: int, mlp_ratio: float, dropout: float) -> None:
   310	        super().__init__()
   311	        mlp_hidden = int(hidden_size * mlp_ratio)
   312	        self.mod = AdaLNModulation(hidden_size, num=2)
   313	        self.norm1 = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_size, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
   314	        self.attn = MultiHeadAttention(hidden_size, num_heads, dropout)
   315	        self.norm2 = nn.LayerNorm(hidden_size, elementwise_affine=False, eps=1e-6)
   316	        self.ffn = nn.Sequential(SwiGLU(hidden_size, mlp_hidden), nn.Dropout(dropout))
   317	
   318	    def forward(
   319	        self,
   320	        x: torch.Tensor,
   321	        cond: torch.Tensor,
   322	        valid: torch.Tensor,
   323	        pos_ids: torch.Tensor,
   324	        rope_axes_dims: List[int],
   325	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   326	        attn_mod, ffn_mod = self.mod(cond)
   327	        h = self.norm1(x)
   328	        h = (1 + attn_mod.scale) * h + attn_mod.shift
   329	        x = x + attn_mod.gate * self.attn(
   330	            h,
   331	            h,
   332	            key_valid=valid,
   333	            query_valid=valid,
   334	            query_pos=pos_ids,
   335	            memory_pos=pos_ids,
   336	            rope_axes_dims=rope_axes_dims,
   337	        )
   338	        h = self.norm2(x)
   339	        h = (1 + ffn_mod.scale) * h + ffn_mod.shift
   340	        x = x + ffn_mod.gate * self.ffn(h)
   341	        return x

    54	def _ddp_setup():

    65	def load_frozen_tokenizer(ckpt_path: str, dev: torch.device) -> GraphVQTokenizer:
    66	    """Rebuild + freeze the Graph-VQVAE tokenizer (for the snapped-decode QA +
    67	    empirical-norm decode path). eval() + requires_grad_(False)."""
    68	    ck = torch.load(ckpt_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
    69	    ta = ck["args"]
    70	    model = GraphVQTokenizer(
    71	        d_model=ta["d_model"], n_heads=ta["n_heads"], d_ff=ta["d_ff"],
    72	        n_graph_layers=ta["n_graph_layers"],
    73	        n_enc_temporal_layers=ta["n_enc_temporal_layers"],
    74	        n_pre_vq_layers=ta["n_pre_vq_layers"], n_post_vq_layers=ta["n_post_vq_layers"],
    75	        n_cross_layers=ta["n_cross_layers"],
    76	        n_dec_temporal_layers=ta["n_dec_temporal_layers"],
    77	        max_coarse=ta["max_coarse"], temporal_stride=ta["temporal_stride"],
    78	        temporal_kernel=ta["temporal_kernel"], dropout=ta["dropout"],
    79	        code_dim=ta["code_dim"], num_codes=ta["num_codes"],
    80	        num_quantizers=ta["num_quantizers"], ema_mu=ta["ema_mu"],
    81	        quantize_dropout_prob=ta["quantize_dropout_prob"],
    82	        dead_code_threshold=ta["dead_code_threshold"],
    83	    ).to(dev)
    84	    model.load_state_dict(ck["model_state_dict"], strict=True)
    85	    model.eval()
    86	    model.requires_grad_(False)
    87	    return model, ta

    90	def build_cond(b: dict, cond_drop_prob: float, training: bool,
    91	               dtype: torch.dtype):
    92	    """Assemble the GraphStructuredCodeFlow conditioning dict from a token batch.
    93	
    94	    has_text starts from the dataset flag; during training we additionally CFG-
    95	    drop (flip True->False) with cond_drop_prob (so the model learns the uncond
    96	    branch). Float conditioning is cast to `dtype` (fp32 unless bf16 autocast
    97	    wraps the forward — the model enforces dtype-match on the fp32 path).
    98	    """
    99	    has_text = b["has_text"].clone()
   100	    if training and cond_drop_prob > 0.0:
   101	        drop = torch.rand(has_text.shape[0], device=has_text.device) < cond_drop_prob
   102	        has_text = has_text & ~drop
   103	    return {
   104	        "text_global": b["caption_emb"].to(dtype),
   105	        "text_tokens": b["caption_token_emb"].to(dtype),
   106	        "text_token_mask": b["caption_token_mask"],
   107	        "has_text": has_text,
   108	        "pooled_adjacency": b["pooled_adjacency"].to(dtype),
   109	        "pooled_geodesic": b["pooled_geodesic"].to(dtype),
   110	        "pooled_skeleton_embeddings": b["pooled_skeleton_embeddings"].to(dtype),
   111	        "coarse_mask": b["coarse_mask"],
   112	        "frame_mask_lat": b["frame_mask_lat"],
   113	    }

   116	def compute_empirical_stats(ds: TokenCacheDataset, D: int, dev: torch.device,
   117	                            max_clips: int = 0, cache_path: str | None = None,
   118	                            cache_key: dict | None = None,
   119	                            is_ddp: bool = False, rank: int = 0):
   120	    """Empirical z_q mean/std over VALID tokens of the train cache (LOCKED:
   121	    empirical normalization over the FULL train set, not codebook-stat). Streamed
   122	    sum / sumsq. max_clips<=0 (default) uses ALL train clips; a positive cap is for
   123	    smoke/debug only (it would normalize on a PREFIX, not the full set).
   124	
   125	    Startup acceleration (2026-06-09): (1) DISK CACHE — if cache_path exists and its
   126	    stored cache_key matches, load mean/std instantly (skips the full scan; survives
   127	    resume/restart/new experiments on the same cache). cache_key encodes the cache
   128	    identity (manifest_md5 + full-content index_md5 + n/D/max_clips) so any re-export
   129	    or clip-set/content change INVALIDATES it (a content hash, not a byte-size).
   130	    (2) rank-0-only under DDP — only rank 0 scans (or loads) + writes the cache, then

/usr/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba scripts/train_graph_codeflow.py | sed -n '130,280p'" in /iridisfs/scratch/ts1v23/workspace/noKslot_clean
 succeeded in 0ms:
   130	    (2) rank-0-only under DDP — only rank 0 scans (or loads) + writes the cache, then
   131	    broadcasts mean/std to all ranks. Avoids every rank re-decompressing the full cache
   132	    (the un-optimized path made a 6-rank/full-length scan take ~30min; this makes a cold
   133	    scan ~6x faster and a warm/cached start instant)."""
   134	    n = len(ds) if max_clips <= 0 else min(len(ds), max_clips)
   135	    mean_t = std_t = None
   136	    count = 0
   137	    # Only rank 0 (or a single process) scans/loads + writes the cache.
   138	    if not is_ddp or rank == 0:
   139	        loaded = False
   140	        if cache_path and Path(cache_path).exists():
   141	            try:
   142	                c = torch.load(cache_path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=False)
   143	                if c.get("D") == D and c.get("cache_key") == cache_key:
   144	                    mean_t, std_t = c["mean"].float(), c["std"].float()
   145	                    count = int(c["count"])
   146	                    # Explicit raise (not assert: survives `python -O`, which would
   147	                    # strip an assert and silently feed a bad-shape mean/std). A bad
   148	                    # shape here is caught by the enclosing except -> loaded=False ->
   149	                    # full re-scan + cache rewrite (self-heals instead of using it).
   150	                    if tuple(mean_t.shape) != (D,) or tuple(std_t.shape) != (D,):
   151	                        raise RuntimeError(
   152	                            f"cached empirical stats shape mismatch: "
   153	                            f"mean{tuple(mean_t.shape)} std{tuple(std_t.shape)} != ({D},)")
   154	                    loaded = True
   155	            except Exception:
   156	                loaded = False
   157	        if not loaded:
   158	            count = 0
   159	            s = torch.zeros(D, dtype=torch.float64)
   160	            s2 = torch.zeros(D, dtype=torch.float64)
   161	            for i in range(n):
   162	                it = ds[i]
   163	                z = it["z_q"].reshape(-1, D).double()       # [T_lat*C, D]
   164	                m = it["token_mask"].reshape(-1)
   165	                zv = z[m]
   166	                count += zv.shape[0]
   167	                s += zv.sum(dim=0)
   168	                s2 += zv.pow(2).sum(dim=0)
   169	            if count == 0:
   170	                raise RuntimeError("compute_empirical_stats: zero valid tokens in cache")
   171	            mean_t = (s / count).float()
   172	            std_t = ((s2 / count) - (s / count).pow(2)).clamp_min(1e-12).sqrt().float()
   173	            if cache_path:
   174	                try:
   175	                    torch.save({"mean": mean_t, "std": std_t, "count": count,
   176	                                "D": D, "cache_key": cache_key}, cache_path)
   177	                except Exception:
   178	                    pass
   179	        mean_t, std_t = mean_t.to(dev), std_t.to(dev)
   180	    else:
   181	        mean_t = torch.zeros(D, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)
   182	        std_t = torch.ones(D, dtype=torch.float32, device=dev)
   183	    # Broadcast rank-0's stats to all ranks (DDP); every rank ends with identical norm.
   184	    if is_ddp:
   185	        dist.broadcast(mean_t, src=0)
   186	        dist.broadcast(std_t, src=0)
   187	        cnt = torch.tensor([count], dtype=torch.long, device=dev)
   188	        dist.broadcast(cnt, src=0)
   189	        count = int(cnt.item())
   190	    return mean_t, std_t, count

   192	
   193	@torch.no_grad()
   194	def projection_qa(flow, tokenizer, b: dict, cond: dict, dev: torch.device,
   195	                  decode: bool = False):
   196	    """THE key gate: compare continuous decode(z_hat) vs snapped decode(z_snap)
   197	    and report projection_error = mse(z_hat, z_snap) over valid tokens.
   198	
   199	    Here z_hat is the model's predicted CLEAN latent from a single flow eval at a
   200	    fixed t (denormalized to raw RVQ space), NOT a full ODE sample (cheap, per-step
   201	    diagnostic). Returns projection_error, per-q generated-code usage, and (if
   202	    decode=True) the max abs decoded-motion gap continuous-vs-snapped.
   203	    """
   204	    z_q = b["z_q"].to(dev)
   205	    token_mask = b["token_mask"].to(dev)
   206	    B, T_lat, C, D = z_q.shape
   207	    # One flow eval at t~U: predict v -> clean (in normalized space) -> denorm.
   208	    x = flow.normalize(z_q) * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   209	    noise = torch.randn_like(x) * flow.noise_scale * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   210	    t = torch.rand(B, device=dev)
   211	    t_view = t[:, None, None, None]
   212	    z_t = (t_view * x + (1.0 - t_view) * noise) * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   213	    v = flow.predict_velocity(z_t, t, cond)
   214	    clean = flow.predict_clean_from_velocity(z_t, t, v)
   215	    z_hat = flow.denormalize(clean) * token_mask.unsqueeze(-1).float()
   216	
   217	    proj = tokenizer.nearest_residual_ids(z_hat, token_mask)
   218	    indices_hat = proj["indices_hat"]
   219	    # per-q generated code usage (#unique codes used on valid tokens).
   220	    usage = []
   221	    for qi in range(indices_hat.shape[-1]):
   222	        ids_q = indices_hat[..., qi][token_mask]
   223	        usage.append(int(torch.unique(ids_q[ids_q >= 0]).numel()))
   224	    out = {"projection_error": float(proj["projection_error"].item()),
   225	           "code_usage_per_q": usage}
   226	    if decode:
   227	        skel_meta = {
   228	            "s_j": b["s_j"].to(dev), "assignment": b["assignment"].to(dev),
   229	            "coarse_mask": b["coarse_mask"].to(dev),
   230	            "frame_mask_lat": b["frame_mask_lat"].to(dev),
   231	            "pooled_adjacency": b["pooled_adjacency"].to(dev),
   232	            "pooled_geodesic": b["pooled_geodesic"].to(dev),
   233	        }
   234	        fake_batch = SimpleNamespace(joint_mask=b["joint_mask"].to(dev))
   235	        cont = tokenizer.decode(z_hat, skel_meta, fake_batch)["pred_motion"]
   236	        snap = tokenizer.decode_from_indices(indices_hat, skel_meta, fake_batch)["pred_motion"]
   237	        out["decode_cont_finite"] = bool(torch.isfinite(cont).all())
   238	        out["decode_snap_finite"] = bool(torch.isfinite(snap).all())
   239	        out["decode_cont_vs_snap_maxabs"] = float((cont - snap).abs().max().item())
   240	    return out

   386	                f"num_frames, or raise --max_T_lat to >= {_cache_T_lat}.")

   388	
   389	    # ---- Resume: rebuild the model from the CKPT's saved architecture args ----
   390	    # The model is constructed BEFORE the resume state_dict load below, so when
   391	    # resuming we must build the SAME architecture the ckpt was trained with (its
   392	    # model_variant + depths), not the CLI default — otherwise load_state_dict
   393	    # (strict=True) would mismatch. Old ckpts (pre-model_variant) default to

   415	    if args.dropout is None:

   417

   420	    if args.hidden_size != args.code_dim:

   429	        max_T_lat=args.max_T_lat,

   433	    # manifest, so a re-export invalidates it) + rank-0 scan + broadcast under DDP.
   434	    _cache_path = None
   435	    _cache_key = None
   436	    try:

   438	        _tc = Path(args.token_cache)
   439	        _man_text = (_tc / "manifest.json").read_text()
   440	        _idx_bytes = (_tc / "train" / "index.jsonl").read_bytes()
   441	        # Strong invalidation: full-manifest md5 (any export-config change) + a full
   442	        # CONTENT md5 of train/index.jsonl (any clip-set/order/content change, even at
   443	        # identical byte-size) + n/D/max_clips. A re-export rewrites manifest/index ->
   444	        # key changes -> cache auto-invalidates. (md5 of ~24MB index is one-time
   445	        # rank-0-only ~tens of ms, negligible vs the scan it guards.)
   446	        _cache_key = {"manifest_md5": hashlib.md5(_man_text.encode()).hexdigest(),

   455	        cache_path=_cache_path, cache_key=_cache_key, is_ddp=is_ddp, rank=rank)
   456	    flow.set_latent_stats(e_mean, e_std)
   457	    log(f"empirical z_q norm over {n_stat} valid tokens: "
   458	        f"mean|.|avg={e_mean.abs().mean().item():.4f} std.avg={e_std.mean().item():.4f}")
   459	    n_params = sum(pp.numel() for pp in flow.parameters() if pp.requires_grad)
   460	    log(f"GraphCodeFlow trainable params: {n_params:,}")

   510	    def lr_at(step: int) -> float:
   511	        """Linear warmup -> half-cosine decay to eta_min_ratio*lr (CodeFlow recipe)."""
   512	        if args.warmup_steps > 0 and step < args.warmup_steps:
   513	            return args.lr * (step + 1) / args.warmup_steps
   514	        if args.lr_scheduler == "none":
   515	            return args.lr
   516	        prog = (step - args.warmup_steps) / max(1, total_steps - args.warmup_steps)
   517	        prog = min(1.0, max(0.0, prog))
   518	        cos = 0.5 * (1.0 + math.cos(math.pi * prog))
   519	        return args.lr * (args.eta_min_ratio + (1.0 - args.eta_min_ratio) * cos)

     1	"""GraphStructuredCodeFlow — Level-A graph rectified-flow backbone over the
     2	FROZEN Graph-VQVAE post-RVQ z_q grid.
     3	
     4	Plan: handoff/20260609_graph_codeflow_rvq_backbone_plan.md §7 (Level A) +
     5	handoff/20260609_0530_graph_codeflow_locked_recipe_and_state.md (LOCKED recipe).
     6	
     7	This is the SEPARATE post-RVQ generation branch (handoff §16): it does NOT touch
     8	the Gaussian VAE / latent diffusion / graph_salad denoiser. It REUSES (by import,
     9	never copy) the shared graph-temporal + dual-text ingredients:
    10	
    11	  - GraphAttentionBlock          (graph_salad.attention) — graph-spatial attn
    12	                                   over the C coarse-slot axis (pooled adjacency /
    13	                                   geodesic bias), per latent frame.
    14	  - TemporalSelfAttention        (motion_decoder)        — temporal attn over the
    15	                                   T_lat latent-frame axis, per coarse slot.
    16	  - SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding,
    17	    DenseFiLM, TextCrossAttention (graph_salad.denoiser)  — timestep FiLM, the
    18	                                   dual_text global-add path, and token-level text
    19	                                   cross-attention (CFG-uncond rows zeroed).
    20	
    21	I/O is the post-RVQ token grid `[B, T_lat, C, D]` (D = RVQ code_dim). The model
    22	predicts the rectified-flow velocity `v` at the same shape. Strict 2D `[T_lat,C]`
    23	masking (`token_mask = coarse_mask & frame_mask_lat`) is re-applied after every
    24	sub-block AND at input/output — padded slots / padded latent frames never leak.
    25	
    26	Text conditioning is the project-default DUAL text (T5-768):
    27	  - GLOBAL: mean-pooled caption [B,768] -> Linear -> additive per-layer (gated by
    28	    has_text for CFG).  Mirrors the denoiser's dual_text global path.
    29	  - TOKEN:  token-level caption [B,L,768] -> Linear -> per-layer cross-attention
    30	    (key-padding-masked; CFG-uncond rows contribute exactly 0).
    31	
    32	NOTE on precision: like the graph_salad denoiser, the graph-spatial block requires
    33	fp32 adjacency/geodesic and forces fp32 softmax. This model is intended to run in
    34	fp32 for the flow math (the trainer keeps z_q / graph tensors fp32); bf16 autocast
    35	may wrap the matmuls (GraphAttentionBlock is bf16-safe for features), but the
    36	adjacency/geodesic/text/skeleton conditioning tensors must match z_t.dtype on the
    37	fp32 path (enforced below, same contract as GraphSaladDenoiser).
    38	"""
    39	
    40	from __future__ import annotations
    41	
    42	import torch
    43	import torch.nn as nn
    44	
    45	from src.models.graph_salad.attention import GraphAttentionBlock
    46	from src.models.motion_decoder import TemporalSelfAttention
    47	from src.models.graph_salad.denoiser import (
    48	    SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding,
    49	    DenseFiLM,
    50	    TextCrossAttention,
    51	)
    52	
    53	
    54	class GraphCodeFlowLayer(nn.Module):
    55	    """One Level-A flow layer over the `[B,T_lat,C,D]` token grid.
    56	
    57	    Ordering mirrors the proven GraphSaladDenoiserLayer (graph_salad/denoiser.py):
    58	      graph-spatial attn -> FiLM -> temporal attn -> FiLM ->
    59	      [token cross-attn] + [global text add] -> FiLM -> strict padded re-mask.
    60	
    61	    The graph-spatial sub-block uses pooled_adjacency / pooled_geodesic exactly as
    62	    CoarseGraphTemporalLayer / the denoiser do; the temporal sub-block self-attends
    63	    over T_lat per slot. Timestep modulation is via DenseFiLM (zero-init -> identity
    64	    at init). Token cross-attn + global add are both gated by has_text for CFG.
    65	    """
    66	
    67	    def __init__(self, d_model: int, n_heads: int, d_ff: int, d_t: int,
    68	                 dropout: float = 0.1) -> None:
    69	        super().__init__()
    70	        self.spatial = GraphAttentionBlock(d_model, n_heads, d_ff, dropout=dropout)
    71	        self.temporal = TemporalSelfAttention(d_model, n_heads, dropout=dropout)
    72	        self.text_cross_attn = TextCrossAttention(d_model, n_heads, dropout=dropout)
    73	        self.film_after_spatial = DenseFiLM(d_t, d_model)
    74	        self.film_after_temporal = DenseFiLM(d_t, d_model)
    75	        self.film_after_text = DenseFiLM(d_t, d_model)
    76	
    77	    def forward(
    78	        self,
    79	        x: torch.Tensor,                  # [B, T_lat, C, D]
    80	        t_emb: torch.Tensor,              # [B, D_t]
    81	        text_global: torch.Tensor,        # [B, D] projected mean caption
    82	        has_text: torch.Tensor,           # [B] bool
    83	        tok_emb: torch.Tensor,            # [B, L, D] projected token caption
    84	        text_key_padding_mask: torch.Tensor,  # [B, L] bool, True = mask
    85	        pooled_adj: torch.Tensor,         # [B, C, C] fp32
    86	        pooled_geo: torch.Tensor,         # [B, C, C] fp32
    87	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,        # [B, C] bool
    88	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,     # [B, T_lat] bool
    89	        *,
    90	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
    91	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
    92	        B, T_lat, C, D = x.shape
    93	
    94	        # --- 1. Graph-spatial self-attn (per latent frame, over C slots) ---
    95	        x_sp_in = x.reshape(B * T_lat, C, D)
    96	        adj_exp = pooled_adj.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C, C)
    97	        geo_exp = pooled_geo.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C, C)
    98	        cm_exp = coarse_mask.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, T_lat, C).reshape(B * T_lat, C)
    99	        x_sp = self.spatial(x_sp_in, adj_exp, geo_exp, cm_exp,
   100	                            validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   101	        x = x_sp.reshape(B, T_lat, C, D)
   102	        x = self.film_after_spatial(x, t_emb)
   103	
   104	        # --- 2. Temporal self-attn (per slot, over T_lat frames) ---
   105	        x_t_in = x.permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous().reshape(B * C, T_lat, D)
   106	        fm_exp = frame_mask_lat.unsqueeze(1).expand(B, C, T_lat).reshape(B * C, T_lat)
   107	        x_t = self.temporal(x_t_in, fm_exp)
   108	        x = x_t.reshape(B, C, T_lat, D).permute(0, 2, 1, 3).contiguous()
   109	        x = self.film_after_temporal(x, t_emb)
   110	
   111	        # --- 3. Dual text: token cross-attn THEN global add (both has_text-gated) ---
   112	        q = x.reshape(B, T_lat * C, D)
   113	        ca = self.text_cross_attn(q, tok_emb, text_key_padding_mask)
   114	        x = x + ca.reshape(B, T_lat, C, D)
   115	        text_gated = text_global * has_text[:, None].to(text_global.dtype)  # [B, D]
   116	        x = x + text_gated[:, None, None, :]
   117	        x = self.film_after_text(x, t_emb)
   118	
   119	        # --- 4. Strict padded re-mask (padded slots/frames must be 0 after layer) ---
   120	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(x.dtype)
   121	        fm = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(x.dtype)
   122	        return x * cm * fm
   123	
   124	
   125	class GraphStructuredCodeFlow(nn.Module):
   126	    """Level-A graph rectified-flow velocity network over post-RVQ z_q.
   127	
   128	    forward(z_t, timesteps, text_global, text_tokens, text_token_mask, has_text,
   129	            pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic, pooled_skeleton_embeddings,
   130	            coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat) -> v_pred [B, T_lat, C, D].
   131	
   132	    Skip-transformer (SALAD-style, n_layers odd: enc + mid + dec with symmetric
   133	    skips) of GraphCodeFlowLayer blocks, mirroring GraphSaladDenoiser's structure
   134	    but operating on the frozen RVQ code grid (D = code_dim) instead of the
   135	    Gaussian latent. Output zero-init so v_pred ≈ 0 at init (flow-stable).
   136	    """
   137	
   138	    def __init__(
   139	        self,
   140	        code_dim: int = 512,
   141	        n_heads: int = 8,
   142	        d_ff: int | None = None,
   143	        n_layers: int = 5,
   144	        d_text: int = 768,
   145	        text_token_dim: int = 768,
   146	        d_t: int | None = None,
   147	        dropout: float = 0.1,
   148	    ) -> None:
   149	        super().__init__()
   150	        if n_layers % 2 == 0:
   151	            raise ValueError(
   152	                f"n_layers must be odd for the SALAD skip-transformer, got {n_layers}")
   153	        if code_dim % n_heads != 0:
   154	            raise ValueError(f"code_dim ({code_dim}) must divide n_heads ({n_heads})")
   155	        if d_ff is None:
   156	            d_ff = 4 * code_dim
   157	        if d_t is None:
   158	            d_t = code_dim
   159	        self.code_dim = code_dim
   160	        self.d_model = code_dim  # the token grid feature dim == RVQ code_dim
   161	        self.n_heads = n_heads
   162	        self.d_ff = d_ff
   163	        self.n_layers = n_layers
   164	        self.d_text = d_text
   165	        self.text_token_dim = text_token_dim
   166	        self.d_t = d_t
   167	
   168	        # Timestep embedding (sinusoidal -> MLP), shared across all FiLMs.
   169	        self.t_sin = SinusoidalTimestepEmbedding(d_t)
   170	        self.t_mlp = nn.Sequential(
   171	            nn.Linear(d_t, d_t * 4), nn.SiLU(), nn.Linear(d_t * 4, d_t))
   172	
   173	        # Dual-text projections (T5-768 -> code_dim): global mean + token level.
   174	        self.text_proj = nn.Linear(d_text, code_dim)
   175	        self.text_token_proj = nn.Linear(text_token_dim, code_dim)
   176	
   177	        # Input projection: z_t + additive skeleton-slot conditioning.
   178	        self.input_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   179	
   180	        self.layers = nn.ModuleList([
   181	            GraphCodeFlowLayer(code_dim, n_heads, d_ff, d_t, dropout=dropout)
   182	            for _ in range(n_layers)
   183	        ])
   184	        self.depth = (n_layers - 1) // 2
   185	        self.skip_mergers = nn.ModuleList(
   186	            [nn.Linear(2 * code_dim, code_dim) for _ in range(self.depth)])
   187	
   188	        # Output: pre-norm + zero-init linear -> initial v_pred ≈ 0.
   189	        self.output_norm = nn.LayerNorm(code_dim)
   190	        self.output_proj = nn.Linear(code_dim, code_dim)
   191	        nn.init.zeros_(self.output_proj.weight)
   192	        nn.init.zeros_(self.output_proj.bias)
   193	
   194	    def forward(
   195	        self,
   196	        z_t: torch.Tensor,                       # [B, T_lat, C, D]
   197	        timesteps: torch.Tensor,                 # [B] (float in [0,1])
   198	        text_global: torch.Tensor,               # [B, 768] mean caption
   199	        text_tokens: torch.Tensor,               # [B, L, 768] token caption
   200	        text_token_mask: torch.Tensor,           # [B, L] bool, True = valid token
   201	        has_text: torch.Tensor,                  # [B] bool (CFG gate)
   202	        pooled_adjacency: torch.Tensor,          # [B, C, C]
   203	        pooled_geodesic: torch.Tensor,           # [B, C, C]
   204	        pooled_skeleton_embeddings: torch.Tensor,  # [B, C, D]
   205	        coarse_mask: torch.Tensor,               # [B, C] bool
   206	        frame_mask_lat: torch.Tensor,            # [B, T_lat] bool
   207	        *,
   208	        validate_inputs: bool = False,
   209	    ) -> torch.Tensor:
   210	        if z_t.dim() != 4 or z_t.shape[-1] != self.code_dim:
   211	            raise ValueError(
   212	                f"z_t must be [B,T_lat,C,D={self.code_dim}], got {tuple(z_t.shape)}")
   213	        B, T_lat, C, D = z_t.shape
   214	        ref_device = z_t.device
   215	        # ---- contract checks (mirror GraphSaladDenoiser; fail-loud) ----
   216	        if timesteps.shape != (B,):
   217	            raise ValueError(f"timesteps must be [B={B}], got {tuple(timesteps.shape)}")
   218	        if coarse_mask.shape != (B, C) or coarse_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   219	            raise ValueError(
   220	                f"coarse_mask must be [B={B},C={C}] bool, got "
   221	                f"{tuple(coarse_mask.shape)} {coarse_mask.dtype}")
   222	        if frame_mask_lat.shape != (B, T_lat) or frame_mask_lat.dtype != torch.bool:
   223	            raise ValueError(
   224	                f"frame_mask_lat must be [B={B},T_lat={T_lat}] bool, got "
   225	                f"{tuple(frame_mask_lat.shape)} {frame_mask_lat.dtype}")
   226	        if has_text.shape != (B,) or has_text.dtype != torch.bool:
   227	            raise ValueError(
   228	                f"has_text must be [B={B}] bool, got {tuple(has_text.shape)} "
   229	                f"{has_text.dtype}")
   230	        if pooled_adjacency.shape != (B, C, C) or pooled_geodesic.shape != (B, C, C):
   231	            raise ValueError(
   232	                f"pooled_adjacency/geodesic must be [B={B},C={C},C={C}], got "
   233	                f"{tuple(pooled_adjacency.shape)} / {tuple(pooled_geodesic.shape)}")
   234	        if pooled_skeleton_embeddings.shape != (B, C, D):
   235	            raise ValueError(
   236	                f"pooled_skeleton_embeddings must be [B={B},C={C},D={D}], got "
   237	                f"{tuple(pooled_skeleton_embeddings.shape)}")
   238	        if text_global.dim() != 2 or text_global.shape != (B, self.d_text):
   239	            raise ValueError(
   240	                f"text_global must be [B={B},{self.d_text}], got {tuple(text_global.shape)}")
   241	        if (text_tokens.dim() != 3 or text_tokens.shape[0] != B
   242	                or text_tokens.shape[2] != self.text_token_dim):
   243	            raise ValueError(
   244	                f"text_tokens must be [B={B},L,{self.text_token_dim}], got "
   245	                f"{tuple(text_tokens.shape)}")
   246	        L = text_tokens.shape[1]
   247	        if text_token_mask.shape != (B, L) or text_token_mask.dtype != torch.bool:
   248	            raise ValueError(
   249	                f"text_token_mask must be [B={B},L={L}] bool, got "
   250	                f"{tuple(text_token_mask.shape)} {text_token_mask.dtype}")
   251	        for name, t in (
   252	            ("timesteps", timesteps), ("coarse_mask", coarse_mask),
   253	            ("frame_mask_lat", frame_mask_lat), ("has_text", has_text),
   254	            ("pooled_adjacency", pooled_adjacency), ("pooled_geodesic", pooled_geodesic),
   255	            ("pooled_skeleton_embeddings", pooled_skeleton_embeddings),
   256	            ("text_global", text_global), ("text_tokens", text_tokens),
   257	            ("text_token_mask", text_token_mask),
   258	        ):
   259	            if t.device != ref_device:
   260	                raise ValueError(
   261	                    f"GraphStructuredCodeFlow: {name}.device {t.device} != "
   262	                    f"z_t.device {ref_device}")
   263	        # Float conditioning tensors must match z_t.dtype on the fp32 path (same
   264	        # contract as GraphSaladDenoiser / GraphAttentionBlock).
   265	        if z_t.dtype in (torch.float32, torch.float64):
   266	            for name, t in (
   267	                ("pooled_adjacency", pooled_adjacency),
   268	                ("pooled_geodesic", pooled_geodesic),
   269	                ("pooled_skeleton_embeddings", pooled_skeleton_embeddings),
   270	                ("text_global", text_global), ("text_tokens", text_tokens),
   271	            ):
   272	                if t.dtype != z_t.dtype:
   273	                    raise ValueError(
   274	                        f"GraphStructuredCodeFlow: {name}.dtype {t.dtype} != "
   275	                        f"z_t.dtype {z_t.dtype}")
   276	
   277	        # ---- timestep embedding ----
   278	        t_emb = self.t_mlp(self.t_sin(timesteps))         # [B, D_t]
   279	
   280	        # ---- dual-text prep (project + build the shared key-padding mask) ----
   281	        text_global_proj = self.text_proj(text_global)    # [B, D]
   282	        tok_emb = self.text_token_proj(text_tokens)        # [B, L, D]
   283	        # valid key = token present AND has_text=True; key_padding_mask is the
   284	        # inverse (True = mask). has_text=False -> whole row masked -> cross-attn
   285	        # output zeroed in TextCrossAttention (CFG-uncond contributes 0).
   286	        valid_key = text_token_mask & has_text[:, None]    # [B, L]
   287	        text_key_padding_mask = ~valid_key                 # [B, L] True = mask
   288	
   289	        # ---- input projection + additive skeleton conditioning ----
   290	        x = self.input_proj(z_t)                           # [B, T_lat, C, D]
   291	        x = x + pooled_skeleton_embeddings.unsqueeze(1).expand(-1, T_lat, -1, -1)
   292	        cm = coarse_mask[:, None, :, None].to(x.dtype)
   293	        fm = frame_mask_lat[:, :, None, None].to(x.dtype)
   294	        x = x * cm * fm
   295	
   296	        # ---- skip-transformer: enc -> mid -> dec with symmetric skips ----
   297	        def _run(layer, h):
   298	            return layer(
   299	                h, t_emb, text_global_proj, has_text, tok_emb, text_key_padding_mask,
   300	                pooled_adjacency, pooled_geodesic, coarse_mask, frame_mask_lat,
   301	                validate_inputs=validate_inputs)
   302	
   303	        enc_outputs: list[torch.Tensor] = []
   304	        for i in range(self.depth):
   305	            x = _run(self.layers[i], x)
   306	            enc_outputs.append(x)
   307	        x = _run(self.layers[self.depth], x)
   308	        for i in range(self.depth):
   309	            dec_layer = self.layers[self.depth + 1 + i]
   310	            skip = enc_outputs[self.depth - 1 - i]
   311	            x = torch.cat([x, skip], dim=-1)               # [B, T_lat, C, 2D]
   312	            x = self.skip_mergers[i](x)                     # [B, T_lat, C, D]
   313	            x = _run(dec_layer, x)
   314	
   315	        # ---- output: pre-norm + zero-init linear + final re-mask ----
   316	        x = self.output_norm(x)
   317	        v_pred = self.output_proj(x)
   318	        return v_pred * cm * fm

    71	        d_text=768, text_token_dim=768, dropout=a.get("dropout", 0.1),
    72	        # model_variant + graph_pscf arch args (old ckpts: level_a defaults so they

    74	        model_variant=a.get("model_variant", "level_a"),

    76	        max_T_lat=a.get("max_T_lat", 75), mlp_ratio=a.get("mlp_ratio", 4.0),
    77	    ).to(dev)

    83	def encode_ood_text(text: str, dev: torch.device):
    84	    """T5-encode an arbitrary string into (global[768], tokens[64,768], mask[64]),
    85	    matching the TRAINING caption cache convention exactly (t5-base, max_length=64;
    86	    global = mask-mean of last_hidden_state, tokens = padded [64,768] + [64] bool).
    87	    See precompute_t5_captions.py (global) + precompute_t5_caption_tokens.py (tokens)."""
    88	    import os
    89	    os.environ.setdefault("HF_HUB_OFFLINE", "1")
    90	    os.environ.setdefault("TRANSFORMERS_OFFLINE", "1")
    91	    from transformers import T5EncoderModel, T5TokenizerFast
    92	    tok = T5TokenizerFast.from_pretrained("t5-base")
    93	    t5 = T5EncoderModel.from_pretrained("t5-base").to(dev).eval()
    94	    enc = tok(text, return_tensors="pt", padding="max_length", truncation=True,
    95	              max_length=64).to(dev)
    96	    with torch.no_grad():
    97	        hs = t5(input_ids=enc.input_ids,
    98	                attention_mask=enc.attention_mask).last_hidden_state[0]   # [64,768]
    99	    m = enc.attention_mask[0].bool()                                      # [64]
   100	    g = (hs * m.unsqueeze(-1).float()).sum(0) / m.sum().clamp_min(1)      # [768]
   101	    return g.float(), hs.float(), m
   102

   250	            g_spd = float(np.linalg.norm(np.diff(gt_world, axis=0), axis=-1).mean())
   251	            if gt_world.shape[0] != snap_world.shape[0]:  # fail loud, no silent misalign
   252	                raise SystemExit(f"[QA FAIL] GT/pred length mismatch: "
   253	                                 f"{gt_world.shape[0]} vs {snap_world.shape[0]}")
   254	            gt_for_render = None if ood_fields is not None else gt_world
   255	
   256	            k = picked[sp]
   257	            # Large-figure T2M (user 2026-06-10): PIL renderer, GT stitched as the
   258	            # RIGHTMOST red panel → input | PRED snapped | PRED continuous | GT.
   259	            gif = out_dir / f"{sp}_clip{k}_t2m.gif"
   260	            make_t2m_large_gif(
   261	                snap_world, cont_world, static_pose, parents, prompt_text,
   262	                str(gif), fps=args.fps, gt=gt_for_render,
   263	                pred_labels=("PRED snapped decode", "PRED continuous decode"))
   264	            ratio_str = ("N/A(OOD)" if ood_fields is not None
   265	                         else f"{p_spd / max(g_spd, 1e-9):.3f}")
   266	            line = (f"{sp} clip{k}: J={J} T={gt_T} prompt={prompt_text[:50]!r} "
   267	                    f"proj_err={proj['projection_error'].item():.4f} "
   268	                    f"snap_speed={p_spd:.4f} GT_speed={g_spd:.4f} "
   269	                    f"speed_ratio={ratio_str} cont_vs_snap_maxabs="
   270	                    f"{(cont - snap).abs().max().item():.4f} -> {gif.name}")
   271	            print(line)
   272	            summary.append(line)
   273	            picked[sp] += 1
   274	            if all(picked[s] >= args.n_per for s in want):
   275	                break
   276	
   277	    (out_dir / "t2m_summary.txt").write_text("\n".join(summary) + "\n")
   278	    print(f"\nDONE {sum(picked.values())} gifs -> {out_dir}")
   279	    print("PER-SPECIES picked:", picked)
   280	    return 0
   281	
   282	
   283	if __name__ == "__main__":
   284	    raise SystemExit(main())

   521	        # independent torch.randn init, so the codebooks (embed / cluster_size /
